ityuktaṃ kaiścidācāryairapare tvanyathā jaguḥ 250
madhyamo muktayā tantryā tarjanyādyaṅgulītrayāt
vāmasyānāmikāvarjyāstrayaḥ syuḥ pañcamādayaḥ 251
muktatantryā'tha ṣaḍjaḥ syādṛṣabhastarjanībhavaḥ
gāndhāro madhyamāṅgulyā dakṣiṇenātha vādanam 252
āroheṇāvaroheṇa saptakadvitaye bhavet
ebhiḥ svarairviracitaṃ vicitraṃ rāgamālapet 253
gāyedgītaṃ nibaddhaṃ ca pravīṇo vīṇayā'nayā
idamālāpinīlakṣma śrīniḥśaṅkena kīrtitam 254
ityālāpinīlakṣaṇam
kiṃnarī dvividhā laghvī bṛhatī ceti kīrtitā
tatra laghvīgataṃ lakṣma sāṃprataṃ pratipādyate 255
syādvitastitrayaṃ dairghye mānaṃ pañcāṅgulādhikam
pañcāṅgulaḥ parīṇāho veṇudaṇḍasya yatra ca 256
garbhe'sya vyāpakaṃ randhraṃ kakubhaḥ śākadārujaḥ
sārdhadvyaṅgulavistāro dairghye pañcāṅgulaśca saḥ 257
tasmādardhāṅgulanyūnā dairghyavistārayorbhavet
madhye kūrmonnatā lauhī patrikā kakubhasthitā 258
gṛdhravakṣo'sthinalikā kaniṣṭhāṅgulisaṃmitā
lauhī kāṃsyamayī yadvā kīrtitā sārikākhyayā 259
śliṣṭā vastramaṣīmiśramadanena caturdaśa
caturdaśasvarasthāne daṇḍapṛṣṭhe niveśayet 260
saptakasya dvitīyasya yo niṣādo bhavedadhaḥ
tasya sthāne bhavedādyā sārikā'nyordhvamaṅgulāt 261
sthāpayedantare kiṃcitkiṃcitpūrvādhike parā
dvyaṅgulāvadhyaṣṭamīṃ tu pūrvasyāstryaṅgulāntare 262
sthāpayitvā parāḥ ṣaṭca pūrvapūrvādhikāntare
caturaṅgulaparyantaṃ sārikāḥ saṃniveśayet 263
tumbaṃ daṇḍasya kakubhasyādhaḥsaṃdhau niveśayet
tṛtīyaturyasāryostu madhye'dhastādvitīyakam 264
pūrvasmādaparaṃ tumbaṃ vistāre'bhyadhikaṃ manāk
daṇḍāgrāddvyaṅgule'dhastādrandhraṃ kṛtvā'tha nikṣipet 265
calaśaṅkuṃ gale randhraṃ dadhamānamato'ṅgulāt
adhastādaṅgulotsedhaṃ meḍhakaṃ bāṇapuṅkhavat 266
kṛtvā tato'grataḥ kiṃcitsthiraśaṅkuṃ niveśayet
tato lohamayīṃ ślakṣṇāṃ vartulāṃ ca samāṃ dṛḍhām 267
gajakeśopamāṃ tantrīṃ nibadhya kakubhe dṛḍham
sārikāmastakanyastāmānītāṃ meḍhakopari 268
lagnāṃ dvitīyaprāntena veṣṭayeccalakīlake
śaṅkuṃ taṃ bhrāmayettāvadyāvattantrī dṛḍhā bhavet 269
bhrāmaṇaṃ vaiparītyena tantrīśaithilyakāraṇam
tantrīdārghye'tha dārḍhyāya calaśaṅkorgalasthite 270
chidre'nyasyātha saṃkīlaṃ sthiraśaṅkau niveśayet
laghvī sā kiṃnarī proktā śārṅgadevena sūriṇā 271
asyāṃ sthāyinamārabhya gaṇayetsaptakadvayam
sārīkakubhayormadhye tarjanyādyaṅgulitrayāt 272
vādayetkiṃnarīvīṇātantrīṃ dakṣiṇapāṇinā
vāmasya tisṛbhistābhiraṅgulībhistu tantrikām 273
tattatsārīpradeśasthāṃ svaravyaktyai nipīḍayet
vitastyabhyadhikā dairghye pariṇāhe'ṅgulādhikā 274
laghvyāḥ syādbṛhatī snāyumayā tantrīstritumbikā
ālāpinīvadasyāṃ ca sthāpyaṃ tumbaṃ tṛtīyakam
anyallaghvīgataṃ lakṣma bṛhatīṃ kiṃnarīṃ śrayet 275
iti dvividhakiṃnarīlakṣaṇam
kiṃnarītritayaṃ tatra deśīsaṃsiddhamanyathā
bṛhatī madhyamā laghvītyathā''sāṃ lakṣma vakṣyate 276
tiryagyavodaraiḥ ṣaḍbhirnistuṣaiḥ syādihāṅgulam
bṛhatīdaṇḍamānaṃ syāddairghye pañcāśadaṅgulam 277
ṣaḍaṅgulo'tra paridhirdaṇḍe dairghye tu kākubhe
ṣaḍaṅgulaṃ śirastvasya dairghyavistārayorbhavet 278
caturaṅgulasaṃmānaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ tu taducchraye
nikṣipetkākubhaṃ daṇḍaṃ vīṇādaṇḍottare tathā 279
daṇḍāṃśaḥ pariśeṣo'tra tāvānyāvati śeṣite
vīṇādaṇḍāntakakubhaśiromadhyāntare sthitaḥ 280
tantrībhāgastṛtīyāṃśādhikatryaṅgulako bhavet
madhye kūrmonnatāṃ lauhīṃ patrikāṃ śirasi kṣipet 281
parito'rdhāṅgulanyūnā śiraso'sau prakīrtitā
vīṇāśīrṣādadhastācca sārdhadvyaṅgulataḥ sthitam 282
ūrdhvādhodairghyabhāgrandhraṃ vistāre'rdhāṅgulāyatam
tiryagdairghyaṃ tāvadanyadrandhraṃ cobhayatomukham 283
tiryagrandhre calaṃ śaṅkumūrdhvarandhraniviṣṭayā
lauhyā sārikayā yuttaprāntāntaraṃ punaḥ 284
sārikāyāḥ śiromūle badhnīyātkākubhe sudhīḥ
tantrīrandhrātpuraḥ sāndhrīdvyaṅgule meḍhako bhavet 285
sa ca syātkartarīyukto gale randhrānvito'thavā
kartaryā galarandhre vā sārikāṃ tāṃ niveśayet 286
tadrandhre daṇḍāntarālāṃ vidadhyātsayavāṅgulam
meḍhakātpurataḥ śaṅkuḥ sthiraḥ sārdhāṅgulottare 287
tiryagrandhre niveśo'sya calaśaṅkuvadiṣyate
calaśaṅkubhrāmaṇādi jñeyaṃ pūrvoktarītitaḥ 288
gṛdhravakṣosthijā yadvā tatpādāsthisamudbhavāḥ
kāṃsyamayyo'thavā lauhyo nalikāḥ sārikā matāḥ 289
sārdhāṅgulāstāḥ paridhau daṇḍapṛṣṭhe niveśayet
yanmeḍhakaśiromadhyādupakramyāntaraṃ bhavet 290
sārīmastakamadhyānāṃ tadidānīṃ nirūpyate
ādyāntaraṃ tatra yavādhikaṃ pañcāṅgulaṃ matam 291
dvitīyamantarālaṃ tu caturaṅgulamuttaram
tasmāttṛtīyaturye tu yavanyūne mate satām 292
yavādhikatryaṅgulaṃ tu jñeyaṃ pañcamamantaram
yavonadvyaṅgulaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sayavadvyaṅgulaṃ punaḥ 293
antarālaṃ saptamaṃ syātsārdhadvyaṅgulamaṣṭamam
navamaṃ tu yavārdhena sārdhāṅgulamitaṃ matam 294
daśamaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ hīnaṃ yavenaikādaśaṃ tataḥ
dvādaśaṃ tu tṛtīyāṃśanyūnaṃ tasmāttrayodaśam 295
satṛtīyāṃśāgulaṃ syādaṅgulaṃ tu caturdaśam
dvitīyāntarato'dhastānnyasyettumbamadhomukham 296
anyatkakubhamūrdhvādhastumbakaṃ saṃniveśayet
ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulaṃ cakre paridhistvādyatumbake 297
kiṃcinnyūnaṃ tatastumbaṃ paridhau syādadhastana
īṣadaspaṣṭasarikā sārikāstā niveśayet 298
madaneneṣṭakācūrṇamiśreṇa śleṣaṇaṃ dṛḍham
sārīṇāmathavā vastramaṣīmiśreṇa saṃmitam 299
muktatantrībhavaṃ kṛtvā svaramādyaṃ caturdaśam
svarāḥ pare syuḥ sārīṇāṃ caturdaśabhirantaraiḥ 300
saptakadvayamevaṃ syādekatārasvarādhikam
yathāsvaṃ svaradeśāṃśaiḥ śrutistasyā vicinvate 301
dvitrāstato'dhikāḥ sārīrnibadhnīyātpare tviha
lakṣayantyantarāṇyāsāṃ svarāvirbhāvato budhāḥ 302
śrīśārṅgadevopadeśāttadbodhaḥ sulabho nṛṇām
kecittrayodaśaivātra sārīrnidadhate budhāḥ 303
bṛhatī kiṃnarītyeṣā śārṅgadevena kīrtitā
madhyamāyāṃ daṇḍadairghyaṃ tricatvāriṃśadaṅgulam 304
paridhirdṛśyate tatra dviyavonaṣaḍaṅgulaḥ
sārdhatryaṅgulavistāraṃ prāgdairghyaṃ kākubhaṃ śiraḥ 305
kṣipetkakubhadaṇḍasya vīṇādaṇḍe'ṅgulatrayam
tāvāṃśca pariśeṣe'sau yāvataḥ pariśeṣaṇe 306
daṇḍāntaśīrṣamadhyānte sārikāvayavasthitaḥ
tṛtīyabhāgarahitāṅgulatrayamito bhavet 307
daṇḍānte sārikotsedhaḥ syādyavonāṅguladvayaḥ
tadardho meḍhakopāntye sārīṇāmantaraḥ tviha 308
prathamaṃ prathitaṃ prājñaiḥ sārdhāṅgulacatuṣṭayam
dvitīyamantaraṃ meyaṃ sayavairaṅgulaistribhiḥ 309
tṛtīyaṃ tu tṛtīyāṃśābhyadhikaistribhiraṅgulaiḥ
caturthaṃ tryaṅgulaṃ proktaṃ pañcamaṃ sārikāntaram 310
yavādhikābhyāṃ sārdhābhyāmaṅgulābhyāṃ mitaṃ matam
ṣaṣṭhaṃ yavadvayanyūnadvyaṅgulaṃ saptamaṃ punaḥ 311
sayavārdhāṅguladvaṃdvamaṣṭamaṃ tvaṅguladvayam
aṅgulaṃ sayavadvaṃdvaṃ navamaṃ daśamaṃ punaḥ 312
yavonamaṅguladvaṃdvamantaratritayaṃ punaḥ
itaṃ parasyāḥ pratyekaṃ sapādāṅgulasaṃmitam 313
viśeṣo'yamihānyattu lakṣma syādbṛhatīgatam
laghvīdaṇḍagataṃ dairghye syātpañcatriṃśadaṅgulam 314
paridhistu tṛtīyāṃśābhyadhikāṅgulapañcakaḥ
aṅgulatrayavistāraṃ kakubhasya śiro bhavet 315
pūrvavatkākubho daṇḍo vīṇādaṇḍe nidhīyate
pariśeṣastathā sā syādyathā daṇḍāntatantrikā 316
ā kākubhaśiromadhyādbhavettryaṅgulasaṃmitā
daṇḍānte sārikotsedhaḥ syātsārdhāṅgulasaṃmitaḥ 317
tadardhe meḍhakopānte tatrā''dyaṃ sārikāntaram
proktaṃ yavenābhyadhikaiścaturbhirmitamaṅgulaiḥ 318
dvitīyaṃ dviyavopetāṅguladvaṃdvamitaṃ bhavet
yavonābhyāmaṅgulībhyāṃ tṛtīyaṃ mīyate budhaiḥ 319
pādonatryaṅgulaṃ turyamatha pañcamamantaram
yavārdhonāṅguladvaṃdvaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sārdhāṣṭabhiryavaiḥ 320
saptamaṃ tu yavonenāṅguladvaṃdvena mīyate
sādhāṅgulaṃ tvaṣṭamaṃ syānnavamaṃ tvaṣṭabhiryavaiḥ 321
daśamaikādaśe protte sapādāṅgulake pṛthak
yavanyūnāṅgulamitaṃ dvādaśaṃ ca trayodaśam 322
anyattu laghukiṃnaryāṃ lakṣma pūrvavadiṣyate
na bṛhatyadhikaṃ mānaṃ na hīnaṃ triṃśadaṅgulāt 323
ādartavyaṃ kiṃnarīṇāṃ raktimādhuryavarjanāt
etayorantarāle tu yatheṣṭaṃ mānakalpanā 324
śaktā vivektumatrāpi svarasthānāni tadvidaḥ 325
pradarśanārthaṃ keṣāṃcidrāgāṇāṃ vādanakramam
asyāṃ kiṃnaravīṇāyāṃ vakti śrīkaraṇeśvaraḥ 326
sthāyinaṃ madhyamaṃ mandraṃ madhyaṃ vā sthāyatatparān
āruhya panisānpañcāvaroha kramataḥ svarān 327
ṣaḍjataḥ sthāyiparyantādyathā hanyāttu dhaivatam
āruhya panisānpañcāvaroha kramataḥ svarān 327
ṣaḍjataḥ sthāyiparyantādyathā hanyāttu dhaivatam
madhyamādeḥ samākhyātaṃ svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ tadā 328
dvyardhamardhasthitaṃ tadvadāruhya dviguṇaṃ svaram
ādyaṃ svasthānamātiṣṭhetsvasthānaṃ tritaye pare 329
asaṃbhave pūrvapūrvasvarasya tu paraṃ param
krameṇa svaramārohetsarvarāgeṣviti sthitiḥ 330
iti madhyamādiḥ
kiṃnarīvādakāḥ prāyaḥ sthāyinaṃ pañcamaṃ svaram
rāge kurvanti baṅgāle tadajñānavijṛmbhitam 331
graho hi madhyamo rāgasyāsya śāstre prakīrtitaḥ 332
yadvā lakṣyapradhānāni śāstrāṇyetāni manvate
tasmāllakṣyaviruddhaṃ yattacchāstraṃ neyamanyathā 333
madhyamādipadaṃ jñeyaṃ pañcamādyupalakṣaṇam 334
prāthamyasāmyato yadvā niyamādṛṣṭakalpanā
madhyamādigrahe kāryā nanvasminmadhyame grahe 335
adhastanaḥ syādgāndhāro madhyamaḥ pañcamagrahe
turyādivyatyaye'pyevaṃ rāgasāmyaṃ kathaṃ bhavet 336
niḥśaṅko'tra samādhatte gāndhārādyaprayojakam
kiṃtu sthāyinamārabhya ye syusturyādayaḥ svarāḥ 337
rāgābhivyaktiśaktāḥ ye syusturyādayaḥ svarāḥ 337
rāgābhivyaktiśaktāḥ syurnanu śāstreṣvanarthikā
ṣaḍjādyuktiḥ prasajyeta satyaṃ tatrocyate tvidam 338
madhyamādigrahaḥ śāstre niyatastadapekṣayā
turyādayo niṣādādyā bhavantītyupapadyate 339
grahāṃśanyāsaniyamau yadvā śāstrārthagocaraḥ
gumphaḥ svarāntarāṇāṃ tu lakṣyasthāne virudhyate 340
sarvatra parihāro'yaṃ lakṣye lakṣmāvirodhini
deśīrāgeṣu nirṇītaḥ śārṅgadevena sūriṇā 341
madhyamaṃ sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā gāndhārāttadadhastanāt
pañcā''ruhya niṣādāntāngaparyantāvarohaṇam 342
kṛtvā sthāyiniṣādau ca vidhāyā''hatya dhaivatam
sthāyyantamavarohaścedbaṅgālo jāyate tadā 343
iti baṅgālaḥ
dhaivataṃ sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā tṛtīyaṃ ca caturthakam
tasmātkṛtvā'varoheṇa sthāyiparyantametya ca 344
tṛtīyaṃ ca tato'dhasthaṃ vidhāya sthāyinaṃ vrajet
yadā tadā bhairavaḥ syāllakṣye sthāyī nirīkṣyate 345
iti bhairavaḥ
sthāyino dhaivatātpūrvaṃ svaramāgatya tatparān
pañcānāruhya turyaṃ ca dvitīyaṃ dviḥ prayujyate 346
sthāyini nyasyate rāgo varāṭī jāyate tadā
lakṣye tu dṛśyate sthāyī kiṃnaryāmṛṣabhasvaraḥ 347
iti varāṭī
sthāyinaṃ madhyamṛṣabhaṃ kṛtvā dvau tadadhastanau
gatvā sthāyinamārabhya trīnāruhyāvaruhya tu 348
pañca svarānnyasyate cedṛṣabhe gurjarī tadā
graho gāndhāra evāsyā dṛśyate lakṣyagocare 349
iti gurjarī
madhyaṣaḍjādgrahātpūrvaṃ svarametya tṛtīyakam 350
turyaṃ coktvā dvitīyādīṃstrīnāruhyāvaruhya ca
grahanyāsādvasantaḥ syāllakṣye tvasyarṣabho grahaḥ 351
iti vasantaḥ
madhyaṣaḍjaṃ grahaṃ kṛtvā tṛtīyaṃ ca caturthakam
uktvā dvitīyatṛtīyau spṛṣṭvā turyaṃ ca pañcamam 352
etatkrameṇāvarohastyaktvā sthāyidvitīyakam
sthāyipūrvāntamāgatya grahaṃ tasmāttṛtīyakam 353
turyaṃ tatastṛtīyaṃ ca procyate nyasyate gṛhe
dhannāsī syāttadā dṛṣṭo lakṣye syātpañcamo grahaḥ 354
iti dhannāsī
ṛṣabhaṃ sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā paraṃ spṛṣṭvā tataḥ param
vilambya turyamāndolya spṛṣṭvā turyādadhastanau 355
grahānniḥsaraṇaṃ kṛtvā parau procya dvitīyakam
etya sthāyini cennyāso deśīrāgastadā bhavet
gāndhārastu graho deśyāṃ deśīvediṣu dṛśyate 356
iti deśī
sthāyinaṃ madhyagāndhāraṃ kṛtvā'dhasturyametya ca
tasmādāṣaṣṭhamāruhya svarāṃstānavaruhya ca 357
grahādhastāttṛtīyaṃ ca kṛtvā cennyasyate grahe
deśākhyā sā tadā lakṣye dṛṣṭo'syā madhyamo grahaḥ 358
iti deśāntarākhyā
grahānmadhyasthitātṣaḍjāddvitīyaṃ svarametya cet
tṛtīyaṃ tadadhasthaṃ ca vilambya sthāyinaṃ spṛśet 359
adhastṛtīyaturyau ca taṃ tṛtīyaṃ punaḥ svaram
kṛtvā sthāyisvare nyāso ḍombakrī jāyate tadā
bhūpālī sā janairuktā sthāyyasyā madhyamo mataḥ 360
iti ḍombakrī
mandrasthaṃ pañcamaṃ kṛtvā sthāyinaṃ saha tena ca 361
āruhya ṣaṭsvarāneṣāmavarohe tṛtīyakam
vilambya kampitaṃ kṛtvā turyaṃ sthāyinamāvrajet 362
krameṇa yadi jāyeta tadā prathamamañjarī
asyāstu mandragāndhāraḥ sthāyī lakṣyeṣu dṛśyate 363
iti prathamamañjarī
dhaivataṃ sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā''ndolya tasmādavarohe gṛhaṃ vrajet
yadā tadā syātkāmodā madhyamo'syā graho bhavet 364
iti kāmodā
ṣaḍjaṃ tu sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā tatpūrvasvarametya ca 365
svaradvayaṃ dvirāruhya tṛtīyaṃ ca caturthakam
prakampyātha tṛtīyaṃ ca vilambyā''hatya pañcamam 366
sthāyyantamavaroheccetsvarānṛṣabhavarjitān
madhyaṣaḍjaṃ grahaṃ kṛtvā saha prācā parau svarau 367
procya turyaṃ vilambyātha tṛtīyaṃ sadvitīyakam
spṛṣṭvā yadā grahe nyāsastadā rāmakṛtirbhavet 368
iti rāmakṛtiḥ
sthāyinaṃ madhyamaṃ kṛtvā'dhaścaturthamupetya ca
grahādharāṃstrīnāruhya svaraṃ spṛṣṭvā tṛtīyakam 369
grahādhasturyaparyantamāgatyāpyavarohiṇā
tatastṛtīyamāruhya karamādetya prakampya ca 370
grahe nyāso yadā rāgastadā gauḍakṛtirbhavet
pañcamo lakṣyate sthāyī lakṣye syāllakṣyavedibhiḥ 371
iti gauḍakṛtiḥ
madhyaṣaḍjaṃ grahaṃ kṛtvā'dhastametya punargraham
kṛtvā tṛtīyaturyau ca kampitaṃ pañcamaṃ svaram 372
vādayitvā grahātturyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sthāyinaṃ tathā
dvitīyaṃ kampayitvā ca tṛtīyaṃ svaramāspṛśet 373
tato yadi grahe nyāsastadā devakṛtirbhavet
asyāstu madhyamo nyāso lakṣye śrīśārṅgiṇoditaḥ 374
iti devakṛtiḥ
dhaivataṃ sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā gatvā'dhasthaṃ punargraham 375
tatparaṃ sthāyinaṃ tasmātpūrvamāgatya ca graham
āruhya trīṃstṛtīyādīnpañcamādavaruhya ca 376
ṣaṭsvarāngrahamuccārya tṛtīyaṃ kampayettataḥ
turyapañcamaturyāṃśca spṛṣṭvā procya tṛtīyakam 377
dvitīyaṃ ca grahe nyāso yadā syādbhairavī tadā
asya rāgasya gāndhāraḥ sthāyī lakṣyeṣu dṛśyate 378
iti bhairavī
mandraṣaḍjaṃ grahaṃ kṛtvā madhyaṣaḍjamupetya ca
avarohikramādetya grahamārohiṇā tataḥ 379
pamāgatya vilambyāmuṃ spṛṣṭvā dhaivatamāvrajet
grahāccedavaroheṇa cchāyānaṭṭā tadā bhavet 380
iti cchāyānaṭṭā
madhyaṣaḍjaṃ grahaṃ kṛtvā tatparau dvau ca pañcamam 381
ṣaṣṭhaṃ kṛtvā'varuhyemau tṛtīyādavaruhya ca
āgrahaṃ prāktṛtīyādvā'varuhya prāktṛtīyakam 382
prakampya tatparaṃ procya grahe cennyasyate tadā
rāmakrī syādasau proktā bahulīpūrvikā janaiḥ 383
iti bahulīrāmakrī
dhaivataṃ sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā tatparaṃ tu vilambitam
spṛṣṭvā grahadvitīyau ca grahāttu prāktṛtīyakam 384
īṣadvilambya cā''ruhya grahādīnvā tṛtīyakam
vilambya tadadhasthaṃ vā'varuhya sparśanāttataḥ 385
grahādīṃstrīnsvarānspṛṣṭvā grahamuccārya tatparam
grahe cennyasyate rāgo mahlāro jāyate tadā
rāge'tra pañcamaḥ sthāyī dṛśyate lakṣyagocaraḥ 386
iti mahlāraḥ
sthāyino madhyamātpañcāavrucca yadi māvadhīn
māttṛtīyaṃ vrajettasmādāruhya caturaḥ svarān 387
spṛṣṭvā sthāyinametasmātpūrvaṃ kṛtvā vilambitam
ṣaḍje cennyasyate gauḍakarṇāṭo jāyate tadā 388
lakṣye tu pañcamasthāyī gauḍasyāsya vilokyate
grahānmandraniṣādāccetsamuccārya svaraṃ param 389
tatastṛtīyaturyau ca gatvā sthāyitṛtīyakam
sthāyituryaṃ ca kṛtvā'smātsvarānpañcāvaruhya ca 390
sthāyino'dhasturīyaṃ ca sthāyino'dhastanaṃ tataḥ
sthāyinaṃ tatparaṃ coktvā sthāyituryaṃ grahātparam 391
kṛtvā nyāso grahe gauḍastauruṣko jāyate tadā
sa eva mālavītyuktaḥ sthāyī lakṣye'sya pañcamaḥ 392
iti turuṣkagauḍaḥ
grahaṃ mālavinaḥ kṛtvā dvitīyaṃ procya tatparam
laṅghayellaṅghitādūrdhvaṃ trīṇyāruhyāvaruhya ca 393
prakampya laṅghitaṃ tasmātpūrvaṃ procya grahaṃ vrajet
yadā tadā drāviḍaḥ syādgauḍo'sau sālako janaiḥ 394
asyāpi sthāyinaṃ prāhurlakṣyajñāḥ pañcamaṃ svaram 395
iti drāviḍagauḍaḥ
ityupāṅgāni
sthānino dhaivatātprācyādavaruhya grahāntaram
grahaṃ coktvā tṛtīyaṃ ca turyaṃ kṛtvā vilambitam 396
tasmādadhastanau spṛṣṭvā tṛtīyaṃ tu vilambayet
spṛṣṭvā grahātparaṃ pūrvau pūrvaṃ coktvā grahe yadi 397
kampite nyasyate rāgastadā syāllalitābhidhaḥ
atra gāndhāramevā''huḥ sthāyinaṃ lakṣyavedinaḥ 398
iti lalitaḥ
evaṃ katipaye rāgāḥ proktāḥ saṃmugdhabuddhaye
vastutaḥ sarvayantreṣu rāgāṇāṃ vādanaṃ samam 399
grahādisvarasaṃbhūtidvārato'nviṣyatāṃ budhaiḥ
kiṃnaryāṃ yaiḥ svaraiḥ svasvasthānajairyasya saṃbhavaḥ
rāgasya tasya taireva vaṃśādāvapi dṛśyate 400
iti kiṃnarīlakṣaṇam
pinākyāṃ dhanuṣaḥ kamraikacatvāriṃśadaṅgulā
dairghye syānmadhyavistāraḥ syātsapādāṅguladvayam 401
ante cāṅgulamānena śikhāṃ kuryādadhastanīm
sapādāṅgulamātrā tu kārmukasyottarā śikhā 402
kāryāvaṅguladairghyau ca pādonāṅgulapiṇḍakau
khaṭakau śikhayorlagnau tābhyāṃ tvarvāgupāntayoḥ 403
pādonamaṅguladvaṃdvaṃ vistāre mānamiṣyate
madhyaprāntāntarāle tu vistāraṃ kalpayetsudhīḥ 404
tantrīmānena badhnīyācchikhayornipuṇo guṇam
mānaṃ vādanacākpe syādaṅgulānyekaviṃśatiḥ 405
dairghye muṣṭau tu vistāro'trāṅgulitritayānmitaḥ
sa tvaṅgulatṛtīyāṃśaḥ śikhe tyaktvā'ntayorbhavet 406
ūrdhvādharaśikhādvaṃdvamānaṃ pādonamaṅgulam
aśvavāladhikeśottho guṇo vādanadhanvanaḥ 407
tumbaṃ dhṛtvā'tha pādābhyāṃ bhuvi nyastamadhomukham
tatra lagnaśikhā'thordhvā pinākīskandhasaṃśritā 408
ākramya vāmahastasthatumbamūlena tadguṇam
tato dakṣiṇahastasthadhanuṣo vādayejjyayā 409
rālāsaṃliptayā'syāṃ ca svarasthānāni nirṇayet
ekatantrīvadadharādharatāratayā sudhīḥ 410
iti pinākīlakṣaṇam
badhyaprāntātirikteṃ'śe tantrī dairghye catuṣkarā 411
uparisthe kvacitkāṣṭhe prāntenaikena badhyate
prāntāntareṇānyakāṣṭhe'dhasthe sārdhakarāyate 412
sthaulyenā''lāpinītulye tyaktvā'grādaṅguladvayam
tantrīṃ baddhvā tato'dhastāttumbamābadhya dāru tat 413
vāmorumūlākrāntāgraṃ jaṅghāyāṃ kuñcitākṛtau
bhūlagnabāhyapārśvāyāṃ vāmāyāṃ nyasya jaṅghayā 414
ākramya vāmetarayā pinākyāmiva vādanam
dhanuṣā vāmahastasthatumbakena ca sāraṇā 315
ārdracarmakṛtāṃ śuṣkāṃ peśīṃ koṇānvitāṃ kharām
vāmenā''dāya tatkoṇenāthavā sāraṇā bhavet 416
yatra niḥśaṅkavīṇā sā śārṅgadevena śīrtitā
tristhānasvararāgādivyaktiḥ saṃjāyate tayā 417
iti niḥśaṅkavīṇālakṣaṇam
yathā yathā svare vyaktī rakteḥ pracuratā bhavet
tathā tathā vidhātavyaṃ tataṃ lokānusārataḥ 418
samyaksvaropayogīni tantrīvādyāni kānicit
uktānyanyānapi prājñastarkayedanayā diśā 419
yo vīṇāvādanaṃ vetti tattvataḥ śrutijātivit 420
tālapātakalābhijñaḥ so'kleśānmokṣamṛcchati
tasmādvīṇā niṣevyeti yājñavalkyādayo'bruvan 421
nādaśrutisvaragrāmajātirāgāditattvavit
dehasauṣṭhavasaṃpannaḥ sthirāsanaparigrahaḥ 422
jitaśramakaradvaṃdvastyaktabhītirjitendriyaḥ
pragalbhadhīḥ suśārīro gītavādanakovidaḥ
sāvadhānamanāśceti vaiṇike varṇitā guṇāḥ 423
iti tatavādyalakṣaṇam
vaiṇavaḥ khādiro dāntaścāndano raktacandanaḥ
āyasaḥ kāṃsyajo raupyo vaṃśaḥ syātkāñcano'thavā 424
vartulaḥ saralaḥ ślakṣṇo granthibhedavraṇojjhitaḥ
kaniṣṭhāṅgulivistāraṃ garbhe ca suṣiraṃ dadhat 425
svadairghyamānadairghyaṃ ca samākṛti samantataḥ
tasya dve trīṇi catvāri cāṅgulāni śiraḥsthalāt 426
tyaktvā phūtkārasuṣiraṃ kāryamaṅgulasaṃmitam
mukharandhrāntare randhraṃ bhavedekāṅgulāntaram 427
ardhāṅgulāntarāṇi syū randhrāṇyanyāni sapta ca
tānyaṣṭau badarībījasaṃkāśāni pracakṣate 428
vaṃśo'dhaḥ sarvarandhrebhyaḥ pariśeṣyo'ṅguladvayam
teṣu svaravibhāgāya sapta randhrāṇi manvate 429
nādahetormārutasya nirgamāyāṣṭamaṃ matam
phūtkāraprabhavo vāyuḥ pūryate mukharandhrataḥ 430
vaṃśasthairnavabhī randhrairekavīro nigadyate 431
vaṃśasya mukharandhrasya tārarandhrasya cāntare
ekaikāṅgulavṛddhyā syuranye vaṃśāścaturdaśa
aṣṭādaśāṅgulādvaṃśādetadaṅgulavardhanam 432
ardhāṅgulāntarāṇi syū randhrāṇyanyāni sapta ca
tānyaṣṭau badarībījasaṃkāśāni pracakṣate 428
vaṃśo'dhaḥ sarvarandhrebhyaḥ pariśeṣyo'ṅguladvayamsvaravibhāgāya sapta randhrāṇi manvate 429
nādahetormārutasya nirgamāyāṣṭamaṃ matam
phūtkāraprabhavo vāyuḥ pūryate mukharandhrataḥ 430
vaṃśasthairnavabhī randhrairekavīro nigadyate 431
vaṃśasya mukharandhrasya tārarandhrasya cāntare
ekaikāṅgulavṛddhyā syuranye vaṃśāścaturdaśa
aṣṭādaśāṅgulādvaṃśādetadaṅgulavardhanam 432
umāpatirdvyaṅgulaḥ syāttribhistripuruṣo'ṅgulaiḥ
caturmukhaścaturbhiḥ syātpañcavaktrastu pañcabhiḥ 433
ṣaḍaṅgulaḥ ṣaṇmukhaḥ syānmuniḥ saptāṅgulo mataḥ
vasuraṣṭāṅgulaḥ prokto nāthendrastu navāṅgulaḥ 434
daśāṅgulo mahānando rudrastvekādaśāṅgulaḥ 435
dvādaśāṅgula ādityo manurvaṃśaścaturdaśaḥ
kalānidhiḥ ṣoḍaśaḥ syādanvartho'ṣṭādaśāṅgulaḥ 436
avispaṣṭāntaratvena neṣṭaḥ saptadaśāṅgulaḥ
trayodaśāṅgulastadvanna ca pañcadaśāṅgulaḥ 437
muralyākhyo'parairvaṃśo viṃśatyaṅgulakaḥ smṛtaḥ
dvāviṃśatyaṅgulo'pyanyo vaṃśastajjñeṣu dṛśyate 438
taṃ ca śrutinidhiṃ prāhurvaṃśaṃ vaṃśavido janāḥ
atimandradhvanitvena neṣyate'sau vicakṣaṇaiḥ 439
viralāścātitāratvādvaṃśāḥ pañcāṅgulādadhaḥ 440
aṣṭādaśāṅgule vaṃśe svararandhreṣu saptasu 441
mudriteṣu bhavetṣaḍjo mandrasaptakasaṃsthitaḥ
ṣaṭsvevamanyavaṃśeṣu syuḥ kramādṛṣabhādayaḥ 442
ārabhyāṣṭāṅgulādevamadho vaṃśeṣu saptasu
madhyasthānagatāḥ saptodyanti ṣaḍjādayaḥ kramāt 443
tārasthānasthitaḥ ṣaḍjastvekavīrasya jāyate 444
sarveṣveteṣu vaṃśeṣu mukte randhradvaye'ntime
svaro dvitīyo jāyeta tṛtīyādyāstataḥ kramāt
saptamāntāḥ prajāyante tryādirandhravimocanāt 445
mukte tu tārarandhre'nyarandhreṣu pihiteṣu ca
aṣṭamasvarasaṃbhūtiḥ pūrvācāryairudīritā 446
vyaktamuktāṅgulitvena samagro jāyate svaraḥ
aṅgulyāḥ kampane tatra śrutirekā'pacīyate 447
śrutidvayaṃ tvardhamukte tatkampe tu śrutitrayam
anyathā varṇayantīha kecitsaptasvarodayam 448
ardhendunāgaphaṇavadvaṃśe sthāpyaṃ karadvayam
vāmasyānāmikāṅgulyā ṣaḍjo madhyamayā punaḥ 449
ṛṣabhaḥ syātpradeśinyā gāndhāra iti vāmataḥ
trayaḥ svarāḥ prajāyante catvāro dakṣiṇātkarāt 450
kaniṣṭhayā madhyamaḥ syātpañcamo'nāmayā svaraḥ
dhaivataḥ syānmadhyamayā pradeśinyā niṣādavān 451
sthānatrayasya niṣpatti te cāsminnanyathā jaguḥ 452
suśikṣitena racitātphūtkārānmadhyasaptake
jāyante vaiṇaśārīrasvarasaṃvādinaḥ svarāḥ 453
tārasthā mukhasaṃyogasaṃkaṭe mukharandhrake
taṃ vādanaprakāraṃ ca ṭīpāmācakṣate janāḥ 454
tayā yadvādyate randhre tatsvaro dviguṇo bhavet
randhrasya mukhasaṃyogaviprakarṣāttu mandragāḥ 455
tīvrātīvratayā vāyoḥ śīghramantharabhāvataḥ
pūraṇāpūraṇābhyāṃ copacayāpacayāddhvaneḥ
kurvantyekatra randhre'pi tajjñā nānāsvarodayam 456
kampitā valitā muktār'rdhamuktā ca nipīḍitā
iti vaṃśe gatiḥ proktā śārṅgadevena pañcadhā 457
adharasthasya vaṃśasya kampanātkampitā matā
varṇālaṃkāraniṣpattiḥ prayoge'syāḥ prayojanam 458
iti kampitā
bhavetsaṃcāriniṣpattau valitā'ṅgulicālanam
iti valitā
randhre'dhile'ṅgulīmukte muktā syānmuktaśabdakṛt 459
iti muktā
ardhamuktā'rdhamukteḥ syādghṛtaśabdavidhāyinī 460
ityardhamuktā
samantātsarvarandhrāṇi pidhāyāṅgulibhiryadā
vaṃśaṃ pūrayate tajjñastadā jñeyā nipīḍitā 461
iti nipīḍitā
atra kīrtidharastvanyāṃ vyavasthāmabhyupāgamat 462
ṣaṭsaptāṣṭāṅgulā vaṃśāstārasvaravidhāyakāḥ
madhyasvarā navadaśaikādaśāṅgulakāstrayaḥ 463
aṅgulairyo dvādaśabhiḥ syāttrayodaśabhiśca yaḥ
hetū mandrakharāṇām tau sarvavaṃśamayaḥ punaḥ 464
caturdaśāṅgulo vaṃśastristhānasvarasādhakaḥ
varṇālaṃkāradhātvādivādyasaṃvādanakṣamaḥ
evaṃ vaṃśā navaiveti yuktāyuktavido viduḥ 465
keciddeśīvido vaṃśānekavīrādikānamūn
mānāntareṇābhidadhusteṣāṃ matamidaṃ bruve 466
prākcaturdaśavaṃśānte'ṅgulaṃ pañcayavaṃ jaguḥ
caturdaśādivaṃśeṣu sārdhapañcayavaṃ tvidam 467
caturdaśāṅgulaṃ daṇḍamekavīre pracakṣate
sārdhāṅgulau śiraḥprāntau pṛthagjātimukhe'ṅgulam 368
mānaṃ tārādirandhrāṇi pratyekaṃ triyavāni tu
triyavānyantarālāni pṛthaksthāneṣu mūlataḥ
pañcamāṃśonitaṃ mānaṃ kaniṣṭhāparvamadhyataḥ 469
ubhāpatestu vaṃśasya daṇḍaḥ pañcadaśāṅgulaḥ
pādonena yavadvaṃdvenādhiko'syāntareṣu tu 470
sapādatriyavaṃ mānaṃ pṛthaganyattu pūrvavat
aṅgulaiḥ saptadaśabhiryavayugmādhikairmataḥ 471
daṇḍastripuruṣe vaṃśe pṛthagantarasaptake
yavonamaṅgulaṃ mānamaparaṃ lakṣma pūrvavat 472
yavadvayādhikaiḥ sārdhairaṣṭādaśabhiraṅgulaiḥ
mitaścaturmukhe daṇḍe śirontau tu mitau pṛthak 473
pādonābhyāmaṅgulābhyāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pūrvavatparam
yavasya sārdhapādena nyūnā dvāviṃśatirmatā 474
aṅgulānāṃ daṇḍamānaṃ pañcavaktre śirontayoḥ
sārdhāṅguladvayaṃ mānaṃ pṛthagantarasaptake 475
caturyavī sārdhapādābhyadhikā'nyattu pūrvavat
ṣaṇmukhe yavapādābhyāṃ sapādābhyāṃ sahāṅgulaiḥ 476
caturviśatisaṃkhyaiḥ syāddaṇḍaḥ parimitastataḥ
tārādyaṣṭasu randhreṣu sapādatriyavā mitiḥ 477
pratyekamantarāleṣu ṣoḍaśāṃśonitā yavāḥ
pañcamānamitāḥ śeṣaṃ lakṣma syātpañcavaktravat 478
ataḥ pareṣu vaṃśeṣu saptasvabhyadhiko yavaḥ
svamānāddṛśyate tārarandhre jātimukhāntare 479
yuktā yavena sārdhenāṅgulaṣaḍviṃśatirbhavet
pramāṇaṃ munidaṇḍasya mānaṃ randhrāṣṭake punaḥ 480
yavatrayaṃ sārdhapādādhikaṃ pratyekamīritam
pṛthakpañcayavī sārdhāntareṣvanyattu pūrvavat 481
vasuvaṃśe tu daṇḍasyāṅgulāṣṭāviṃśatirmitiḥ
yavādhikā jātimukhaṃ yavenādhikamaṅgulam 482
yavādhikāṅgulā jñeyā'ntarāleṣu mitiḥ pṛthak
śeṣaṃ tu pūrvavadvaṃśe nāthendre daṇḍasaṃmitiḥ 483
yavapādādhikā triṃśadaṅgulānāṃ śirontayoḥ
pādonatryaṅgulaṃ mānaṃ pṛthagantarasaptake 484
sapādamaṅgulaṃ mānaṃ khānimānaṃ tu mūlataḥ
ṣaṣṭhabhāgavihīnaṃ syātkaniṣṭhāparva madhyamam 485
aparaṃ pūrvavaddaṇḍe mahānandasya saṃmitiḥ
pādanyūnena pādena yavasyābhyadhikā bhavet 486
dvātriṃśadaṅgulānāṃ tacchirontau tryaṅgulau pṛthak
sapādāṅgulakaṃ jātimukhamantarasaptake 487
pṛthaksapādapādenābhyadhikaṃ mānamaṅgulam
mūlataḥ saptamāṃśonakaniṣṭhāmadhyaparvaṇā 488
mitā khāniḥ paraṃ lakṣma pūrvoktaṃ tairudīritam
sapādāni catustriṃśadaṅgulāni mitirbhavet 489
yavārdhasahitā rudradaṇḍasyāntarasaptake
sārdhāṅgulaṃ pṛthaṅmānaṃ kaniṣṭhāmadhyaparvaṇā 490
khāniḥ parimitā śeṣaṃ pūrvavaṃśavadiṣyate
saptatriṃśadyavārdhenābhyadhikānyaṅgulāni tu 491
āditye daṇḍamānaṃ syātpādonaṃ tvaṅguladvayam
antareṣu pṛthaṅmānamaparaṃ rudravaṃśavat 492
manordaṇḍe sapādaikonacatvāriṃśadaṅgulam
yavādhikaṃ sapādenāṣṭamāṃśena yavasya ca 493
sahitaṃ mānamākhyātaṃ pādanyūnāṅgulatrayaiḥ
śiraḥprāntau pṛthagjātimukhe syānmānamaṅgulam 494
ṣoḍaśāṃśonapādenādhikaṃ randhrāṣṭake punaḥ
yavatrayaṃ pṛthaksārdhamantareṣu ca saptasu 495
pratyekaṃ yavapādena nyūnaṃ syādaṅguladvayam
mānaṃ lakṣaṇamanyattu bhavedādityavaṃśavat 496
yavasya sārdhapādābhyāṃ dvātriṃśāṃśe navādhikaiḥ
catuścatvāriṃśatā syātpādonairaṅgulairmitaḥ 497
daṇḍaḥ kalānidhau vaṃśe śirontau tryaṅgulau pṛthak
antareṣvaṅguladvaṃdvaṃ samādayavasaṃyutam 498
pratyekaṃ mānamākhyātaṃ lakṣmānyanmanuvanmatam
yavasyāṣṭamabhāgena sapādena samanvitaiḥ 499
aṣṭācatvāriṃśatā syātpādonaiḥ saṃmito'ṅgulaiḥ
aṣṭādaśāṅgule daṇḍaḥ sārdhaṃ tvaṅgulayordvayam 500
antareṣu pṛthaṅmānaṃ vedyamanyatkalānidheḥ
nātra trayodaśo vaṃśo nava pañcadaśāṅgulaḥ 501
iṣyate'lpāntaratvena nava saptadaśāṅgulaḥ
evaṃ pañcadaśaivaite vaṃśāsteṣāmime matāḥ 502
mādhuryaraktisaṃyuktā rāgābhivyaktihetavaḥ
yadidaṃ lakṣma śāstroktaṃ yacca deśīgataṃ matam
te dve rañjayituṃ śakte na manāṃsi kalāvidām 503
vaktre phūtkārarandhrasthe kasya vā vitatākṛteḥ
svararandhrāṇyāpnuyātāmapi dīrghatarau karau 504
antaraṃ svararandhrāṇāmuktamardhāṅgulaṃ ca yat
śāstreṇa tena kasyāpi svarasya vyaktirīritā 505
ye pūrvoktakramāḥ sapta svarāḥ sarigamādayaḥ
mānte'tra dṛśyate teṣāṃ nāsau kramaparikramaḥ 506
mūrchanārāgabhāṣādeḥ sudūrotsāritā kathā
kiṃcchiā!strakṛtāṃ prācāmācāryāṇāṃ mahātmanām 507
upālambho'pi doṣāyakiṃ tayā kathayā'pi naḥ
tadāstāmucyate kiṃtu kiṃciddeśīvidaḥ prati 508
māne pañcayave kasmāddṛśyate'tra yavo'dhikaḥ
saptāṅgulādivaṃśeṣu kathaṃ cordhvaṃ caturdaśāt 509
pūrvamānādhikāḥ santi te saptāṣṭa na vā yavāḥ
sārdhapañcayave māne lakṣma syādavyavasthitam 510
trayodaśādayo ye ca trayo vaṃśā nirākṛtāḥ
na tatra yuktileśo'sti yena tuṣyanti sūrayaḥ 511
alpāntaratvamuktaṃ yattadasārataraṃ punaḥ
vaṃśāntarāntaraistulyamantaraṃ teṣu dṛśyate 512
dvādaśārdhasvaranyūno yathaikādaśavaṃśataḥ
mudritaḥ svararandhraḥ syāttathā tasmāttrayodaśa 513
evaṃ caturdaśātpañcadaśo vaṃśāttu ṣoḍaśāt
vaṃśaḥ saptadaśo lakṣmāprasiddhamiti cenna tat 514
vaktuṃ taduktarītyā hi śakyaṃ teṣvapi lakṣaṇam
trayodaśāṅgulo vaṃśo viśvamūrtirudāhṛtaḥ 515
adhikaṃ yavapādena syāccatvāriṃśadaṅgulam
viśvamūrtau daṇḍamānaṃ tārādau suṣirāṣṭake 516
pratyekaṃ triyavī sārdhā mānamantarasaptake
aṅguladvitayaṃ mānaṃ pṛthagādityavatparam 517
yuktairyavena pādena dvācatvāriṃśadaṅgulaiḥ
yavasya cāṣṭamāṃśena sapādenādhikairmataḥ 518
daṇḍaḥ śrīśārṅgidevokto vaṃśe pañcadaśāṅgule
aṅgulatritayaṃ mānaṃ śiraḥprānte pradeśayoḥ 519
pratyekamantarāleṣu saptasvardhayavādhikam
aṅguladvitayaṃ mānaṃ manuvallakṣaṇaṃ param 520
yavasya sārdhapādena dvātriṃśāṃśayavādhikaḥ
ṣaṭcatvāriṃśatā pādanyūnaiḥ syādaṅgulairmitaḥ 521
daṇḍaḥ saptadaśe vaṃśe'ntareṣu tvaṅguladvayam
pṛthaksadviyavaṃ mānaṃ syātkalānidhivatparam 522
evaṃm prasiddhalakṣmāṇo rāgādivyaktihetavaḥ
trayodaśādayo vaṃśā niṣidhyante mudhā paraiḥ 523
api pañcayavaṃ mānaṃ vaṃśeṣvaṣṭādaśasvapi
vyaktaṃ pragalbhate vaktuṃ sūriḥ śrīkaraṇāgraṇīḥ 524
tasmāddeśyanusāreṇa lakṣyalakṣaṇatattvavit
śārṅgadevo'nyathā vaṃśasvarūpaṃ pratyapādayat 525
aṅgulaṃ nibabandhāsau siddhaṃ gaṇitaśāstrataḥ
na hyaṅgulaṃ pañcayavaṃ dṛśyate śāstralokayoḥ 526
aṅgulaṃ nistuṣaiḥ ṣaḍbhistiryagbhiḥ syādyavodaraiḥ
dakṣiṇasya karasya syātkhānimāne kaniṣṭhikā 527
na sthūlā na kṛśā'tyantaṃ gṛhyate kiṃtu madhyamā
mūlaśabdo'nvayaṃ yāti madhyamenātra parvaṇā 528
samā sarvatra khāniḥ syādviṣamaḥ svarabhaṅgakṛt
caturdaśāṅgulo daṇḍa ekavīrasya dṛśyate 529
tacchiraḥprāntayormānamaṅguladvitayaṃ pṛthak
mānaṃ jātimukhākhye syātphūtkārasuṣire'ṅgulam 530
tārarandhraṃ tato'dhastādaṅgulāntaritaṃ matam
ṣoḍaśāṃśādhikaṃ tasya mānamardhāṅgulaṃ viduḥ 531
tadadho'dhaśca tāvanti sapta randhrāṇi kalpayet
teṣvaṣṭāsūrdhvarandhrāṇi sapta syuḥ svarasiddhaye 532
antimaṃ vāyurandhraṃ syādantarālāni sapta ca
pṛthagardhāṅgulānyeṣāṃ garbharandhraṃ tu saṃmitam 533
mūlataḥ pañcamāṃśena kaniṣṭhāmadhyaparvaṇā
khānistaducyate deśyāṃ pūrvoktoktasvarodayaḥ 534
trayodaśāpare tārarandhrajātimukhāntare
ekaikāṅgulavṛddhyā syurācaturdaśa vaṃśataḥ 535
ubhāpatau daṇḍamānaṃ bhavetpañcadaśāṅgulam
ekavīravadanyattu lakṣma tripuruṣe punaḥ 536
daṇḍaḥ sārdhaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ sayacārdhairmito'ṅgulaiḥ
saptāntarāṇyadhastārātpṛthaksārdhaistribhiryavaiḥ 537
mitāni lakṣaṇaṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvavaṃśavadiṣyate
daṇḍaścaturmukhe sārdhāṣṭādaśāṅgulasaṃmitaḥ 538
pṛthaksaptāntarālāni caturbhiḥ sāṅghribhiryavaiḥ
mitāni pūrvavaccheṣaṃ pañcavaktre tu saṃmitiḥ 539
daṇḍaśca yavayugmenābhyadhikāṅgulaviṃśatiḥ
saptānāmantarālānāṃ pṛthakpañcayavā mitiḥ 540
anyattu pūrvavallakṣma daṇḍamānaṃ tu ṣaṇmukhe
aṅgulānāṃ yavārdhānāṃ dvāviṃśatirudīritā 541
sārdhapañcayavaṃ mānaṃ pṛthagantarasaptake
śeṣaṃ tu pūrvavadvaṃśe munau daṇḍamitiḥ punaḥ 542
trayoviṃśatyaṅgulānāṃ sārdhayā'ntarasaptake
pratyekamaṅgulaṃ mānaṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvavadiṣyate 543
vaṃśe vasau daṇḍamānaṃ caturbhiradhikā yavaiḥ
pañcaviṃśatirākhyātā'ṅgulānāmantarāṇi tu 544
pṛthaksaptayavāni syuḥ śeṣaṃ pūrvoktamiṣyate
nāyendradaṇḍamānaṃ syātsapādā saptaviṃśatiḥ 545
aṅgulānāmantarāṇi syuḥ sapādāṅgulāni tu
pratyekaṃ mūlataḥ ṣaṣṭhāṃśonamadhyamaparvaṇā 546
kaniṣṭhāy mitā khāniranyallakṣma tu pūrvavat
mānaṃ mahānandadaṇḍe triṃśadaṅgulakaṃ mitam 547
pṛthaksārdhāṅgulāni syurantarālāni sapta ca
nāyendravatparaṃ lakṣma rudre daṇḍamitiḥ punaḥ 548
sapādāni trayastriṃśadaṅgulānyantareṣu tu
pratyekaṃ mānāmākhyātaṃ pādanyūnāṅguladvayam 549
randhreṣvaṣṭasu tārādiṣvaṣṭamāṃśasamanvitam
aṅgulārdhaṃ pṛthaṅmānaṃ khānermānaṃ tu mūlataḥ 550
saptamāṃśavihīnaṃ syātkaniṣṭhāparva madhyamam
pūrvoktamanyadāditye vaṃśe daṇḍastu kathyate 551
aṣṭamāṃśādhikaiḥ pañcatriṃśatā saṃmito'ṅgulaiḥ
antareṣvaṣṭamāṃśonamaṅguladvitayaṃ pṛthak 552
mānaṃ khānau tvaṣṭamāṃśanyūnamāmūladeśataḥ
kaniṣṭhāmadhyapūrvoktamaparaṃ rudravaṃśavat 553
ṣoḍaśāṃśonitaiḥ sārdhaiḥ saptatriṃśanmitāṅgulaiḥ
mito daṇḍo viśvamūrtāvantareṣvaṅguladvayam 554
ṣoḍaśāṃśādhikaṃ māne pratyekaṃ madhyaparvaṇā
kaniṣṭhāyā mitā khāniḥ śeṣamādityavaṃśavat 555
manau daṇḍastu pādonaikacatvāriṃśatā'ṅgulaiḥ
mitaḥ śiraḥ pṛthakprāntadeśau sārdhāṅguladvayau 556
pratyekamantarāleṣu sapādadvyaṅgulā mitiḥ
viśvamūrtivadanyattu lakṣma śrīśārṅgiṇoditam 557
vaṃśāścaturdaśaivaivaṃ proktāḥ soḍhalasūnunā
nātaḥ paraṃ tu vaṃśānāmīdṛśāmasti saṃbhavaḥ 558
teṣu randhrāṅgulīprāptirna kasyāpi hi dṛśyate
atimandradhvanitvācca na te tattvavidāṃ matāḥ 559
gatānugatikatvena tvekavīrādayastrayaḥ
kathitā na tu teṣvasti raktimādhuryadhuryatā 560
caturmukhādayastasmādekādaśa manoharāḥ
svamate'bhyupagamyante vaṃśāḥ soḍhalasūnunā 561
śārṅgadevo'nyamāṅgena vaṃśarūpaṃ nyarūpayat
tiryagyavodaraiḥ sārdhaiścaturbhirnistuṣairiha 562
aṅgulaṃ tena pūrvoktarītyā'rabhyaikavīrataḥ
santi dvāviṃśatirvaṃśaviśeṣāsteṣu cakṣmahe 563
vaṃśaśreṇīmimāmāha niḥśaṅkonupamābhidhām
ekavīre daṇḍamānaṃ syātṣoḍaśabhiraṅgulaiḥ 564
sārdhena yavapādenābhyadhikaistacchirontayoḥ
sārdhāṅguladvayaṃ mānaṃ pratyekaṃ parikīrtitam 565
sapādamaṅgulaṃ jātimukhe randhrāṣṭake punaḥ
pṛthaktṛtīyabhāgonamaṅgulaṃ mānamiṣyate 566
ardhāṅgulāni pratyekamantarālāni sapta ca
mūlataḥ pañcamāṃśonakaniṣṭhāmadhyaparvaṇā 567
mitā khānirbhavettārarandhrajātimukhāntaram
ekavīrādisaṃjñābhiḥ sarvavaṃśeṣu veditam 568
aṣṭamāṃśavihīnena yavārdhenādhikairbhavet
aṅgulaiḥ saptadaśabhirdaṇḍamānamumāpatau 569
ekāṅgulādhikastasmāddaṇḍastripuruṣe mataḥ
anayorvaṃśayoḥ śeṣaṃlakṣma syādekavīravat 570
mānaṃ syādyavapādonavihīnāṅgulaviṃśatiḥ
caturmukhasya daṇḍasyāntarāleṣvardhamṅgulam 571
aṣṭamāṃśādhikaṃ mānaṃ pṛthaganyattu pūrvavat
sadvātriṃśāṃśapādonadviyavādhikayā mitaḥ 572
daṇḍo'ṅgulaikaviṃśatyā pañcavaktrasya kīrtitaḥ
aṅgulasya tribhiḥ pādaiḥ pādonairantarāṇi tu 573
mitāni sapta pratyekaṃ pūrvavallakṣaṇaṃ param
sārdhatrayoviṃśatiḥ syādaṅgulānāṃ yavādhikā 574
mānaṃ ṣaṇmukhadaṇḍasya pādonaṃ tvaṅgulatrayam
pṛthakśirontayormānamantareṣu tu saptasu 575
yavapādādhikaṃ mānaṃ pṛthakpādonamaṅgulam
śeṣaṃ tu pūrvavadvaṃśe munau syāddaṇḍasaṃmitiḥ 576
yavapādonapādonāṅgulaṣaḍviṃśatirmatā
aṅgulaṃ yavapādena nyūnamantarasaptake 577
pratyekaṃ mānamākhyātaṃ śeṣaṃ ṣaṇmukhavaṃśavat
vasau vaṃśe yavārdhena dvātriṃśāṃśayujā'dhikā 578
syātsaptaviṃśatiḥ sārdhāṅgulānāṃ daṇḍasaṃmitiḥ
sapādena tu pādena dvātriṃśāṃśayutena ca 579
yavasyābhyadhikaṃ mānamantareṣvaṅgulaṃ pṛthak
śeṣaṃ tu munivaddaṇḍe nāthendrasya tu saṃmitiḥ 580
yavāṣṭamāṃśasahitairekonatriṃśadaṅgulaiḥ
sārdhapādādhikairuktā pṛthaktu suṣirāṣṭake 581
tārādau yavayuktenārdhāṅgulena mitirbhavet
antareṣu pṛthaṅmānamaṣṭamāṃśāhdikāṅgulam 582
śeṣaṃ tu pūrvavaddaṇḍe mahānandasya saṃmitiḥ
uktā yavāṣṭamāṃśonāṅgulaikatriṃśadiṣyate 583
sapādāvantarāleṣu pratyekaṃ mānamucyate
sapādamaṅgulaṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvokaṃ śārṅgiṇoditam 584
aṅgulāni trayastriṃśadaṣṭamāṃśayutāni tuā!ṣṭamāṃśayuktāni rudre daṇḍamitirbhavet 585
yuktaṃ sārdhena pādenāṅgu lamantarasaptake
pratyekaṃ mānamākhyātaṃ khānermānaṃ tu mūlataḥ 586
ṣaṣṭhāṃśonaṃ madhyaparva kaniṣṭhāyāḥ prakīrtitam
śeṣaṃ prāgvadathā''ditye syātpañcatriṃśatāṅgulaiḥ 587
yavāṣṭamāṃśasahitairdaṇḍamānamathāntaram
sārdhāṅgulakamekaikaṃ khānimānaṃ tu kīrtitam 588
mūlataḥ saptamāṃśonaṃ kaniṣṭhāparva madhyamam
prāgvatparam viśvamūrtau vaṃśe daṇḍasya saṃmitiḥ 589
yavāṣṭamāṃśasahitaiḥ syātsaptatriṃśatā'ṅgulaiḥ
sārdhapādādhikaiḥ śīrṣaṃ prāntau tryaṅgulakau pṛthak 590
antareṣvaṣṭamāṃśonāhdikaṃ sārdhāṅgulaṃ pṛthak
mānaṃ khānestvaṣṭamāṃśanyūnamāmūlato matam 591
madhyaparva kaniṣṭhāyāḥ pūrvoktaṃ śeṣamiṣyate
daṇḍamānaṃ manorekonacatvāriṃśatāṅgulaiḥ 592
sapādairaṣṭamāṃśena yavasya sahitairapi
pādonamaṅguladvaṃdvaṃ pṛthagantarasaptake 593
mānaṃ khānistu mātavyā kaniṣṭhāmadhyaparvaṇā
pūrvoktamaparaṃ sārdhaikacatvāriṃśatā'ṅgulaiḥ 594
yuktairyavasya pādonapādadvaṃdvena saṃmitiḥ
daṇḍe pañcadaśasya syāttataḥ sārdhayavatrayā 595
pratyekamaṣṭarandhrī syādantareṣvaṅguladvayam
aṣṭamāṃśonitaṃ mānaṃ pratyekaṃ manuvatparam 596
kalānidhau sapādaiḥ syāttricatvāriṃśatā'ṅgulaiḥ
yavādhikairdaṇḍamānamantarāleṣu saptasu 597
pratyekamaṅguladvaṃdvaṃ sapādaṃ pūrvavatparam
daṇḍo'ṣṭādaśavaṃśasyāṣṭācatvāriṃśatāṅgulaiḥ 598
yavānvitaiḥ sapādena sapādenādhikairmitaḥ
pratyekaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśonasārdhāṅgulayugaṃ matam 599
pramāṇamantarāleṣu paraṃ pūrvavadiṣyate
daṇḍe tvekonaviṃśasya sāṅghri pañcāśadaṅgulam 600
yavenābhyadhikaṃ mānamantareṣu tu saptasu
pratyekaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśonādhikaṃ sārdhāṅguladvayam 601
prāgvatparaṃ muralyāstu daṇḍamānaṃ yavādhikam
dvāpañcāśadbhavetsārdhāṅgulānāmantarāṇi tu 602
pādonatryaṅgulāni syuḥ pratyekaṃ pūrvavatparam
ekaviṃśe daṇḍamānaṃ pañcapañcāśadaṅgulam 603
sapādaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśenābhyadhikaṃ yavasaṃyutam
aṅgulānyantarāṇi syuḥ pratyekaṃ pūrvavatparam 604
daṇḍamānaṃ śrutinidhāvaṣṭāpañcāśadaṅgulam
sārdhaṃ yavādhikaṃ tasya tvantareṣvaṅgulatrayam 605
sapādaṣoḍaśāṃśonādhikaṃ mānaṃ pṛthaṅmatam
pūrvoktamaparaṃ lakṣma śārṅgadevena kīrtitam 606
sarveṣāmapi vaṃśānāṃ piṇḍaḥ sārdhayavo bhavet
raktimādhuryavirahātpañcavaktrādadhastanāḥ 607
caturmukhādayo vaṃśā neṣṭāḥ śrīśārṅgisūriṇā
deśīvaṃśeṣu sarveṣu mudritātpūrvavaṃśataḥ 608
uttaro mudrito vaṃśaḥ svarārdhenādhiko bhavet
vaṃśānāmalpamānānāṃ sarvatrottaratā matā 609
svarārdhaṃ syādūrdhvatayā dakṣiṇasya kaniṣṭhayā
atastasyā mudritāyāṃ pūrvavaṃśasya jāyate 610
uttaro mudrito vaṃśaḥ pūrvatulyasvarodayaḥ
uddhṛtāṅgulivṛddhau tu tasmādapyuttarottare 611
milanti mudritā vaṃśā yathāsaṃkhyaṃ purātanaiḥ
svaroday'pyaṅgulayaḥ śātrīye laukike tathā 612
yaṃśe tulyaprakārāḥ syuriti śrīśārṅgiṇoditam
ekasvarāṇi randhrāṇi saṃnidhau vyavadhāvapi 613
milanti sarvavaṃśānāmiti brūte harapriyaḥ
mānahīnaṃ tu yadrandhraṃ khānirvā yatra tādṛśī 614
na tadrandhraiḥ svajātīyairmilanti svarabhaṅgataḥ
caturdaśādivaṃśānāmāvaṃśādekavīrataḥ 616
idānīṃ tādṛśīṃ vaṃśasvarūparacanāmimām
śārṅgadevaḥ samācaṣṭe śivānugrahaśuddhadhīḥ 617
vaṃśapaddhatireṣā ca śārṅgavidvābhidhīyate
aṅgulaṃ ṣaḍyavaṃ cātra randhrādimitaye matam 618
ekavīre daṇḍamānaṃ sapādadvādaśāṅgulam
yavārdhenādhikaṃ tasya śirontau dvyaṅgulau pṛthak 619
mukharandhre'ṅgulaṃ mānaṃ tārādau suṣirāṣṭake
pratyekamaṅguladalaṃ teṣāṃ tvantarasapteke 620
yaddvayaṃ pṛthaṅmānamuktaṃ śrīśārṅgisūriṇā
sarvavaṃśeṣu tārasya mukharandhrasya cāntare 621
ekavīrādisaṃjñābhirvijñeyā'ṅgulasaṃmitiḥ
umāpatau tripuruṣe caikaikāṅgulavardhitam 622
daṇḍamānaṃ paraṃ lakṣma tvekavīravadiṣyate
caturmukhe daṇḍamānaṃ pādanyūnayavādhikaiḥ 623
aṅgulaiḥ pañcadaśabhiḥ sārdhairasyāntareṣu tu
sapādadviyavī mānaṃ pṛthaganyattu pūrvavat 624
sapādaiḥ saptadaśabhiḥ pādanyūnayavādhikaiḥ
aṅgulairdaṇḍamānaṃ syātpañcavaktre'ntareṣu tu 625
ṣoḍaśāṃśādhikā mānaṃ pādonatriyavī pṛthak
śeṣaṃ tu pūrvavaddaṇḍe ṣaṇmukhasya tu saṃmitiḥ 626
yuktā yavena sārdhenāṣṭamāṃśarahitena ca
ekonaviṃśatiḥ proktā'ṅgulānāmantareṣu tu 627
yavasya sārdhapādābhyāṃ yuktamardhāṅgulaṃ pṛthak
pūrvoktamaparaṃ lakṣma daṇḍe tu syānmitirmuneḥ 628
aṅgualirekaviṃśatyā sārdhapādadvayādhikaiḥ
yavasya cāṣṭamāṃśena sapādena samanvitaiḥ 629
sapādayavapādena dvātriṃśāṃśayutena ca
yuktamantaramānaṃ syātpṛthakpādonamaṅgulam 630
śeṣaṃ tu pūrvavallakṣma jñeyaṃ vaṃśe vasau punaḥ
aṣṭamāṃśavihīnena yavenādhikayā bhavet 631
caturviṃśatyaṅgulānāṃ caraṇanyūnayā mitiḥ
pṛthagaṣṭāsu randhreṣu sapādatriyavī mitiḥ 632
pratyekamantarāleṣu sāṣṭamāṃśacavādhikam
pādonamaṅgulaṃ mānamaparaṃ lakṣma pūrvavat 633
sapādayavasaṃyuktā'ṅgulaṣaḍviṃśatirbhavet
mānaṃ nāthendradaṇḍasyāntarāleṣu tu saptasu 634
aṣṭamāṃśādhikaṃ mānamaṅgulaṃ pūrvavatparam
yavṣṭāamāṃśasahitā sārdhāṣṭāviṃśatirmatā 635
aṅgulānāṃ daṇḍamānaṃ mahānande'ntareṣu tu
sapādamaṅgulaṃ sārdhayavapādādhikaṃ pṛthak 636
prāgvatparaṃ rudradaṇḍe tvaṅgulānāṃ yavādhikā
ekatriṃśanmitā mānamatha sārdhayavatrayā 637
pratyekamaṣṭarandhrī syādantarāleṣu saptasu
sārdhāṅgulaṃ pṛthaṅmānaṃ pūrvoktaṃ śeṣamiṣyate 638
daṇḍasya mānamāditye syāccatustriṃśadaṅgulam
yavena ca sapādena dvātriṃśāṃśayujā'dhikam 639
dvātriṃśāśonapādābhyāṃ yavasya sahitaṃ pṛthak
pādonamaṅguladvaṃdvaṃ mānamantarasaptake 640
prāgvatparaṃ viśvamūrtau syātsaptatriṃśadaṅgulam
daṇḍamānaṃ yutaṃ sārdhayavapādadvayena ca 641
pratyekamantarāleṣu tvaṅguladvitayaṃ matam
yavasya sārdhapādenābhyadhikaṃ pūrvatparam 642
daṇḍo yavāṣṭamāṃśena sapādena samanvitaiḥ
catuścatvāriṃśatā syādaṅgulaiḥ saṃmito manoḥ 643
sārdhāṅguladvayamitau śiraḥprāntau matau pṛthak
sādhena ṣoḍaśāṃśena yavasyābhyadhikaistribhiḥ 644
pādonairaṅgulairmānamantarāleṣu saptasu
pūrvoktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śeṣamavocatkaraṇāgraṇīḥ 645
khāniḥ sarveṣu vaṃśeṣu kaniṣṭhāmadhyaparvaṇā
mūlena saṃmitā kāryetyuktaṃ soḍhalasūnunā 646
sarvamanyattu vaṃśānāṃ deśīśāstrabhuvāṃ samam
ekavīrādayo'trāpi trayaḥ prāgvanna saṃmatāḥ 647
dhātūnvṛttitrayaṃ tattvānugataudhāṃśca yānpurā
vādyānyāśrāvaṇādīni vīṇāyāṃ yānyavādiṣam 648
geyaṃ śrutisvaragrāmamūrchanādi ca yanmatam
tatsarvaṃ vaṃśavādye'tra viśeṣeṇopadarśayet 649
vaṃśavīṇāśarīrāṇi trayo'mī svarahetavaḥ
lalito madhuraḥ snigdhasteṣu vaṃśaḥ praśasyate 650
vaṃśavīṇāśarīrāṇāmekībhāvena yo dhvaniḥ
tatra raktiviśeṣasya pramāṇaṃ vibudhā viduḥ 651
adhvanyānāṃ pravāseṣu kāminīnirjiteṣu ca
śokārteṣu prayuñjīta mṛdumadhyalayadhvanim 652
vaṃśaṃ yuñjīta śṛṅgāre drutādilalitadhvanim
kampitasphuritadhvānaṃ vaṃśaṃ drutalayāśrayam
krodhābhimānayoḥ kuryānmataṅgeneti kīrtitam 653
snigdhatā ghanatā raktivyaktiḥ pracuratā dhvaneḥ
lālityaṃ komalatvaṃ ca nādānuraṇanaṃ tathā 654
tristhānatvaṃ śrāvakatvaṃ mādhuryaṃ sāvadhānatā
dvādaśeti guṇāḥ proktāḥ phūtkāre sūriśārṅgiṇā 655
tatra śabdaguṇeṣvevaikādaśa proktalakṣaṇāḥ
phūtkṛteḥ sāvadhānatvaṃ nyūnatādhikyavarjanam 656
iti dvādaśa phūtkāraguṇāḥ
phūtkātro yamalaḥ stokaḥ kṛśaḥ skhalita ityamī
phūtkāradoṣā yamalaṃ bruvate pratiphūtkṛtim
evamanvarthanāmatvānnocyate lakṣaṇaṃ pṛthak 657
kapilastumbakī kākī saṃdaṣṭaścāvyavasthitaḥ
pañceti phūtkṛterdoṣānaparānūcire pare 658
yaḥ kaphopahatādvaktrādvisvaraḥ sphuṭito bhavet
kapilo'sau tumbudhvānaprāyaḥ proktastu tumbakī 659
tāranyūnatayā kākasvaraḥ kākīti kathyate
alpaḥ saṃdaṣṭavadbhāti yo'sau saṃdaṣṭa ucyate
ūno'dhiko vā yo rūkṣaḥ kathyate so'vyavasthitaḥ 660
kaṇṭhasya guṇadoṣā ye puroktāsteṣu kecana
phūtkāre'pi yathāyogaṃ yojanīyā manīṣibhiḥ 661
iti daśa phūtkāradoṣāḥ
aṅgulīsāraṇābhyāsaḥ susthānatvaṃ surāgatā
surāgavyaktimādhuryānvitā vegādgatāgate
gītavādanadakṣatvaṃ gātṝāṃ! sthānadāyitā
taddoṣācchādanaṃ mārgadeśīrāgeṣu kauśalam 663
nā!vadapasthāne rāgodbhūtipragalbhatā
vāṃśikasya guṇānetānvakti śrīkaraṇāgraṇīḥ 664
iti vāṃśikaguṇāḥ
mithyāprayogabāhulyametadguṇaviparyayaḥ
iṣṭasthānānavāptiśca śirasaḥ kampanaṃ tathā
vāṃśikasyeti doṣāḥ syurvarjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ 665
iti vāṃśikadoṣāḥ
ekaḥ syādvāṃśiko mukhyaścatvāro'syānuyāyinaḥ
vāṃśikānāmiti prāyastajjñairvṛndaṃ nigadyate 666
iti vāṃśikavṛndam
adhunā mugdhabodhārthamudāharaṇamātrataḥ
vaṃśe deśīstharāgāṇāṃ keṣāṃcidvādanaṃ bruve 667
madhyamaṃ sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā tṛtīye kampite svare
vilambite dvitīye'tha sthāyini nyasyate yadā
svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ proktaṃ madhyamādestadā budhaiḥ 668
tṛtīyakampanādūrdhvaṃ caturthaṃ pañcamaṃ tataḥ 669
turyasvaraṃ dvitīyaṃ ca kṛtvā nyāso grahe yadā
tadā dvitīyaṃ svasthānaṃ śārṅgadevena kīrtitam 670
caturthasvaramāhatya sthāyinaṃ tatparaṃ tataḥ
samuccārya svaraṃ turyaṃ pañcamaṃ tu vilambya ca 671
ṣaṣṭhaṃ ca pañcamaṃ kṛtvā dvitīyaṃ ca grahe yadā
nyāsastadā tṛtīyaṃ syātsvasthānamiti tadvidaḥ 672
grahadvitīyaturyāṃśca pañcamaṃ saptamaṃ tataḥ
kṛtvā'ṣṭamaṃ bilambyātha dvitīyaṃ tatparaṃ tataḥ 673
dvitīyaṃ saptamaṃ kṛtvā pañcamaṃ ca caturthakam
dvitīyaṃ ca grahe nyāsaḥ svasthānaṃ turyamiṣyate 674
dṛśyate madhyamādestu graho vaṃśeṣu mudritaḥ 675
iti madhyamādiḥ
ṣaḍje grahe mamāhatya ṣaḍjaṃ kṛtvā hatau rimau
pamāndolya prakamyāpi pamagāṃ dviḥ prayujya ca 676
āndolya maṃ prakampyāpi laghuṣaḍjaṃ vidhāya ca
kampayitvā rimau dvitrānvārānāvartya ṣaḍjagau 677
sthāyinyāsādhbavedādyaṃ svasthānaṃ mālavaśriyaḥ
ādyasvasthānavidhinā vidhāya svarapañcakam 678
niṃ vilambyāvarohī cetpūrvaḥ svasthānagaḥ kṛtaḥ
tadā dvitīyaṃ svasthānaṃ dhāntamāndolya saptamam
āhatya ca dvitīyasya svasthānasyāvarohaṇam 680
kṛtaṃ yatra tṛtīyaṃ tatsvasthānamuditaṃ budhaiḥ
āhatya maṃ dviguṇasaṃ kṛtvā riṃ tatparaṃ tataḥ 681
taṃ saṃ procya nimāhatya vilambya punarapyamum
kṛtvā tṛtīyaṃ svasthānāvarohī kriyate yadā
tadā caturthaṃ svasthānamākhyātaṃ mālavaśriyaḥ 682
ṣaḍjo'nyo vā svaro vaṃśe graho'syā mudritaḥ svaraḥ 683
prakallpya tasya ṣaḍjatvaṃ pareṣāmṛṣabhāditam
lakṣmedaṃ yojayetsarvamityuktaṃ sūriśārṅgiṇā 684
iti mālavaśrīḥ
sthāyinaṃ madhyamaṃ kṛtvā kampayitvā paraṃ svayam
turyaṃ vilambya kṛtvā ca tṛtīyaṃ taṃ drutāhatam 685
vidhāya taṃ punaḥ procya grahanyāsena jāyate
svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ toḍyāḥ proktaṃ munivarairiti 686
prāksvasthānavadāruhya pañcamādavaruhyate
sthāyisvarāntaṃ yatrādaḥ svasthānaṃ syāddvitīyakam 687
prāgvā ṣaṣṭhamārohāvarohī ca grahāvadhiḥ
asyāstṛtīye svasthāne caturthe tvaṣṭamāvadhau 688
ārohiṇyavarohī syātpūrvavattadvidāṃ mataḥ
mudritastu grahaḥ prokto lakṣye syāllakṣmavedibhiḥ 689
iti toḍī
madhyamaṃ grahamāsthāya prāñcaṃ procya tato graham 690
sadvitīyaṃ kampayitvā tṛtīyaṃ sacaturthakam
laghūkṛtya tṛtīyaṃ coccāryā''hatya ca taṃ tataḥ 691
prācīnaṃ svaramuccārya sthāyini nyasyate yadā
bhavedbaṅgālarāgasya svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ tadā 692
ārohī pañcamāntaḥ sthātsvasthāne'syādvitīyake
saptamāntastṛtīye syātturye syādaṣṭamāvadhi 693
prākprāgvadavarohī syātsvasthāne tūttarottaram
lakṣye tu sarvavaṃśastho dvitīyo'sya graho mataḥ 694
iti baṅgālaḥ
dhaivataṃ sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā prāñcaṃ taṃ ca tṛtīyakam
punaḥ prāñcaṃ vidhāyātha kampayitvā tṛtīyakam 695
vilambya cā''hataṃ kṛtvā dvitrivāraṃ dvitīyakam
grahe nyāsādbhairavasya svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet 696
dvitīye pañcamāntaḥ syādāroho'tha tṛtīyake
ṣaṣṭhontaḥ saptamānto vā caturthe tvaṣṭamāvadhiḥ 697
pare pare'varohī syātsvasthāne pūrvapūrvavat
dvitīyo'tra svaraḥ sthāyī sarvavaṃśeṣu dṛśyate 698
iti bhairavaḥ
sthāyinaṃ dviguṇaṃ ṣaḍjaṃ kṛtvā'rdhaṃ vādayettataḥ
pūrvaṃ grahaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtīyamatha vādayet 699
atha dvitīyamāgatya nyasyate sthāyini svare
yadā varāṭyāḥ svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ jāyate tadā 700
iha bhairavavatkāryaṃ svasthānatritayaṃ param
svasthānaprakriyaiṣaiva jñeyā rāgāntareṣvapi
dvitīyo'syāḥ svaro lakṣye grahatvenopalakṣyate 701
iti varāṭī
ṛṣabhe sthāyini prāñcaṃ kampayitvā'rdhamasya ca 702
kṛtvā grahaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ tadadhaḥ svaram
grahametya tataḥ prāñcaṃ prakampyoktārdhamasya ca 703
grahe nyāsena garjaryāḥ svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet
tṛtīyo dṛśyate prāyo graho'syāṃ lakṣyagocare 704
iti gurjarī
ṣaḍje grahe dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ sakṛdāhṛtam
vegātkṛtvā'tha turyaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ tadadhastanam 705
uktvā tṛtīyaturyau ca tṛtīyaṃ tadadhastanam
grahe nyāso vasantasya svasthāne prathame bhavet
tṛtīyastvasya vaṃśeṣu sthāyitvenopalabhyate 706
iti vasantaḥ
sthāyini dviguṇe ṣaḍje tadardhaṃ laghu vādayet
tatastatsakṛdāhatya grahaṃ tasmāttṛtīyakam 707
turyaṃ coktvā tṛtīyaṃ ca sakṛdāhatya tatparam
drutaṃ kṛtvā pañcamaṃ tu vilambyāsmādadhastanau 708
laghūkṛtya grahe nyāsaḥ svasthāne prathame bhavet
dhannāsyā dṛśyate vaṃśe syāddvitīyasvaro grahaḥ 709
iti dhannāsī
tameva sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā dvitīyaṃ vādayeddrutam
laghū grahadvitīyau ca kṛtvā tābhyāṃ paraṃ svaram 710
vilambya sphuritaṃ tu dvirvidhyātpañcamaṃ tataḥ
adharāṃstrīnkramāduktvā sthāyini nyasyate yadā
deśyāstadā''dyaṃ svasthānaṃ dvitīyo'syā graho jane 711
iti deśī |
gāndhāre sthāyini procya dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtīyakam
turyaṃ vilambya tatprāñcaṃ kṛtvā spṛṣṭvā dvitīyakam 712
dīrghīkṛtya tṛtīyaṃ coktvā dvitīyaṃ grahasvare
nyāso yadā syātsvasthānaṃ deśākhyāyāstadā''dimam 713
mudrito'syā graho vaṃśe lakṣyate lakṣyavedibhiḥ 714
iti deśākhyā
grahaṃ dviguṇasaṃ kṛtvā pūrvaṃ spṛṣṭvā tṛtīyakam
kṛtvā spṛṣṭvā grahaṃ procya tatparaṃ sphuritau vadet 715
dvistrirvā grahatatpūrvo nyasyate kampite grahe
yadā ḍombakriyaḥ proktaṃ svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ tadā
sā bhūpālī śrutā loke dvitīyaṃ grahamāśritā 716
iti ḍombakrīḥ
dhaivate sthāyitāṃ nīte dviruccārya paraṃ laghum
taṃ cātha prāñcamasyārdhaṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ vidhāya tam 717
vilambya kampayitvā'rdhaṃ tasyā''hatya ca tanmuhuḥ
pūvaṃ prakampya tasyārdhaṃ dīrghīkṛtya grahaṃ vrajet 718
athordhapreritau pūrvagrahau kṛtvā dvitīyakam
procya spṛṣṭvā grahaṃ tasmātparaṃ procya prakampya tu 719
tṛtīyaṃ grahapāścātyagrahau tūccārayenmuhuḥ
tatastṛtīyamākampya vilambya tadadhastanam 720
īṣadviramya spṛṣṭvā ca dvitīyaṃ nyasyate grahe
yadā tadā''dyaṃ svasthānaṃ velāvalyāḥ prakīrtitam
tṛtīyagrahatā tvasyā dṛśyate vaṃśagocare 721
iti velāvalī
pañcamaṃ grahamāsthāyā''rohiṇā pañcamaṃ tataḥ
gatvā vilambya taṃ tasmādadharaṃ drutatāṃ nayet 722
tato'dhaḥ sthaṃ drāghayitvā spṛṣṭvā ca drāghitātparam
tatparaṃ dīrghatāṃ nītvā punaḥ procyāvarohiṇā 723
tasmādetya grahanyāse svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet
rāge prathamamañjaryāṃ dvitīyo'syā graho jane 724
iti prathamamañjarī
sthāyino'rdhātsamāruhya tṛtīyādicatuḥsvarīm
kampayitvā vilambyāpi ṣaṣṭhaturyaṃ vidhāya ca 725
etya pañcamametasmādgrahāntamavaruhya ca
grahārdhaṃ grahapūrvaṃ ca gatvā spṛṣṭvā grahaṃ tataḥ 726
dvitīyaṃ kampitaṃ kṛtvā tṛtīyaṃ sphuritaṃ tataḥ
sthāyini syasyate yatra svasthānaṃ syāttadā''dimam
āḍīkāmodikāyāḥ syāddvitīyagrahatā jane 727
ityāḍīkāmodī
sthāyini dviguṇe ṣaḍje dvirāhatya tṛtīyakam
triścaturvā tataḥ spṛṣṭvā grahaṃ pūrvamudīrya ca 728
kampite sthāyini nyāsātsvasthānaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet
rāge śuddhavarāṭyāṃ sā dvitīyasthāyikā jane 729
iti śuddhavarāṭī
tameva sthāyinaṃ kṛtvā taṃ tadardhaṃ dvirāhatam
dvistrirvoktvā'tha dvitīyaṃ kampayitvā vilambya ca 730
āhatya dviścaturvā'smātparaṃ kṛtvā laghuṃ graham
uktvā grahārdhapūrve tu dīrghīkṛtya grahe yadā 731
nyasyate śuddhanaṭṭāyāḥ svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ tadā
vaṃśeṣvasyāmapi prāyo dvitīyo dṛśyate grahaḥ 732
iti śuddhanaṭṭā
grahaṃ dviguṇasaṃ procya tadardhaṃ ca dvitīyakam
vilambite tṛtīye'tha dvitīyaṃ drutatāṃ nayet 733
grahārdhe ca sthirībhūya kampayitvā grahaṃ tataḥ
parau svarau drutīkṛtya laghūkṛtya paraṃ grahe 734
nyāse kṛte rāmakṛteḥ svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet
dvitīyasvaramevāsyā vaṃśe vīkṣāmahe graham 735
iti rāmakrīḥ
tasminneva grahe kṛtvā tṛtīyaṃ ca dvitīyakam
grahārdhaṃ drutamuccārya spṛṣṭvā pūrvaṃ grahasya ca 736
ardhaṃ procya viramyātha pūrvaṃ spṛṣṭvā tṛtīyakam
laghūkṛtya laghoḥ pūrvau spṛṣṭvā'tha sthāyinaḥ parau 737
laghūkṛtya drutaṃ kṛtvā dvitīyaṃ nyasyate graha
yadā tadā gauḍakṛterbhavetsvasthānamādimam 738
dvitīyo'syāmapi prāyo graho lakṣyeṣu dṛśyate 739
iti gauḍakrīḥ
dhaivate sthāyini prāñcaṃ grahaṃ grahatṛtīyakam
turyaṃ kṛtvā tamāhatya dvitīyaṃ kampayettataḥ 740
avaruhya tṛtīyādikrameṇa caturaḥ svarān
grahanyāsāddevakṛterādyaṃ svasthānamādiśet
tṛtīyagrahatāṃ tvasyāḥ paśyāmo vaṃśagocare 741
iti devakrīḥ
dhaivataṃ grahamāsthāya tṛtīyādavaruhya ca
svaratrayaṃ viramyātha grahaṃ paramatha graham 742
pūrvaṃ kṛtvā tamāhatya grahanyāsena jāyate
svasthānamādyaṃ bhairavyāstṛtīyo'syā graho jane 743
iti bhairavī
sthāyini dviguṇe ṣaḍje'dhastṛtīyaṃ vidhāya ca
adhastanaṃ prakampyātha vilambya sthāyinaṃ tataḥ 744
prācīnaṃ laghutāṃ nītvā dvistrirvā''hatya taṃ graham
laghūkṛtyādharasyārdhamāspṛśya grahapañcamam 745
vilambya taṃ drutīkṛtya tṛtīyasparśamācaret
athoktvā pañcamaṃ taṃ ca dīrghīkṛtyādharaṃ manāk 746
kampayitvā grahanyāsādbhavetsvasthānamādimam
chāyānaṭṭākhyarāgasya graho vaṃśe tṛtīyakaḥ 747
etāṃ sālaganaṭṭāṃ tu bhāṣante lakṣyavedinaḥ 748
anye tu pañcamaṃ kṛtvā sthāyinaṃ riṃ vidhāya ca
maṃ prakampya sthirībhūya pañcame dviḥ pamadhyamau 749
kṛtvā gamṛṣabhārdhaṃ ca saṃspṛśyārdhaṃ punarvrajet
tato dīrghau rimau kṛtvā grahe cennyasyate tadā
ādyaṃ sālaganaṭṭāyāḥ svasthānaṃ tadvido viduḥ 750
maṃ vidhāya grahaṃ taṃ ca drutīkṛtya paraṃ tataḥ
uktvā turyaṃ vilambyātha paramāspṛśya pañcamam 751
vilambyāto'varoheṇa grahameti yadā tadā
svasthānamādimaṃ rāmakṛtervaṃśe dvitīyakaḥ
grahaścindhamarāmakrīḥ sā siddhā lokavartmani 752
iti cindhamarāmakrīḥ
rimeva grahamāśritya dvistrirvā''hatya maṃ tataḥ 753
taṃvilambitatāṃ nītvā dīrghīkṛtya nu madhyamam
rigau ridalametāni drutīkṛtya vilambya nim 754
punaruccārya taṃ kṛtvā dhaivataṃ sakṛdāhatam
vādayitvā niṣādaṃ ca grahanyāsena jāyate 755
nāṭyarāmakṛterādyaṃ svasthānamiti tadvidaḥ
athavā madhyamaṃ kṛtvā grahamāhatya pañcamam 656
dvistrirvā'pyavaroheṇa dhātkramādṛṣabhaṃ drutam
etyārdhaṃ tasya ṣaḍjaṃ ca rerardhaṃ vādayetpunaḥ 757
athoccārya rimau paṃ tu vilambya nyasyate grahe
yatra svasthānamādyaṃ tannāṭyarāmakṛtermatam 758
iti nāṭyarāmakrīḥ
grahaṃ kṛtvā dhamāhatya sakṛdgrahaparaṃ svaram
kṛtvā grahātparau dvistu grahamāhatya pūrvakam 759
dvitīyaṃ ca kramāduktvā grahe cennyasyate tadā
svasthānamādyaṃ mahlāre graho vaṃśe dvitīyakaḥ 760
iti mahlāraḥ
grahaṃ dviguṇasaṃ kṛtvā drutīkṛtya tato'dharam 761
tamāndolya dvitīyaṃ tu vidhāyātha tṛtīyakam
sphuritaṃ grahatatpūrvau vādayitvā grahe yadā 762
nyāsaḥ kaṇāṭagauḍasya svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ tadā
vaṃśe grahastṛtīyo'sya gṛhyate lakṣyavedibhiḥ 763
iti karṇāṭagauḍaḥ
grahaṃ tameva saṃ kṛtvā procya turyatṛtīyakau
sthāyinaṃ taddalaṃ coktvā pūrvasthāyidalaṃ tataḥ 764
sthāyinyāsāddeśavālagauḍasvasthānamādimam
jāyate'syāpi vaṃśe tu dvitīyo dṛśyate grahaḥ 765
iti deśavālagauḍaḥ
niṣāde sthāyini procya pūrvaṃ grahamathottaram
turyaṃ dvitīyatastu trīnavaruhya grahe yadā 766
nyāsasturuṣkagauḍasya tadā svasthānamādimam
loke mālavagauḍo'sau tṛtīyo'sya graho gataḥ 767
iti turuṣkagauḍaḥ
niṣādādgrāhatāṃ nītādārohedācaturthakam
taṃ vilambya tato gacchedgrahe cedavarohiṇā 768
tadā drāviḍagauḍasya svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ bhavet
loke sālagagauḍo'yaṃ dvitīyo'sya graho mataḥ 769
iti drāviḍagauḍaḥ
pañcamaṃ grahamāsthāya tṛtīyaṃ ca dvitīyakam
muktvā tṛtīyaṃ spṛṣṭvā ca prāntamuktvā tṛtīyakam 770
spṛṣṭvā vilambya dvitīye nyasyate kampite grahe
yadā bhavaṅti kaiśikyāḥ svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ tadā 771
tṛtīyo'syāḥ svaro vaṃśe graho lakṣyeṣu dṛśyate 772
iti kaiśikī
sthāyinaṃ dviguṇaṃ ṣaḍjaṃ drutīkṛtya dvitīyakam
uktvā dvitīyatṛtīyau drutīkṛtyādharādharam 773
udīrya taṃ drutīkṛtya dvitīyaṃ vādayettataḥ
tṛtīyaṃ dvistrirāhatya syāyini nyasyate yadā 774
lalitāyāstadā proktaṃ svasthānaṃ prathamaṃ budhaiḥ
asyāmapi svaro lakṣye tṛtīyo dṛśyate grahaḥ 775
iti lalitā
grahaṃ dviguṇasaṃ kṛtvā'dhastanaṃ sthāyinaṃ tathā
kṛtvā dvitīyaturyau ca dvitīyasthāyinau spṛśet 776
vilambite dvitīye'tha sthāyini nyasyate yadā
śrīrāgasya budhaiḥ proktaṃ tadā svasthānamādimam
svaro vaṃśe dvitīyo'sya grahatvenopalakṣitaḥ 777
iti śrīrāgaḥ
yo grahaḥ driyate vaṃśe tamapekṣyātra yojayet
lakṣaṇasthaṃ dvitīyādi yadā kiṃtu grahādayaḥ 778
yāvanto yatra rāge syuḥ svarā lakṣmaṇi tāvatām
saṃbhavaḥ sthāyinā yena sthāyinaṃ tatra saṃśrayet 779
anugrahāya mugdhānāmityete katicinmayā
rāgāḥ proktāḥ pathā'nena saṃpaśyadhvaṃ budhāḥ parān 780
iti vaṃśaprakaraṇam
pāvo veṇusamutpannaḥ syānnavāṅgulavaṃśavat
kṛtāveṣṭo vaṅgapatrairlokarītyaiṣa vādyate 781
iti pāvalakṣaṇam
pāvikā vaiṇavī kāryā dvādaśāṅguladairghyabhāk
sthaulye'ṅguṣṭhamitā garbhe svadairghye suṣiraṃ śritā 782
kaniṣṭhāgramitasthaulyamasyā lokānusārataḥ
phūtkārarandhramekaṃ syātpañca syuḥ svarasiddhaye
vādanaṃ vividhaṃ nāgayakṣāveśavidhāyakam 783
iti pāvikālakṣaṇam
hastadvayādhikā māne mukharandhrasamanvitā
catuḥsvaracchidrayutā muralī cārunādinī 784
iti muralī
śṛṅgajā dāravī vā syātkāhalākṛtidhāriṇī
aṣṭāviṃśatyaṅgulā ca dairghye madhukarī śubhā 785
mukharandhraṃ ca tasyāḥ syāttuvarībījasaṃnibham
mukharandhrādaṅgulāni tyaktvā catvāri vaṃśavat 786
vidhāya sapta randhrāṇi kurvīta vivarāntaram
teṣāṃ ca mukharandhrasya madhye'dhobhāgasaṃsthitam 787
madhuradhvānasiddhyai tanmukharandhre tu tāmrajā
nidhātavyā yavasthūlā nalikā caturaṅgulā 788
tadūrdhvaṃ cakrikā sthāpyā dantajā śuktijā'thavā
randhramadhye kāśamayīṃ yadvā devanalodbhavām 789
śuktikāṃ kiṃcidunnidramālatīkalikākṛtim
mṛdulāṃ kṣīrapākeṇa kṣiptvā madhukarīṃ tataḥ 790
vaṃśavadvādayedrandhraṃ pidadhītāpyadhastanam
vāmāṅguṣṭhāgrabhāgeṇetyuktaṃ niḥśaṅkasūriṇā 791
iti madhukarīlakṣaṇam
tāmrajā rājatī yadvā kāñcanī suṣirāntarā
dhattūrakusumākāravadanena virājitā 792
hastatrayamitā dairghye kāhalā vādyate janaiḥ
hāhūvarṇavatī vīrabirudoccārakāriṇī 793
iti kāhalālakṣaṇam
hastadvayakṛtā yā sā saiva tuṇḍakinī matā
turuturyapi sā loke tittirī ca nigadyate
yamalaṃ tuṇḍakinyośca vādyaṃ vādyavido viduḥ 794
iti tuṇḍakinīlakṣaṇam
tuṇḍakinyeva cukkā syāddairghye hastacatuṣṭayā 975
iti cukkālakṣaṇam
kareṇuvadanākāravadanaṃ doṣavarjitam
vṛṅgaṃ yanmāhiṣaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ snigdhaṃ sughaṭitaṃ kṛtam 796
śṛṅgasyānaḍuhaścātha dhattūrakusumākṛti
khaṇḍamaṣṭāṅgulaṃ nyasya mūle'sya dhvanivṛddhaye 797
agraṃ dvitrāṅgulaṃ tasya cchitvā phūtkārarandhrakam
vidhāya tuthukāreṇa vādayedvividhadhvanim
etadvai dhvānagambhīraṃ vādyaṃ gopālakeliṣu 798
iti śṛṅgalakṣaṇam
nirdoṣasyotkhātanābheḥ śaṅkhasyaikādaśāṅgulam
śikharaṃ nirmitaṃ dhātumicchakābhyāṃ manoharam 799
mūlataścāgraparyantaṃ hrasvamānākṛpi kramāt
randhramardhāṅgulamitaṃ śikharasya mukhe bhavet 800
antastu māṣamātraṃ syācchaṅkamevaṃvidhaṃ sudhīḥ
dhṛtvā karkaṭahastena bhūriśvāsabhṛtodaram
huṃbhuṃtho digidityetadvarṇodbodhena vādayet 801
iti śaṅkalakṣaṇam
mārgadeśīgatatvena paṭaho dvividho bhavet
mārgasthapaṭahasyātra lakṣaṇaṃ tāvaducyate 802
sārdhahastadvayaṃ tasya dairghyaṃ syātparidhiḥ punaḥ
ṣaṣṭyaṅgulo madhyadeśaḥ pṛthulo dakṣiṇaṃ mukham 803
kartavyaṃ saṃmitaṃ sārdhairekādaśabhiraṅgulaiḥ
vāmaṃ tu daśabhiḥ sārdhaiḥ saṃmitaṃ dakṣiṇe mukhe 804
āyasaṃ valayaṃ kāryaṃ vāme vallīsamudbhavam 805
ṣāṇmāsiko mṛto vatso yastasyā''dāya pārikām
tayā'vaguṇṭhya valayaṃ vallījaṃ vāmavaktragam 806
saptarandhrānvitaṃ kṛtvā sudṛḍhaiḥ sūkṣmadorakaiḥ
nikṣiptaisteṣu randhreṣu bandhavyāḥ kalaśāḥ ślatham 807
te hemādimayāḥ sapta dairghyeṇa caturaṅgulāḥ
tyaktvā'ṅgulāni catvāri vāme syāllohapatrikām 808
aṅgulatrayavistārāṃ paṭahe veṣṭayeddṛḍham
bāhyakāyasya yaccarma paśostena ghanena ca 809
kambalākhyena badhnīyātpihitaṃ vadanadvayam
dakṣiṇāsyasya kavalaṃ paritaḥ kṛtarandhrakam 810
randhrakṣiptairguṇairgāḍhamākṛṣyetaravaktragaiḥ
valaye tānguṇānsamyagbadhnīyāddārḍhyasiddhaye 811
vāmāsyakavalāntasthasaptarandhraniveśitaiḥ
guṇairāveṣṭya kalaśānāyasairvalayairdṛḍhaiḥ 812
antaḥ kṣiptvā samākṛṣṭairvalaye gāḍhatāṃ nayet
kalaśebhyo bahirvāmavalayānte ca badhyate 813
veṣṭanāya kaṭeḥ kacchetyukto'yaṃ skandadaivataḥ
śrīyajñapuravaryeṇa paṭaho mārgasaṃbhavaḥ 814
deśīstho'pyevameva syāddairghye'sau sārdhahastakaḥ
saptāṅgulaṃ dakṣiṇāsyamanyatsārdhaṣaḍaṅgulam 815
āntaraṃ jāṭharaṃ carma paśorgaditamuddalī
tayā tu svecchayā vaktraṃ vāmamasya nibadhyate 816
viśeṣo'yaṃ bhavedasminnubhau khādiradārujau
tridhokto'yaṃ dvayoranyairuttamo madhyamo'dhamaḥ 817
uttamaḥ proktamānaḥ syāddvādaśāṃśonitaḥ punaḥ
madhyamaḥ ṣaṣṭhabhāgena vihīnastvadhamo bhavet 818
athā''dyavarṇāḥ
ṅavarjitaḥ kavargaśca ṭatavargau rahāvapi
iti ṣoḍaśa varṇāḥ syurubhayoḥ pāṭasaṃjñakāḥ 819
saṃyogaviprayogābhyāṃ kramavyutkramayogataḥ
sasvaratvāsvaratvābhyāṃ pāṭāste syuranekadhā 820
uddalyāmeva jheṃkāro deṃkāraḥ kavale'dhikaḥ
deśīpaṭahamevā''hurimamaḍḍāvajaṃ janāḥ 821
aṣṭādaśāṅgulaḥ kiṃcinnatāgro madhyataḥpṛthuḥ
koṇaḥ kāryo mūladeśe madanāmbaraveṣṭitaḥ 822
padmāsanopaviṣṭena goṣṭhyāmūrudvayopari
saṃsthāpya mārgapaṭaho vādyo varṇodbhave paṭuḥ 823
nāṭake tvardhavaktro'yaṃ vādyate ghaṭavādyavat
koṇena pāṇinā vā'sya vādanaṃ sūrayo viduḥ 824
iti paṭahalakṣaṇam
ye pāṭāḥ paṭahe proktāsteṣāṃ vividhagumphanāt
hastodbhavāḥ pāṭabhedā hastapāṭāḥ prakīrtitāḥ 825
te cānantā na śakyante pratyekaṃ vedituṃ janaiḥ
pradarśanārthameteṣu katicitpratipādaye 826
sadyojātānnāgabandhaḥ svastiko vāmadevataḥ
alagno'bhūdaghorāttu śuddhistatpuruṣābhidhāt 827
āsīdīśānavaktrastu samaskhalitasaṃjñakaḥ
jātāḥ pañceti pañcabhyo mukhebhyaḥ pārvatīpateḥ 828
ataste sarvapāṭānāṃ pāṭā mukhyatamā matāḥ
kramāttaddevatā brahmaviṣṇuśarvaravīndavaḥ 829
nāgabandhaśca pavanastathaikaikasarābhidhau
tato duḥsarasaṃcāravikṣepā nāgabandhajāḥ 830
saptetyatha svastikajāḥ svastiko balikohalaḥ
phullapūrvaśca vikṣepaḥ kuṇḍalīpūrvakaśca saḥ 831
saṃcāravilikhī khaṇḍanāgabandhaśca pūrakaḥ
bhedāḥ saptetyalagnasya tvete sapta prakīrtitāḥ 832
alagnotsāraviśrāmā viṣamādyā khalī sarī
sphurī sphuraṇakaśceti śuddhe syācchuddhirādimaḥ 833
svarasphuraṇakocchallavalatāvaghaṭāstataḥ
takāro māṇikyavallī bhedāḥ sapteti saṃmatāḥ 834
samaskhalitakasyā''dyaḥ samaskhalitasaṃjñakaḥ
vikaṭaḥ sadṛśaḥ pāṭakhalī cāḍukhalī tataḥ 835
anucchallastathā khutto bhedāḥ sapteti santyamī
pañcatriṃśaddhastapāṭāstalapāṭāśca kīrtitāḥ 836
koṇāhataśca saṃbhrānto viṣamārdhasamāviti
catvāro hastapāṭāḥ syurnandikeśvarabhāṣitāḥ 837
utphullaḥ khalakastadvatpāṇyantaranikuṇṭakaḥ
daṇḍahastaḥ piṇḍahasto yugahasto'rdhahastakau 838
sthūlahasto'rdhārdhapāṇiḥ pārśvapāṇyardhapāṇyapi
kartarīsamakartaryau tato viṣamakartarī 839
samapāṇiśca viṣamapāṇiḥ syātpāṇihastakaḥ
nāgabandho'pyavaghaṭaḥ svastikaśca samagrahaḥ
ityekaviṃśatirhastapāṭāḥ syuḥ paṭahādiṣu 840
ullolaḥ pāṇyantaraśca nirghoṣaḥ khaṇḍakartarī
daṇḍahastaḥ samanakho binduryamalahastakaḥ 841
recito bhramaro vidyudvilāso'pyardhakartarī
alagnākhyastato rephaḥ samapāṇirataḥ param 842
parivṛttaḥ ṣoḍaśeti vādyeṣu paṭahādiṣu
etānprāyo huḍukkāyāṃ kurvate vādyavedinaḥ 843
aṣṭau viṣamapāṭāḥ syurapāṭākhyānbruve'dhunā 844
talaprahāraḥ praharo valito guruguñjitaḥ
ardhasañcastrisañcaśca viṣamo'bhyasta ityamī 845
syātāmalagapāṭau dvau sañcavicchuritābhidhau
bhramaraḥ kuñcitaśceti citrapāṭāvubhau matau 846
aṣṭāśītirime hastapāṭā niḥśaṅkakīrtitāḥ 847
śivavaktrotthapāṭānāṃ svarūpaṃ pratipādaye
teṣāmutpādakānpāṇīm̐llokato lakṣayetsudhīḥ 848
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhasaṃyogāccheṣāṅgulivivardhanāt
koṇāhato hastapāṭaḥ śārṅgadevodito yathā 849
khuṃkhuṃdhari khuṃkhuṃdhari karagiḍa karagiḍa |
iti koṇāhataḥ 1
sarvāṅgulyagraghātaśca sarvāṅgulivivartanam
yasya niṣpādakaṃ so'tra saṃbhrāntaḥ kathito yathā 850
daragiḍadaragiḍa girigiḍada dāṇakiṭa maṭaṭaku
iti saṃbhrāntaḥ 2
calanaṃ maṇibandhasya patākasyātha kampanam

dahreṃ dahreṃ khuṃkhuṃ dahreṃ khuṃkhuṃ dahreṃ tataki tataki

ityardhasamaḥ 4

katheṃ katheṃ ityutphullaḥ 1
yadā prasāritāṅguṣṭhaḥ śukatuṇḍasya hanyate
viralāṅgulibhirvādyaṃ krameṇa khalakastadā 854
dāṃgiḍa giḍadagidā iti khalakaḥ 2
yastu dakṣiṇahastasya tarjanyaṅguṣṭhaghātataḥ
kramavyutkramaghātāttu vāmahastena rephavat

dagiḍadāṃ kharikkadāṃ kharikka kharikkadāṃdāṃ kharikharidāṃ giḍadāṃ
iti pāṇyantaranikuṭṭakaḥ 3

ardhapāṇirbhavedekakarāgrakṛtatāḍanāt
dagiḍa dagiḍa daragiḍa daragiḍa ityardhapāṇiḥ 11

ṭiri ṭiri ṭiri kiṭathaoṃ digidāṃ tiri ṭiri kiṭajheṃjhe taki-

aṅguṣṭhāṅgulisaṃghātau hastayoryutapadyadā

iti nāgabandhaḥ 18
talena hatvā praharetsāṅguṣṭhāṅgulibhiḥ kramāt

tale kecidiha prāhuraṅguṣṭhāṅgulivarjite 869
takiṭa kiṭataka iti samagrahaḥ 21
ityekaviṃśatirhastapāṭāḥ
lolo vyavahitāṅguṣṭho dakṣiṇo yasya sādhakaḥ
vāmaścecchvasito hastamullolaṃ taṃ pracakṣate 870

vyaṅguṣṭho dakṣiṇo hastaḥ puṭe cenmuhurullaset

anye pāṇyantaraṃ prāhurvyaṅguṣṭhādyuktalakṣaṇam

nakheṃ nakheṃ nakheṃ kheṃkheṃkheṃ nakheṃ nakheṃ nakheṃ kheṃkheṃkheṃkheṃ dakhuṃ khuṃda khuṃda iti pāṇyantaraḥ 1

ghātāddakṣiṇahastena viralāṅgulinā tataḥ

dāṃ khukhudāṃ2

ṭaithoṭeṃgeṃ tthaoṃdhithaoṃhadhihaṭeṃ | ityupaśamo dviḥ | tarkkarga daḍa-
rada kuthikurgadaḍadhikka dakkarga daḍagdakthaoṃgattakadhika kadhikata dhika-
kadhi ṭeṃgeṃnathaoṃga2
thaoṃgakkathaoṃ iti dvitīyakhaṇḍam |
ṭaiṃ thaoṃ haṭageṃ thaoṃṭeṃ | ityupaśamo dviḥ | thaoṃgaṭaṃ daragaḍa dhiragaḍa dhitthāḥ thaoṃkaṭa takathaoṃ kaṭadhikkak | thaoṃdhika taddhitak | iti tṛtīyakhaṇḍam |
katthaoṃṭakka thaoṃhakaṭage dhikaṭai thau haṭageṃ thaoṃ haṭeṃ ityupaśamo dviḥ | tathaoṃ giḍdak diganadigana diganaka thaoṃgataddhitak | thaoṃhaṭeṃ | iti cchaṇḍaṇaḥ |
ityekatālyāmudāharaṇam | iti rigoṇī |
nātidīrghaṃ dvirādyaṃ syātkhaṇḍaṃ śuddhādinirmitam
yadvā varṇasareṇātha tādṛkkhaṇḍaṃ sakṛdbhavet 970
ityudgrāhadhruvau kṛtvā svodgrāhāntyadale'thavā
svodgrāhe yatra muktistatkavitaṃ kavayo viduḥ 971
asya nāmāntaraṃ prāhuranye'vaccheda ityapi
bhavediha drutaṃ mānaṃ varṇāḥ prāyaḥ syuruddhatāḥ
bāhulyānnartanaṃ dīptaṃ niḥśaṅkeneti kīrtitam 972
yathā--gaḍdakdaginadaṃdaṃ ginathaoṃga dhikkaṭa nakādhitaka deṃhakadara gaḍadariktha riktha ridaragaḍa tharika thaoṃ gaṃḍake | dhikthaoṃṭeṃ2
|
ityudgrāho dviḥ | jheṃkaka nakhakhina2
taheṃ hake nakhakhinathaoṃ | iti dhruvākhyaṃ khaṇḍam
tato deṃhakaḍaragaḍetyādinodgrāhāntyakhaṇḍenodgrāheṇa vā sakalena gaḍgatityādinā tyāgaḥ | niḥsārutāle cedamudāharaṇam |
iti kavitam
udgrāho'lpo dhruvo nātidīrghaḥ śuddhādinirmitaḥ
syādvarṇasarabaddho vā chaṇḍaṇo'nte yadā tadā
padaṃ vadanti vādyajñāḥ prāyastaddīptanartane 973
yathā--taddeheṃ taddeṃ tataktaddai | ityudgrāhaḥ | takaṭa dhikaṭa dhidhikiṭa dhikiṭargāḍdagadhikataka dhikathaoṃgaṭeṃ | iti dhruvaḥ |
gata kaṭadhidhi kaṭadhikkiḍadagadhidhikaṭa dhikkiraḍāḍagadhigakki-
gḍāhagathaoṃṭaiṃ haṃdhikkathaoṃgaṭeṃ | iti cchaṇḍaṇaḥ | idamudāharaṇaṃ varṇatāle |
vādayitvā yatiṃ madhye prabandhasya vimucyate
pāṭena yatra tatproktamaparaiḥ sūribhiḥ padam 974
iti padam
ekatālyāṃ drute māne nartanārambhagocare
samaṃ ca karaṭāṭīpikāhalāvādane sati 975
abhyasto vādyakhaṇḍo'llpaḥ kūṭenaikena nirmitaḥ
taddhīmiticchaṇḍaṇāntaḥ smṛto melāpako budhaiḥ 976
vidyāvantaḥ svasamaye prāhurmelāpanīmimām
varṇānāṃ ṭirikītyeṣāmāvṛttiṣṭīpirucyate 977
yathā--thaoṃgaṭeṃ gaḍdagnaṃ thaoṃgaṭeṃ | iti yāvatprabandhapūrti punaḥ punarvādanīyam | taddhīmiti | iti cchaṇḍaṇaḥ | iti melāpakaḥ |
khaṇḍaṃ śuddhādibhiḥ pāṭairbaddhaṃ varṇasareṇa vā
alpaṃ komalanādaṃ ca sukumārākṣarānvitam
abhyastaṃ komale nṛtte bhavedupaśamābhidham 978
yathāk--ṭeṃthaoṃkkage thoṭeṭe thaoṃhaṭage thātheṃ thaoṃṭe | ityupaśamaḥ |
ādau vādyaprabandhānāṃ śuddhakūṭādinirmitaḥ
yaḥ khaṇḍo vādyate prāhurudgrāhaṃ taṃ mahattamāḥ 979
udgrāhopaśamādīnāṃ yadyapyanyāṅgatoditā
yāvatpūraṇamāvṛttestathā'pi syātprabandhatā 980
dvirāvṛttiranāvṛttirvā syādaṅgatayā sthitau 981
teṃ ṭeṃ hai taṭṭeṃ takkataṭe iti yāvatprabandhapūraṇamabhyāsādudgrāha-
prabandhaḥ | aṅgatvaṃ tu dviḥ sakṛdvā | prākprayogastvaṅgaprabandhayostulyaḥ | ityudgrāhaḥ |
uddhataṃ dhvanitaṃ kūṭabaddhaṃ khaṇḍaṃ muhurmuhuḥ
prayuktaṃ syātpraharaṇaṃ dhruvādyābhogagocare
nṛtte prāyaḥ prayoktavyamanyatrāpīcchayā bhavet 982
yathā--kathaoṃgakka thaoṃgatthaoṃgaṭathaoṃgakka thoga thogathok kathogak thaoṃkaṭa yogakthokaṭaṃ thogak | gaḍdakkādhika thaoṃgak | ṭageṃ daṃthaoṃha | diṅ-nikukudhittho hadhikkaṃ dhiṭaiṃ iti punaḥ punaḥ prayojyam | ekatālyāmidamudāharaṇam | iti praharaṇam |
udgrāhaḥ syāttataḥ khaṇḍaṃ śuddhakūṭādinirmitam 983
tadvarṇasarabaddhaṃ vā prayujya dviridaṃ dvayam
tataḥ prākkhaṇḍasahitaṃ tādṛkkūṭamayaṃ dalam 984
vādyate chaṇḍaṇo'nte ca yatra so'vatsako bhavet

yathā-- gaḍdagdaṃdaṃ gaḍdakthikkaṭa takadhikkaṭa takdhiktak | ityudgrāhaḥ |
khaḍi khaḍi khakhanakha khidak jheṃkhakhanakha khidaktadakka dhikkaka-
kagiṇanaga thaoṃgadihikiṃ thogadihikkitaka dhikathaoṃgaṭe gaḍak |
takadhika thaoṃgaṭeṃ | iti dvitīyaṃ khaṇḍam | etacca sahodgrāheṇa punaḥ punarvādayet | jhaka jhakhikhinnakhanakhakhita hreṃ khakhanakhajheṃ khana khariba tuḍi hidihi | kathaoṃgak | iti tṛtīyakhaṇḍaṃ pūrvakhaṇḍena

ityavatsakaḥ |

iti cchaṇḍaṇaḥ
