yoge dhyāne ca sa proktaḥ prāṇāyāme ca sūribhiḥ 482
iti vimuktaḥ
cittasyānyaparatvena yaḥ prayatnena vartate
sa vismito vismaye syādudbhūte vā'rthacintane 483
iti vismitaḥ
mahattvādduḥkhaniṣkrāntaḥ kathitaḥ skhalito'nilaḥ
syāddaśamyāṃ daśāyāṃ sa vyādhau praskhaliteṣu ca 484
iti skhalitaḥ
prasupte prasṛto dīrghaḥ saśabdo nirgato mukhāt 485
iti prasṛtaḥ
samo'tra svasthaparyāyo bhrānto'ntarbhramaṇānmarut
anye tvanvarthanāmānastatra sūtkṛtasītkṛte 486
śabdānukaraṇe vaktratyājyagrāhye'nile kramāt
samaḥ svābhāviko bhrāntaḥ prathame priyasaṃgame 487
vilīno mūrchite śailārohe tvāndolito mataḥ
kampitaḥ surate śastramokṣādau stambhito bhavet 488
ucchvāsaḥ kusumāghrāṇe niḥśvāso'nuśayādiṣu
sūtkṛtaṃ vedanādau syācchīte kleśe tu sītkṛtam 489
nakhakṣate mṛgākṣīṇāṃ nirdayādharacarvaṇe
viniyogāntarāṇyatra budhyatāṃ lokato budhāḥ 490
iti matāntareṇa daśadhā śvasanaḥ
vivartitaḥ kampitaśca visṛṣṭo vinigūhitaḥ
saṃdṛṣṭakaḥ samudgaścetyadharaḥ ṣaḍvidho mataḥ 491
anye'nyānūcurudvṛttavikāsyāyatarecitān
oṣṭhayoḥ saṃpuṣṭastiryaksaṃkocena vivartitaḥ
avajñāvedanāsūyāhāsyādiṣu vidhīyate 492
iti vivartitaḥ
vyathāyāṃ kampito'nvartho bhītau śīte jape ruṣi 493
iti kampitaḥ
visṛṣṭaḥ syādviniṣkrānto rañjane'laktavādinā
strīṇāṃ vilāse vivvoke śārṅgidevena śīrtitaḥ 494
mukhāntarnihitaḥ prāṇasādhye'rthe vinigūhitaḥ
roṣerṣyayośca nārīṇāṃ balāccumbati vallabhe 495
iti vinigūhitaḥ
saṃdaṣṭako'dharo dantairdaṣṭaḥ krodhe vidhīyate 496
iti saṃdaṣṭakaḥ
samudgastu bhavedoṣṭhasaṃpuṭo dadhadunnatim
syātphūtkāre'nukampāyāṃ cumbane cābhinandane 497
iti samudgaḥ
āsyotkṣiptatayodvṛttaḥ so'vajñāparihāsayoḥ
ityudvṛttaḥ
īṣadvṛśyordhvadaśano vikāsī sa smite smṛtaḥ 498
iti vikāsī
sa sārdhamuttaroṣṭhena tataḥ syādāyataḥ smite
ityāyataḥ
paryantaṃcalanādukto vilāse recito'dharaḥ 499
iti recitaḥ
dantakarmāṇi vakṣyāmo dantalakṣaṇasiddhaye
kuṭṭanaṃ khaṇḍanaṃ chinnaṃ cukkitaṃ grahaṇaṃ matam 500
daṣṭaṃ niṣkarṣaṇaṃ ceti dantakarmāṣṭakaṃ jaguḥ
saṃgharṣaṇaṃ kuṭṭanaṃ syācchītarugbhījarāsu tat 501
iti kuṭṭanam
khaṇḍanaṃ tu muihurdantaśleṣaviśleṣaṇaṃ matam
japādhyayanasaṃlāpabhakṣaṇeṣu bhavedidam 502
iti khaṇḍanam
chinnaṃ syādgāḍhasaṃśleṣo rodane bhītiśītayoḥ
vyādhau ca vīṭikācchedavyāyāmādiṣu kīrtitam 503
iti cchinnam
dantapaṅktyoḥ sthitirdūre cukkitaṃ jṛmbhaṇādiṣu
iti cukkitam
tṛṇasya cāṅgulairdante dhāraṇaṃ grahaṇam bhaye 504
iti grahaṇam
kiṃcicchleṣaḥ samaṃ tacca svabhāvābhinaye matam 505
iti samam
adhare daṃśanaṃ dantairdaṣṭaṃ krodhe'bhidhīyate
iti daṣṭam
niṣkarṣaṇaṃ tu niṣkāsaḥ kāryaṃ markaṭarodane 506
iti niṣkarṣaṇam
ṛjvī ṛkvānugā vakronnatā lolā'valehinī 507
jihveti ṣaḍvidhā tatra prasṛtāsye prasāritā
ṛjvī śrame pipāsāyāṃ śvāpadānāṃ prayujyate 508
iti ṛjvī
sṛkkānugā līḍhasṛkkā prakopasvādubhakṣayoḥ
iti sṛkkānugā
vyāttāsyasthonnatāgrā ca vakrā nṛharirūpaṇe 509
iti vakrā
vyāttāsyasthonnatā'nvarthā jṛmbhāsyāntasthavīkṣayoḥ 510
ityunnatā
vyātte vaktre calā lolā vetālābhinaye bhavet
iti lolā
dantoṣṭhaṃ lihatī jihvā lehinī saṃmatā muneḥ 511
iti lehinī
jihvoṣṭhadantakriyayā civukaṃ lakṣyate tataḥ
uktaprāyaṃ sukhārthaṃ tu vakṣye lakṣyānusārataḥ 512
vyādīrṇaṃ śvasitaṃ vakraṃ saṃhataṃ calasaṃhatam
sphuritaṃ calitaṃ lolamevaṃ cibukamaṣṭadhā
vyādīrṇaṃ dūraniṣkrāntaṃ jṛmbhālasyādiṣu smṛtam 513
iti vyādīrṇam
ekāṅgulamadhaḥsrastaṃ śvasitaṃ vīkṣite'dbhute
iti śvasitam
vakraṃ tiryaṅnataṃ tattu grahāveśe vidhīyate 514
iti vakram
niścalaṃ mīlitamukhaṃ maune saṃhatamipyate
iti saṃhatam
saṃlagnauṣṭhaṃ calaṃ nārīcumvane calasaṃhatam 515
iti calasaṃhatam
kampitaṃ sphuritaṃ prāhuḥ śīte śītajvare tathā
iti sphuritam
calitaṃ śliṣṭaviśliṣṭaṃ vākstambhe kṣobhakopayoḥ
iti calitam
romanthe kevalāvarte lolaṃ tiryaggatāgatam 516
iti lolam
vyābhugnaṃ bhugnamudvāhi vidhutaṃ vivṛtaṃ tathā
vinivṛttamiti prājñāḥ ṣaḍvidhaṃ vadanaṃ jaguḥ 517
kiṃcidāyāmivakraṃ ca vyābhugnaṃ mukhamucyate
nirvedautsukyacintādau taduktaṃ pūrvasūribhiḥ 158
iti vyābhugnam
adhomukhaṃ tu bhugnaṃ tallajjāyāṃ prakṛtau yateḥ
iti bhugnam
līlāsūtkṣiptamudvāhi garvānādarato gatau 519
ityudvāhi
vāraṇe naivamityuktau vidhutaṃ tiryagāyatam
iti vidhutam
viśliṣṭauṣṭaṃ tu vivṛtaṃ hāsyaśokabhayādiṣu 520
iti vivṛtam
vinivṛttaṃ parāvṛttaṃ roṣerṣyāsūyiteṣu tat 521
iti vinivṛttam
iti dvādaśa śirogatānyupāṅgāni
utkṣiptā patitotkṣiptapatitā'ntargatā tathā
bahirgatā mitho yuktā viyuktā'ṅgulisaṃgatā
pārṣṇirityaṣṭadhā pādacārī sthāneṣu dṛśyate 522
gullphāvaṅguṣṭhasaṃśliṣṭāvantaryātau bahirgatau
mitho yuktau viyuktau ca pañcadhā sthānakādiṣu 523
saṃhatā viyutā vakra valitāḥ patitāstathā 524
kuñcanmūlāḥ kare'ṅgulyaḥ prasṛtāśceti saptadhā
ete pārṣṇyādibhedāḥ syuḥ saṃjñāvijñānalakṣaṇāḥ 525
adhaḥ kṣiptāstathotkṣiptāḥ kuñcitāśca prasāritāḥ
saṃlagnāśceti caraṇāṅgulayaḥ pañcadhā matāḥ 526
muhuḥ pātādadhaḥ kṣiptā vivvoke kilakiñcite
utkṣiptā muhurutkṣepānnavoḍhāyāstrapābhare 527
saṃkocātkuñcitāḥ śītamūrchātrāsagrahārdite
prasāritā ṛjustabdhā stambhe svāpe'ṅgamoṭane 528
aṅguṣṭhasyāpyamī bhedā jñātavyā nṛttakovidaiḥ
svāṅguṣṭhāsu mitho lagnāḥ saṃlagnā gharṣaṇe matāḥ 529
patitāgraṃ coddhṛtāgraṃ bhūmilagnamathoddhṛtam
kuñcanmadhyaṃ tiraścīnamiti ṣoḍhā talaṃ viduḥ 530
iti pārṣṇigulphāṅgulitalāni karacaraṇopāṅgāni |
vivṛṇoti manovṛttiṃ mukharāgo rasātmikām
ato rasopayogitvānmukharāgo'bhidhīyate 531
svābhāvikaḥ prasannaśca raktaśyāmo'parastathā
caturdhā mukharāgo'trānvarthaḥ svābhāviko mataḥ 532
anāviṣṭeṣu bhāveṣu sudhībhiḥ sa vidhīyate
iti svābhāvikaḥ
prasanno nirmalo hāsye śṛṅgāre cādbhute bhavet 533
iti prasannaḥ
rakto'ruṇaḥ syātkaruṇe raudre vīre'dbhute tathā
iti raktaḥ
anvartho bhavati śyāmaḥ sa bībhatse bhayānake 534
iti śyāmaḥ
śaśineva diśo'ṅgāni śobhante mukharāgataḥ 535
rasabhāvāntare netramanyadanyatkṣaṇe kṣaṇe
yathātathocitaḥ kāryo mukharāgo rase rase 536
iti caturdhā mukharāgaḥ
uttāno'dhomukhaḥ pārśvagataḥ pāṇiriti tridhā 537
pracāraṃ bharatā mene pañcadhā tvapare jaguḥ
agrago'dhastalaśceti dvau trayaśca puroditāḥ 538
tatra tvagraga uttāno'dhomukho'dhastalaḥ karaḥ
antarbhūtaṃ vadanbhaṭṭastritvameva samādadhe 539
uttāno'dhomukhaḥ pārśvagato hasto'gratastalaḥ
svasaṃmukhatalaścordhvamukho'dhovadanastathā 540
parāṅmukhaḥ saṃmukhaśca hasto'nyaḥ pārśvatomukhaḥ
ūrdhvago'dhogataḥ pārśvagato hasto'grago'paraḥ 541
saṃmukhāgata ityetānpracārāndaśa pañca ca
lakṣyalakṣaṇatattvajñaḥ śārṅgadevo'bhyabhāṣata 542
iti pañcadaśa hastapracārāḥ
niṣpattau nirapekṣāyāṃ karasyābhinayāya yaḥ
kriyāviśeṣaḥ kriyate taddhastakaraṇaṃ matam 543
āveṣṭitodveṣṭite ca vyāvartitamataḥ param
parivartitamityetaccaturdhā dhīrasaṃmatam 544
tarjanyādyaṅgulīnāṃ yadbhavedāveṣṭanaṃ kramāt
talasaṃmukhamāvakṣaḥ karo'pyāyāti pārśvataḥ
tadāveṣṭitamākhyātaṃ karasya karaṇaṃ budhaiḥ 545
yattu krameṇa niryāṇamaṅgulīnāṃ talādbahiḥ
vakṣaḥsthalātkarasyāpi tadudveṣṭitamucyate 546
āveṣṭitaprakriyayā kāryamāvartitaṃ kare
udveṣṭitena vyākhyātaṃ karaṇaṃ parivartitam 547
kiṃtvetatkaraṇadvaṃdvaṃ kaniṣṭhādyaṅgulīkṛtam 548
iti catvāri karakaraṇāni
dhūnanaṃ śleṣaviśleṣau kṣepo rakṣaṇamokṣaṇe
parigraho nigrahaścotkṛṣṭākṛṣṭivikṛṣṭayaḥ 549
tāḍanaṃ tolanaṃ chedabhedau sphoṭanamoṭane
syādvisarjanamāhvānaṃ tarjanaṃ ceti viṃśatiḥ
karakarmāṇi nṛttajñairanvarthāni babhāṣire 550
iti viṃśatiḥ karakarmāṇi
pārśvadvayaṃ purastācca paścādūrdhvamadhaḥ śiraḥ 551
lalāṭakarṇaskandhoronābhayaḥ kaṭiśīrṣake
ūrudvayaṃ ca hastānāṃ kṣetrāṇīti caturdaśa 552
iti hastakṣetrāṇi
karaṇaiḥ karaṇātīto'naṅgahāro'ṅgahārakaiḥ
yaḥ sthāṇū rodasīraṅge nṛtyatīśaṃ namāmi tam 553
syātkriyā karapādādervilāsenātruṭadrasā 554
karaṇaṃ nṛttakaraṇaṃ bhīmavadbhīmasenavat
prabhedānkaraṇasyāsya brūmahe bharatoditān 555
talapuṣpapuṭaṃ līnaṃ vartitaṃ ca litoru ca
maṇḍalasvastikaṃ vakṣaḥsvastikākṣiptarecite 556
svastikānyardhadikpṛṣṭhādyāni svastikamañcitam
apaviddhaṃ samanakhonmatte svastikarecitam 557
nikuṭṭārdhanikuṭṭe ca kaṭīchinnaṃ kaṭīsamam
bhujaṃgatrāsitālātabikṣiptākṣiptakāni ca 558
nikuñcitaṃ ghūrṇitaṃ syādūrdhvajānvardharecitam
mattalli cārdhamattalli syādrecakanikuṭṭakam 559
lalitaṃ valitaṃ daṇḍapakṣaṃ pādāpaviddhakam
nūpuraṃ bhramaraṃ chinnaṃ bhujaṃgatrastarecitam 560
bhujaṃgāñcitakaṃ daṇḍarecitaṃ caturaṃ tataḥ
kaṭibhrāntaṃ vyaṃsitaṃ ca krantaṃ vaiśākharecitam 561
vṛñśikaṃ vṛścikādye ca syātāṃ kuṭṭitarecite
latāvṛścikamākṣiptārgale talavilāsitam 562
lalāṭatilakaṃ pārśvanikuṭṭaṃ cakramaṇḍalam
uromaṇḍalamāvartaṃ kuñcitaṃ dolapādakam 563
vivṛttaṃ vinivṛttaṃ ca pārśvakrāntaṃ niśumbhitam
vidyuddhāntamatikrāntaṃ vikṣiptaṃ ca vivartitam 564
gajakrīḍitakaṃ gaṇḍasūcī syādgarudaplutam
talasaṃsphoṭitaṃ pārśvajānu gṛdhrāvalīnakam 565
sūcyardhasūcinī sūcīviddhaṃ ca hariṇaplutam
parivṛttaṃ daṇḍapādaṃ mayūralalitaṃ tataḥ 566
preṅkholitaṃ saṃnataṃ ca sarpitaṃ karihastakam
prasarpitamapakrāntaṃ nitambaṃ skhalitābhidham 567
siṃhavikrīḍitaṃ siṃhākarṣitaṃ cāvahitthakam
niveśamelakākrīḍajanitopasṛtāni ca 568
talasaṃghaṭṭitodvṛttaviṣṇukrāntāni lolitam
madaskhalitasaṃbhrāntaviṣkambhodghaṭṭitāni ca 569
śakaṭāsyorūdvṛttākhye vṛṣabhakrīḍitaṃ tataḥ
nāgāpasarpitamatho gaṅgāvataraṇaṃ tathā 570
ityaṣṭottaramuddiṣṭaṃ karaṇānāṃ śataṃ mayā
gatisthitiprayogāṇāmānantyātkaraṇānyapi 571
anantānyaṅgahāreṣu kriyantāmupayogitā
atha lakṣaṇameteṣāṃ vakṣye lakṣyavidāṃ matam 572
prāyaśo nartanārambhe samapādau latākarau
caturasraṃ bhavedaṅgaṃ viśeṣastvayamucyate 573
cāryā dvyardhikayā pāde viniṣkrāmati dakṣiṇe
vyāvartanātkarayuge dakṣiṇaṃ pārśvamāgate 574
parivartanato vāmapārśvaṃ satatamāśrite
tatkucakṣetrasaṃviṣṭo yasya puṣpapuṭaḥ karaḥ
talapuṣpapuṭaṃ tatsyātpāde'gratalasaṃcare 575
yadyanantarametatsyātkaraṇaṃ karaṇāntarāt 576
tadā karakriyā tattattyājyagrāhyakarānugā
etatpuṣpāñjalikṣepe lajjāyāṃ ca niyujyate 577
iti talapuṣpapuṭam
ūrdhvamaṇḍalinau kṛtvā karau vakṣaḥsthito'ñjaliḥ
nihañcitaṃ cāṃsakūṭaṃ yatra grīvā natā kṛtā
līnaṃ tatkaraṇaṃ yojyaṃ vallabhābhyarthane budhaiḥ 578
iti līnam
āśliṣṭau maṇibandhasthasvastikābhimukhau karau
kṛtvā vakṣasyekakālaṃ vyāvṛttaparivartitau 579
uttānau pātayedūrvoryatra tadvartitaṃ matam
asūyāyāṃ prayogaḥ syātpatākau yadi pātayet 580
adhomukhanighṛṣṭau tau krodhaṃ sūcayataḥ karau
śukatuṇḍādayo'pyanye viniyogavaśādiha 581
iti vartitam
vakṣaḥkṣetre samaṃ hastau vyāvṛttaparivartitau 582
vidhāyā''kṣiptayā cāryā saṃhatau parivartanāt
tatrā''nīya nidhīyete śukatuṇḍāvadhomukhau 583
baddhā yāti sthitiryatra valitoru taducyate
mugdhastrīvrīḍite cāsya prayogaḥ śārṅgiṇoditaḥ 584
iti valitoru
caturasrau karau kṛtvā vidadhadvicyavāṃ tataḥ
udveṣṭitakriyāpūrvamūrdhvamaṇḍalinau karau 585
vidhāya svaskikau kuryādatra sthānaṃ ca maṇḍalam
maṇḍalasvastikaṃ tatsyātprasiddhārthāvalokane 586
iti maṇḍalasvastikam
caturasrau karau vakṣaḥsthitau kṛtvā'tha recitau
vyāvartitena cā''nīyābhugne vakṣasi yatra tau 587
svastikau svastikau pādau tadvakṣaḥsvastikaṃ matam
anābhugnāṃsametacca yojyaṃ lajjānutāpayoḥ a588
iti vakṣaḥsvastikam
hastau hṛtkṣetragau yatra vyāvṛtyordhvaṃ ca pārśvayoḥ
kṣiptaikaṃ haṃsapakṣaṃ ca drutabhramamadhomukham 589
vakṣaḥ prāpyāparaṃ tādṛgbhūtaṃ niṣkrāmayettataḥ
pādāvañcitasūcyau ca tatsyādākṣiptarecitam
anenābhinaye tyāgaparigrahaparamparām 590
ityākṣiptarecitam
dakṣiṇaḥ karihastaḥ syādvāmastu khaṭakāmukhaḥ
vakṣasthaḥ svastikau pādau yatrārdhasvastikaṃ tu tat 591
anye karikarasthāne pakṣavañcitakaṃ param
pakṣapradyotakaṃ cārdhacandraṃ kaṭigataṃ viduḥ 592
ityardhasvastikam
pārśvayoragrataḥ pṛṣṭhe svastiko'tra diśaṃ gataḥ
karāṅghrī recitau yatra taddiksvastikamucyate 593
tadgītaparivarteṣu viniyuktaṃ purātanaiḥ
svastikaprakriyaiṣā syātsvastikoktyantareṣvapi 594
iti diksvastikam
udveṣṭitakriyāpūrvaṃ bāhvorvikṣiptyamāṇayoḥ
kṛtvā cārīmapakrāntāṃ kriyamāṇe'paveṣṭane 595
recayitvā'nyacaraṇaṃ sūcīsvastikamācaret
yatrāṅghribhyāṃ karābhyāṃ ca tatpṛṣṭhasvastikaṃ bhavet 596
etanniṣedharābhasyaparānveṣaṇabhāṣaṇe
prayuktaṃ nāṭyatattvajñairanyairyuddhaparikrame 597
iti pṛṣṭhasvastikam
udveṣṭena niṣkāsya pāṇyorvyāvartamānayoḥ 598
utplutya yugapadyatra karāṅghriḥ svastikaḥ kṛtaḥ
tatsvastikaṃ prayoktavyaṃ budhaiḥ pūrvoktavastunoḥ 599
iti svastikam
vyāvartaparivartābhyāṃ nāsādeśaṃ gato yadgā
karihasto'lapadmatvaṃ dhatte syādañcitaṃ tadā
saṃmukhaprekṣaṇe tacca yojyaṃ svasyātikautukāt 600
ityañcitam
caturasrakaraḥ sthitvā hastaṃ vyāvartya dakṣiṇam 601
niṣkrāmayanbhajeccārīmākṣiptāmatha dakṣiṇam
śukatuṇḍaṃ karaṃ tasyaivorau tu paripātayet 602
yatrāpaviddhaṃ tadvāme vakṣasthe khaṭakāmukhe
tatkopāsūyayoryojyamuktaṃ soḍhalasūnunā 603
ityapaviddhaṃ
dehaḥ svābhāviko yatra pādau samanakhau yutau
latāhastau samanakhaṃ syātpraveśe tadādime 604
cāryā viddhāñcitapade paryāyādrecitaḥ karaḥ
yatronmattaṃ tu tadgarve saubhāgyādisamudbhave 605
ityunmattam
caturasraḥ sthitaḥ kṛtvā haṃsapakṣau drutabhramau
vyāvṛttiparivṛttibhyāmadhaścordhvaṃ śiraḥsthalāt
ānīyā''viddhavakrau cedvakṣasi svastikau kṛtau 606
viprakīrṇau tataḥ kāryau pakṣavañcitakau karau
pakṣapradyotakau paścāccārī tadvaśagā bhavet 607
ante'vahitthakaṃ sthānaṃ tadā svastikarecitam
nṛttābhinayane tacca praharṣādau niyujyate 608
iti svastikarecitam
maṇḍalasthānake kṛtvā caturasratayā sthitaḥ
udveṣṭya dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ nītvā skandhaśirasyamum 609
patanotpatanāviṣṭakaniṣṭhādyaṅgulīdvayam
alapadmākṛtiṃ kṛtvodghaṭṭite'ṅghrau ca dakṣiṇe 610
āviddhavaktratāṃ nītvā kare'tra caturasrite
tathaiva vāmapāṇyaṅghri yatra syāttannikuṭṭakam
ātmasaṃbhāvanākhyānapare vākye niyujyate 611
iti nikuṭṭakam
tadevārdhanikuṭṭaṃ syādekenāṅgena cetkṛtam
aprarūḍhavacaḥprokte tatraivārthe niyujyate 612
ityardhanikuṭṭakam
pārśvena bhramarīṃ kṛtvā maṇḍalasthānake sthitaḥ
kṛtvā chinnāṃ kaṭīmekāṃ bāhvoḥ śirasi pallavam 613
karamaṅgāntareṇaivaṃ yatra tricaturāḥ kṛtāḥ
āvṛttayaḥ kaṭīchinnaṃ tadvismayanirūpaṇe 614
iti kaṭīchinnam
ākṣiptāmapyapakrāntāṃ cārīṃ kṛtvā karadvayam
svastikīkṛtya nābhistho dakṣiṇaḥ khaṭakāmukhaḥ 615
kaṭyāmanyo'rdhacandraśca kṛtaḥ pārśvaṃ ca tannatam
anyadudvāhitaṃ yatra vṛttiraṅgāntaraistathā 616
tatkaṭīsamamādiṣṭaṃ vaiṣṇavasthānakānvitam
prayojyaṃ sūtradhāreṇa jarjarasyābhimantraṇe 617
iti kaṭīsamam
bhujaṃgatrāsitāṃ cārīṃ kṛtvotkṣipya ca kuñcitam
aṅghrimūrukaṭījānutryasraṃ yatra vivartayet 618
vyāvṛttiparivṛttibhyāmeko dolākaro'paraḥ
khaṭakākhyastadanvarthaṃ bhujaṃgatrāsitaṃ matam 619
iti bhujaṃ gatrāsitam
cāryālātā nitambaśca karo'tha caturasrakaḥ
etaddakṣiṇato vāma ūrdhvajānustathaiva cet
aṅgāntaraṃ tadā'lātaṃ nṛtte salalite matam 620
ityalātam
haste vyāvartamāne tu so'ṅghrirvikṣipyate bahiḥ
caturasraḥ paraḥ pāṇiḥ pūrvo'tha parivartane 621
pāṇirākṣipyate cāṅghriryatrāṅgaṃ cānyadīdṛśam
vikṣiptākṣiptakaṃ ca syādgatāgatanirūpaṇe 622
imaṃ tu nāṭyatattvajñā viniyogaṃ na manvate
vākyārthābhinayastatra yatrābhinayahastakāḥ 623
prādhānyena vidhīyante nṛttamātraparaṃ tvidam
ato'ntarālasaṃdhāne gatīnāṃ ca parikrame 624
tathā yuddhaniyuddhasthacārīsthānakasaṃkrame
kārye tālānusaṃdhāne yojyaṃ karaṇamīdṛśam 625
vṛścikaṃ caraṇaṃ kṛtvā yatrārālaṃ śirasthale
taddikkaṃ karamādhāha nāsākṣetrārjavena tu 626
kṛto vakṣasyarālo'nyastadvadanti nikuñcitam
etadutpatanautsukyavitarkādau prayujyate 627
anye patākasūcyāsyāvasminnāsāgragau viduḥ 628
iti nikuñcitam
ūrdhvaṃ vyāvartanenādhaḥ parivartanataḥ kare
pārśvakṣetrāddhāmyamāṇe jaṅghāsvastikamācaret 629
tato'pakrāntacārīkastaddikkaścaraṇo yadā
vāmo dolākaraḥ proktaṃ karaṇaṃ ghūrṇitaṃ tadā 630
iti ghūrṇitam
kuñcite caraṇe cāryāṃ cordhvajānau yadā karaḥ
taddigbhavo'lapadmo vā'rālaścordhvamukho bhavet 631
pakṣavañcitakau cordhvaṃ stanasāmyasthajānunaḥ
khaṭakākhyo'paro vakṣasyūrdhvajānustadocyate 632
ityūrdhvajānuḥ
maṇḍale sthānake sthitvā vakṣasthaḥ khaṭakāmukhaḥ
sūcīmukhaṃ cāpasārya yadā tasyāntike kṛtaḥ 633
aṅghrirudghṛṭṭitaḥ pārśvaṃ saṃnataṃ cāpasāraṇe
tadardharecitaṃ yojyamasamañjasaceṣṭane 634
ityardharecitam
gulphayoḥ svastikaṃ kṛtvā pādayorapasarpaṇe
yugapatkarayoryatrodveṣṭanaṃ cāpaveṣṭānam
kuryānmuhurmahustattu mattalli madavedakam 635
iti mattali
upetyāprasṛtau pādau vāmaścedrecitaḥ karaḥ
kaṭyāmanyastadā tvardhamattalli taruṇe made 636
recito dakṣiṇo hastaḥ so'ṅghriruddhaṭṭito bhavet
yatra dolākaro vāmastadrecakanikuṭṭakam 637
iti recakanikuṭṭakam
nitambakeśabandhādivartano dakṣiṇaḥ karaḥ 638
bahuśaḥ karihasto'nyaḥ pādaścodghaṭṭitastathā
aṅgāntaraṃ cellalitaṃ tadā nṛtte vilāsini 639
iti lalitam
dehakṣetrātkare sūcīmukhasaṃjñe'pasarpati
sūcīpādo'pyapasṛtaścārī syāddhamarī tataḥ
kramādaṅgadvaye'pyevaṃ lalite valitaṃ bhavet 640
iti valitam
yatrordhvajānuścārī syāllatāhastau karau kṛtau 641
tayorekaṃ tato nyasyeduparyūrdhvasthajānutaḥ
punaraṅgāntareṇaiva daṇḍapakṣaṃ taducyate 642
iti daṇḍapakṣam
parāṅmukhau nābhitaṭe hastau cetkhaṭakāmukhau
sūcīpādo'ṅghriṇā'nyena yuktvā'pakrāntayā'nvitaḥ
aṅghriranyastathaiva syāttadā pādāpaviddhakam 643
iti pādāpaviddhakam
vidhāya bhramarīṃ cārīṃ tato nūpurapādikām 644
pādenaikena taddikkaṃ hastaṃ recitamācaret
dvitīyaṃ tu latāhastaṃ yatra tannūyuraṃ viduḥ 645
iti nūpuram
pādamākṣiptacārīkaṃ tadaivodveṣṭitaṃ karam
trikaṃ ca valitaṃ kṛtvā pādasvastikamācaret 646
tatastadvaddvitīyāṅgaṃ yugapaccolbaṇau karau
yatra taddhamaraṃ yojyaṃ taccoddhataparikrame 647
iti bhramaram
alapadmau kaṭīpārśvakṣetre yatra kramātkarau
kaṭīchinnā ca vaiśākhaṃ sthānaṃ tacchinnamucyate
syāttālabhañjane tacca tathā'ṅgapratisāraṇe 648
iti cchinnam
bhujaṃgatrāsitā cārī tato yatra ca recitau
hastau sto vāmapārśve tadbhujaṃgatrastarecitam 649
iti bhujaṃgatrastarecitam
cārī ceddakṣiṇāṅghreḥ syātpūrvoktā dakṣiṇaḥ karaḥ
recito'nyo latāhasto bhujaṃgāñcitakaṃ tadā 650
iti bhujaṃgāñcitam
cārī ceddaṇḍapādā syāddaṇḍapakṣau ca hastakau
pramodanṛttaviṣayaṃ tadā syāddaṇḍarecitam
prayogamapare tvāhurasyoddhataparikrame 651
iti daṇḍarecitam
vakṣaḥkṣetrasthayoḥ pāṇyorvāmaścedalapallavaḥ
dakṣiṇaścaturo'ṅghristūdghaṭṭitaścaturaṃ tadā
etadvismayasūcāyāṃ vaidūṣikyāṃ niyujyate 652
iti caturam
drutāpasāritaṃ vāmaṃ sūcīpādaṃ vidhāya cet
tatpārśve dakṣiṇaṃ nyasyettatkālaṃ recayetkaṭim 653
kurvanvā bhramarīṃ hastau vyāvṛttaparivartitau
kṛtvā'nte caturasraḥ syātkaṭibhrāntaṃ tadā bhavet 654
asya tālāntarāleṣu yatīnāṃ paripūraṇe
prayogamāha niḥśaṅkastathā gatiparikrame 655
iti kaṭibhrāntam
eka udveṣṭitenādho viprakīrṇaḥ karo'paraḥ
ūrdhvaṃ tādṛkparāvṛttyā vakṣaḥkṣetragatastataḥ 656
uttāno recitaścaiko'paro'dhomukharecitaḥ
ālīḍhaṃ sthānakaṃ yatra tadvyaṃsitamudāhṛtam
ālīḍhaṃ sthānakaṃ yatra tadvyaṃsitamudāhṛtam
prayojyamāñjaneyādimahākapiparikrame 657
iti vyaṃsitam
cāryā'tikrāntayā pādaṃ pātyamānaṃ tu kuñcayet
vyāvartitena tatkālaṃ karaṃ niṣkrāmayettataḥ 658
ākṣipya parivartena taṃ kuryātkhaṭakāmukham
vakṣasyevaṃ dvitīyāṅgaṃ yatra tatkrāntamiṣyate
prayogamāhurācāryāstasyoddhataparikrame 659
itikrāntam
hastapādakaṭigrīvaṃ recitaṃ sthānakaṃ punaḥ
vaiśākhaṃ yatra tajjñeyaṃ vijñairvaiśākharecitam 660
iti vaiśākharecitam
karihastau karau pādaḥ pṛṣṭhe vṛścikapucchavat
dūrasamnatapṛṣṭhaṃ ca yatra tadvṛścikaṃ viduḥ
etadairāvaṇādīnāṃ vyomayāne niyujyate 661
iti vṛścikam
vṛścikaṃ caraṇaṃ kṛtvā bāhuśīrṣe yadā kramāt
alapadmau nikuṭyete tadā vṛścikakuṭṭitam
savismaye vyomayānavāñchādau viniyujyate 662
iti vṛścikakuṭṭitam
yatrāṅghrī vṛścikībhūtau svastikau recitau karau
vicyutau cettadā''kāśayāne vṛśikarecitam 663
iti vṛścikarecitam
vṛśikaścaraṇo yatra vāmahasto latābhidhaḥ
tallatāvṛścikaṃ yojyaṃ gaganotpatane budhaiḥ 664
iti latāvṛścikam
ākṣiptā yatra cārī syādākṣiptaḥ khaṭakāmukhaḥ
caturo vā tadākṣiptaṃ vidūṣakagatau matam 665
ityākṣiptam
kaniṣṭhāṅgulibhāge cedvāmāṅghrerdakṣiṇaḥ sthitaḥ 666
stabdhajaṅghaḥ sārdhatāladvitayaṃ syātprasāritaḥ
tadaiva stabdhabāhuḥ sanvāmapārśve'lapallavaḥ 667
bāmetaraḥ karaḥ kiṃcitprasṛtāgro'rgalaṃ tadā
parikrame'ṅgadādīnāṃ śārṅgadevena kīrtitam 668
ityargalam
ūrdhvamūrdhvāṅgulitalaḥ pādaḥ pārśve prasāritaḥ 669
tadagrayukpatāko'ṅgaṃ yatretthamaparaṃ kramāt
sūtradhārādiviṣaye tatsyāttalavilāsitam 670
iti talavilāsitam
aṅguṣṭho vṛścikāṅghreścelalāte tilakaṃ likhet
tadā lalāṭatilakaṃ vidyādharagatau matam 671
iti lalāṭatilakam
pāṇyoḥ svastikayorūrdhvamukha pārśve nikuṭṭitaḥ 672
eko'nyodhomukho yatra tadvatpādo nikuṭṭitaḥ
tatprakāśanaṃ saṃcārābhyāse pārśvanikuṭṭakam 673
iti pārśvanikuṭṭakam
yatra kṛtvoḍḍitāṃ cārīṃ dolābhyāṃ cakravaddhamet 674
antarnatena gātreṇa tadūcuścakramaṇḍalam
surāṇāṃ varivasyāyāṃ taduddhataparikrame 675
iti cakramaṇḍalam
cāryau baddhāsthitāvarte uromaṇḍalinau karau
yatroromaṇḍalaṃ tattu śivavallabhabhāṣitam 676
ityuromaṇḍalam
udveṣṭitenāpaveṣṭe yatra dolābhidhau karau
cārī cāṣagatistatsyādāvartaṃ bhayasarpaṇe 677
ityāvartam
uttāno vāmapārśvastho'lapadmo dakṣiṇaḥ karaḥ 678
yatra tatkuñcitaṃ pāde savye'gratalasaṃcare
tena devānabhinayedānandabharanirbharān 679
iti kuñcitam
ūrdhvajānoḥ paraṃ cārī dolāpādā yadā bhavet
dolau hastau tadā proktaṃ dolāpādaṃ vidāṃvaraiḥ 680
iti dolāpādam
pādamākṣiptayā cāryā''kṣipyā''kṣipya karāvapi 681
vyāvṛttaparivṛttābhyāṃ kṛtvā bhramarikāṃ karau
recitau cedvivṛttaṃ syāttaccoddhataparikrame 682
iti vivattam
sūcyaṅghriṇā dvitīyāṅghreḥ pārṣṇitaḥ svastikaṃ bhavet
vyāvṛttipratyāvṛttibhyāṃ valayitvaikapārśvataḥ 683
trikaṃ baddhābhidhāṃ cārīṃ kuryāddhastau ca recitau
yatra tadvinivṛttaṃ syātpūrvoktaviniyogabhāk 684
iti vinivṛttam
pārśvakrāntā bhaveccārī karau pādānugau yadā a 685
pārśvakrāntaṃ tadā yadvā'bhineyavaśagau karau
tadyojyaṃ bhīmasenāde raudraprāye parikrame 686
iti pārśvakrāntam
pārṣṇideśe dvitīyāṅghraḥ kuñcitaścaraṇo bhavet
samunnatamuraḥ pāṇeḥ khaṭakākhyasya madhyamā 687
yatrāṅgulistilakayellalāṭaṃ tanniśambhitam
yadvā vṛścikahastaḥ syādabhineyo maheśvaraḥ 688
iti niśumbhitam
pṛṣṭhato bhrāmitaṃ pādaṃ sarvatomaṇḍalaplutaḥ 689
bhrāmayeccecchiraḥ kṣetre vidyuddhāntaṃ tadocyate
prayogamasya ca prāhuruddhatānāṃ parikrame 690
iti vidyuddhāntam
kṛtvā cārīmatikrāntāmaṅghrimagre prasārayet
yatra hastau prayogārhāvatikrāntamado viduḥ 691
ityatikrāntam
vidyuddhāntādaṇḍapāda kramāccāryau vidhāya cet
udveṣṭitāpaveṣṭābhyāmekamārgagatau karau 692
recayannagrataḥ pṛṣṭhe pārśvayorvikṣipettadā
vikṣiptamabhinetavyastenoddhataparikramaḥ 693
iti vikṣiptam
karaṃ caraṇamākṣipya yatra trikavivartanam
karo'paro recitaśca tadvadanti vivartitam 694
iti vivartitam
dolāpādā yadā cārī karihasto'sya karṇagaḥ
kriyāviṣṭaḥ karo'nvarthaṃ gajakrīḍikaṃ tadā 695
iti gajakrīḍitakam
sūcīpādo nataṃ pārśvaṃ vakṣasthaḥ khaṭakāmukhaḥ 696
vāmo
lapallavo gaṇḍakṣetre cedgaṇḍasūcitam
kṣetre'tra kecidicchanti sūcīpādaṃ pare punaḥ 697
sūcīmukhaṃ nṛttahastamanye'bhinayahastakam
karaṇenābhinetavyā kapolālaṃkṛtistadā 698
iti gaṇḍasūcī
latārecitakau hastau vṛścikāṅghriḥ samunnatam
uro yatra tadanvarthābhidhānaṃ garuḍaplutam 699
iti garuḍaplutam
daṇḍapādākhyayā cāryā yadvā'tikrāntayā drutam 700
utkṣipya pātyamāne'ṅghrāvagre cettālikāṃ karau
saśabdāṃ kuruto'nvarthaṃ talasaṃsphoṭitaṃ tadā 701
iti talasaṃsphoṭitam
samapādastu pṛṣṭhe cennihitaścaraṇo'paraḥ 702
muṣṭihastaḥ sthito vakṣasyardhacandraḥ kaṭītaṭe
pārśvajānu tadā jñeyaṃ yojyaṃ yuddhaniyuddhayoḥ 703
iti pārśvajānu
pṛṣṭhe'ṅghriḥ prasṛto bhūmiśliṣṭāṅguṣṭhau latākarau
yadā tadā mahājpakṣiyuddhe gṛdhrāvalīnakam 704
iti gṛdhrāvalīnakam
utkṣipya kuñcitaḥ pādaḥ sthāpyate bhūmimaspṛśan
khaṭakākhyaśca taddikko hasto vakṣasyathāparaḥ 705
alapadmaḥ śirodeśe tatahivāṅgāntaraṃ kramāt
yatra tacchārṅgadevena gaditaṃ sūci vismaye 706
iti sūci
sūcyevaikāṅgaracitamardhasūci pracakṣate 707
ityardhasūci
pakṣavañcitako vā'rdhacandro hastaḥ kaṭīgataḥ
khaṭakākhyo'paro vakṣasyanyapārṣṇisthito'paraḥ
sūcyaṅghriścettadā sūcīviddhaṃ cintādigocaram 708
iti sūcīviddham
hariṇaplutayā cāryā dolākhaṭakahastakam
hariṇaplutamākhyātaṃ nāmoktaviniyogakam 709
iti hariṇaplutam
ūrdhvamaṇḍalinau hastau sūcyaṅghriryatrabaddhayā
vivṛtto'tha bhramarikā parivṛttaṃ taducyate 710
iti parivṛttam
cārīnūpurapādo'tha daṇḍapādāddrutaṃ karaḥ
daṇḍavannyasyate yatra daṇḍapādaṃ taducyate
sūrayo viniyuñjanti tatsāṭopaparikrame 711
iti daṇḍapādam
hastau cedrecitāvūrū vṛścikāṅghriṃ nikuñcya ca
bhramarī kriyate'nvarthaṃ mayūralalitaṃ tadā 712
iti mayūralalitam
dolāpādākhyacārīkādaṅghreranyāṅghriṇā yadā
utplutya bhramarīṃ kuryādbhavetpreṅkholitaṃ tadā 713
iti preṅkholitam
kṛtvā mṛgaplutaṃ yatra kṛto'ṅghriḥ svastiko'grataḥ
dolau hastau saṃnataṃ tadadhamāpasṛtau bhavet 714
iti saṃnatam
añcite'pasaratyaṅghrāvanyāṅghrernāmitaṃ śiraḥ
tasminpārśve karo'pyeṣa rocito'ṅgāntaraṃ tathā
yatra tatsarpitaṃ mattasyopasarpāpasarpaṇe 715
iti sarpitam
vāmaścetkhaṭako vakṣasyudveṣṭitatayā paraḥ 716
tripatākaḥ karaḥ karṇe taddikkaścaraṇo'ñcitaḥ
niṣkrāmyate yadā proktaṃ karihastaṃ tadā budhaiḥ 717
iti karihastam
hasto yo recitastaddikpādaścedbhūmigharṣaṇāt
caletpādāntarānmandaṃ mandamanyo latākaraḥ
tadā khecarasaṃcāragocaraṃ syātprasarpitam 718
baddhājpakrāntayoścāryoḥ kṛtayorhastayoḥ punaḥ
tattatprayogānugayorapakrāntamudāhṛtam 719
ityapakrāntam
adhomukhāṅgulī hastau patākau cecchiraḥsthalam
ānīya parivṛttena niṣkrāmyordhvāṃsayostayoḥ 720
mithomukhāvavasthāpya svadehābhimukhāṅgulī
nitambākhyau vidhīyete nitambaṃ karaṇaṃ tadā 721
iti nitambam
dolāpādāṅghrigamanāgamane haṃsapakṣakaḥ
abhyetyathetthamanyāṅgaṃ yatra tatskhalitaṃ matam 722
iti skhalitam
kṛtvā'lātāṃ puro'ṅghriṃ ceddrutaṃ nyasya capeṭavat
kṛto hastastathā'nyāṅgaṃ siṃhavikrīḍitaṃ tadā
etadraudragatau yojyaṃ brūte śrīkaraṇāgraṇīḥ 723
iti siṃhavikrīḍitam
vṛściko'ṅghriḥ padmakośau vaurṇanābhau yadā karau 724
anyāṅghrau vṛścike prāñcau bhaṅktvā tau tādṛśau punaḥ
kṛtau siṃhābhinayane siṃhākarṣitakaṃ tadā 725
iti siṃhākarṣitam
vidhāya janitāṃ cārīṃ yadyarālālapallavau
lalāṭavakṣaḥkṣetrasthau hastāvabhimukhāṅgulī 726
kramātkṛtvodveṣṭitena vyāvṛttyā pārśvagau tataḥ
vakṣodeśe'paveṣṭena parivṛttyā ca tādṛśau 727
mithomukhau nidhīyete tadā syādavahitthakam
gaoṃpanaprāyavākyārthābhinaye tanniyujyate 728
anye'vahitthahastena yuktatvādavahitthakam
vadanti cintādaurbalyaprabhṛtyabhinayakṣamam 729
ityavahitthakam
yatra vakṣasi nirbhugne nihitau khaṭakāmukhau
maṇḍalaṃ sthānakaṃ tattu niveśaṃ gajavāhane 730
iti niveśam
elakākrīḍitā cārī ceddolakhaṭakau karau
saṃnataṃ valitaṃ gātramelakākrīḍitaṃ tadā
adhamaprakṛtiprāṇigatigocaramiṣyate 731
ityelakākrīḍitam
cārī cejjanitā muṣṭirvakṣasyanyo latākaraḥ
yadā tadā kriyārambhābhinaye janitaṃ bhavet 732
iti janitam
ākṣiptāṃ vāmataścārīṃ vyāvṛtya parivartitam 733
karaṃ kṛtvā nate pārśve dakṣiṇe'rālatāṃ nayet
yatra tatsyādupasṛtaṃ vinayenopasarpaṇe 734
ityupasṛtam
dolāpādāṃ bhajaṃścārīṃ saṃghaṭṭitatalau karau
patākau recayitvā cedvaiṣṇave sthānake sthitaḥ 735
kaṭisthaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ vāmaṃ recitamācaret
anukampāvidhāne syāttalasaṃghaṭṭitaṃ tadā 736
iti talasaṃghaṭṭitam
yatrāpasāritānītaṃ hastapādaṃ vidhīyate
gātramudvṛttacārīkaṃ tadudvṛttaṃ vidurbudhāḥ 837
ityudvṛttam
kuñcitaḥ prasaratyaṅghriragre yatrodgamonmukhaḥ
karau ca recitau viṣṇukrāntaṃ tatkramaṇe hareḥ 738
iti viṣṇukrāntam
recitaḥ pāṇirekaḥ syādvakṣasyanyo'lapallavaḥ
lolitaṃ śīrṣamubhayorviśrāntaṃ pārśvayorapi
vaiṣṇavaṃ sthānakaṃ yatra tadāhurlolitaṃ budhāḥ 749
iti lolitam
svastikāpasṛtau pādau krameṇa parivāhitam 740
śiro dolau yadā hastau madaskhalitakaṃ tadā
etanmadhyamade yojyaṃ bhāṣate bhavavallabhaḥ 741
iti madaskhalitam
āviddhāṃ vidadhaccārīṃ vyāvṛttaparivartitam
alapadmakaraṃ nyasyedūrupṛṣṭhe yadā tadā
saṃbhrāntaṃ tatprayoktavyaṃ sasaṃbhramaparikrame 742
iti saṃbhrāntam
apetyopavrajedvāmaṃ dakṣiṇo nṛttahastakaḥ 743
sūcīmukho nikuṭyeta sāṅghrirvāmakaro yadi
tadvadaṅgāntaraṃ kṛtvā sūcīpādaśca dakṣiṇaḥ 744
hasto'sāvalapadmaḥ syādvāmahastastu pūrvavat
evaṃ punaḥ punaryatra viṣkambhaṃ tadbabhāṣire 745
iti viṣkambham
udghaṭṭito'ṅghriḥ pārśvaṃ tatsaṃnataṃ tālikodyatau
hastau yatrodghaṭṭitaṃ tadāvṛttāṅgaṃ pramodakam 746
ityudghaṭṭitam
śakaṭāsyā bhaveccārī prasāryetāṅghriṇā saha 747
eko hasto dvitīyastu vakṣasthaḥ khaṭakāmukhaḥ
yatra tacchakaṭāsyaṃ syāttādṛśe bālakhelite 748
iti śakaṭāsyam
sahorūdvṛttayā cāryā yatra vyāvartanānvitau
arālakhaṭakau hastau nidadhyādūrupṛṣṭhayoḥ
ūrūdvṛttaṃ tadīrṣyāyāṃ prārthanāpremakopayoḥ 749
ityūrūdvṛttam
alātāṃ vidadhaccārīṃ hastau kurvīta recitau 750
vyāvṛttyā kuñcitau kṛtvā'lapadmau bāhuśīrṣayoḥ
nyasyete yatra taddhīrā vṛṣabhakrīḍitaṃ jaguḥ 751
iti vṛṣabhakrīḍitam
hastau cedrecitau syātāṃ śirastu parivāhitam
svastikāpasṛtau pādau tadā nāgāpasarpitam
prāhuḥ prayogametasya sūrayastaruṇe made 752
iti nāgāpasarpitam
aṅghrerutkṣepanikṣepāvanu pronnatisaṃnatī 753
bhajetāṃ tripatākau cedevameva śirastadā
gaṅgāvataraṇaṃ gaṅgāvatāre śārṅgiṇoditam 754
iti gaṅgāvarataṇam
kartavyaḥ karaṇe prāyo vāmo vakṣaḥsthitaḥ karaḥ
pāṇistu dakṣiṇastatra syāttattatkaraṇānugaḥ 755
ityaṣṭottaraśataṃ karaṇāni
atha deśyanusāreṇa deśe deśe lasadyaśāḥ
vadatyutplutipūrvāṇi karaṇāni harapriyaḥ 756
añcitaṃ caikacaraṇāñcitaṃ syādbhairavāñcitam
daṇḍapraṇāmāñcitaṃ ca kartaryañcitakaṃ tataḥ 757
alagaṃ trīṇi kūrmordhvāntarapūrvālagāni ca
lohaḍī kartarīlohaḍyekapādādilohaḍī 758
tataḥ syāddarpasaraṇaṃ śayanaṃ jalapūrvakam
nāgabandhaṃ kapālādyaṃ cūrṇanaṃ natapṛṣṭhakam 759
syānmatsyakaraṇaṃ cātha karasparśanasaṃjñakam
eṇaplutaṃ tatastiryakkaraṇaṃ tiryagañcitam 760
tiryaksvastikasaṃjñaṃ ca sūcyantamatha kīrtitam
bahyāntaśchannatiripālagacakrocitādimāḥ 761
sapta bhramaryo bhramarī śiraḥpūrvā digādimā
samapādāñcitaṃ bhrāntapādāñcitamataḥ param 762
skandhabhrāntaṃ ca ṣaṭtriṃśaditi soḍhalanandanaḥ
saṃkṣipyotplutipūrvāṇi karaṇāni samādiśat 763
añcitaṃ samapādena sthitvottālotpluto bhavet
ityañcitam
ekapādāñcitaṃ tatsyādekapādavinirmitam 764
ityekacaraṇāñcitam
ūrupṛṣṭhasthitaikāṅghrerutplutau bhairavāñcitam
iti bhairavāñcitam
utplutyāñcitavadyatra nipateddaṇḍavadbhuvi
daṇḍapraṇāmāñcitaṃ tadgaditaṃ nṛttavedibhiḥ 765
iti daṇḍapraṇāmāñcitam
añcitaṃ svastikāṅghribhyāṃ kartaryañcimucyate
iti kartaryañcitam
adhomukhotpluto'gre ca patitvā kukkuṭāsanam
badhnīyādyatra tatproktamalagaṃ sūriśārṅgiṇā 766
ityalagam
kūrmāsanaṃ yadyalage bhavetkūrmālagaṃ tadā
iti kūrmālagam
ūrdhvālagaṃ tatpatitvā samāṅghrerūrdhvasaṃsthitau 767
ityūrdhvālagam
kṛtālago nipatyorvyāmuttānoraḥsthalasthitaḥ
pṛṣṭhataḥ śroṇisaṃsparśi śiraḥ syādantarālage 768
ityantarālagam
samapādasthito yatra vivartya trikamutplutaḥ
tiryakpāte lohaḍī talluṇṭhitaṃ cocyate budhaiḥ 769
iti lohaḍī luṇṭhitaṃ vā
svastikāṅghrikṛtā saiva kartarīlohaḍī matā
iti kartarīlohaḍī luṇṭhitaṃ vā
ekāṅghrijā tvekapādalohaḍī saṃmatā satām 770
ityekapādalohaḍī ekapādaluṇṭhitaṃ vā
vaiṣṇave sthānake sthitvā patetpārśvena cedbhuvi
karaṇaṃ darpasaraṇaṃ tadāha karaṇādhipaḥ 771
iti darpasaraṇam
tadeva jalaśayyākhyamāsane jalaśāyivat
iti jalaśayanam
nāgabandhaṃ tadeva syānnāgabandhavadāsane 772
iti nāgabandham
samapādasthito yatra saṃspṛśya śirasā bhuvam
parāvṛttastatkapālacūrṇanaṃ varṇitaṃ budhaiḥ 773
iti kapālacūrṇanam
kapālacūrṇanaṃ kṛtvottānaṃ vakṣasthalaṃ tataḥ
yatroktaṃ natapṛṣṭhaṃ tadvaṅkolamaparairadaḥ 774
utplutya madhyamāvartya vāmapārśvena matsyavat
parivarteta cenmatsyakaraṇaṃ varṇitaṃ tadā 775
iti matsyakaraṇam
alagaṃ karaṇaṃ kṛtvā hastenā''śritya ca kṣitim
parivarteta yatrādaḥ karasparśanamūcire 776
iti karasparśanam
utplutyānyatamāṃ sūcīṃ khe kṛtvā bhajate kṣitau
yadotkaṭāsanaṃ cordhvasthānameṇaplutaṃ tadā 777
ityeṇaplutam
tiryagekena pādena samutplutya nipatya cet
tiṣṭhedbhuvyaṅghriṇā'nyena syāttiryakkaraṇaṃ tadā 778
iti tiryakkaraṇam
samapādātparam tiryagutplutau tiryagañcitam
iti tiryagañcitam
syāttiryaksvastikaṃ kṛtvā svastikaṃ tiryagutplutau 779
iti tiryaksvastikam
samādyanyatamāṃ sūcīṃ dadhate nidhane yadi
karaṇāni tadā prāñci sūcyantāni pracakṣate 780
iti sūcyantam
dakṣiṇenāṅghriṇā sthitvā vāmamaṅghriṃ tu kuñcayet
vāmāvartaṃ bhavedyatra sā bāhyabhramarī matā 781
iti bāhyabhramarī
etasyāstu viparyāsādantarbhramarikā bhavet
ityantarbhramarī
trivikramākāradhārī sthānamāsthāya yatra tu
vāmāvartabhramādāhustāṃ channabhramarīṃ budhāḥ 782
iti cchannabhramarī
tiripabhramarī tiryagbhrame'ṅghrisvastikātparam 783
iti tiripabhramarī
vaiṣṇavasthānake sthitvā yadā vāmāṅghriṇā sthitaḥ
dehaṃ bhrāmayate tiryagalagabhramarī tadā 784
ityalagabhramarī
cakrabhramarikā khaṇḍasūcyardhe cakravaddhamāt
iti cakrabhramarī
samapādānanataraṃ cettiraścīṃ bhrāmayettanum
ucitabhramarīṃ prāha tadā śaṃkaravallabhaḥ 785
ityucitabhramarī
śirasaiva bhuvi sthitvordhvīkṛtau caraṇau dadhat
paribhramati yatra triḥ sā śirobhramarī matā 786
iti śiro'bhramarī
prāgvatsakṛtsakṛddhāntvā hastabhrāntyā dhṛtakṣitiḥ
dikcatuṣke kramāttiṣṭhedyadā digbhramarī tadā 787
iti digbhramarī
samapādāñcitaṃ kṛtvā skandhenaikena bhūsthitaḥ 788
parivṛtya ca tiryakcetpādāvullolayettadā
samapādāñcitaṃ prāha sūriḥ śrīkaraṇāgraṇīḥ 789
iti samapādāñcitam
bhrāmayitvā dakṣiṇāṅghriṃ tadīyatalapṛṣṭhataḥ
jaṅghāmadhyamavaṣṭābhya vāmāṅghrerañcitaṃ padam 790
vidhāya tiryaksaṃdhābhyāmadhiṣṭhāya vivṛtya ca
pādāvullolayetproktaṃ bhrāntapādāñcitaṃ tadā 791
iti bhrāntapādāñcitam
utkaṭāsanamāsthāyāñcitaṃ kṛtvā sayugmataḥ
sthitvā bhuvi vyomni kṛtvā'ṅgāntarāṇi tato bhuvam 792
avaṣṭābhya karābhyāṃ ceddhāntvā bhrāntvā ca pūrvavat
tiṣṭhetpratidiśaṃ proktaṃ skandhabhrāntaṃ tadā budhaiḥ 793
iti skandhabhrāntam
vidyante'nye'pi bhūyāṃso bhedāḥ karaṇasaṃśrayāḥ
na te śrīśārṅgiṇā proktā granthaprathimabhīruṇā 794
iti ṣaṭtriṃśadutplutikaraṇāni
pūrvaraṅge prayoktavyāndṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalānapi
aṅgahārānpravakṣyāmi nāmato lakṣmatastathā 795
aṅgānāmucite deśe prāpaṇaṃ savilāsakam
mātṛkotkarasaṃpādyamaṅgahāro'bhidhīyate 796
yadvā hāro harasyāyaṃ prayogo'ṅgairiti smṛtaḥ 797
karaṇābhyāṃ mātṛkā syātkalāpaḥ karaṇaistribhiḥ
caturbhiḥ khaṇḍako jñeyaḥ saṃghātaḥ pañcabhirmataḥ
iti saṃghaviśeṣeṇa saṃjñābhedānpare jaguḥ 798
karaṇanyūnatādhikyaṃ teṣāṃ mene muniḥ svayam
dvābhyāṃ tricaturābhirvetyetadvāśabdasūcitam 799
sthirahasto'tha paryastaḥ sūcīviddho'parājitaḥ
vaiśākharecitaḥ pārśvasvastiko bhramaro'paraḥ 800
ākṣiptakaḥ paricchinno madādvilasitastataḥ
ālīḍhācchuritau pārśvacchedasaṃjño'pasarpitaḥ 801
mattākrīḍastathā vidyuddhānto'mī ṣoḍaśoditāḥ
caturasreṇa mānenāṅgahārā munisattamaiḥ 802
viṣkambhāpasṛtau mattaskhalito gatimaṇḍalaḥ
apaviddhaśca viṣkambhodghaṭṭitākṣiptarecitāḥ 803
recito'rdhanikuṭṭaśca vṛścikāpasṛtastataḥ
alātakaḥ parāvṛttaḥ parivṛttādirecitaḥ 804
uddhṛttakaśca saṃbhrāntasaṃjñaḥ svastikarecitaḥ
ṣoḍaśeti tryasramānā dvātriṃśadubhaye matāḥ 805
karaṇavrātasaṃdarbhānantyātteṣāmanantatā
dvātriṃśatte tathā'pyuktāḥ prādhānyaviniyogataḥ 806
ekaikaṃ karaṇaṃ kāryaṃ kalayā gururūpayā
sarveṣāmaṅgahārāṇāmityāha karaṇāgraṇīḥ 807
līnaṃ samanakhaṃ kṛtvā vyaṃsitaṃ cātra vicyutau
karau kṛtvojjhitālīḍhaḥ pratyālīḍhaṃ vrajettataḥ 808
nikuṭṭakorūdvṛttākhyasvastikākṣiptakānyatha 809
nitambaṃ karihastaṃ ca kaṭīcchinnamiti kramāt
karaṇaiḥ sthirahastaḥ syāddaśabhiḥ śivavallabhaḥ 810
aṅgahāreṣu sarveṣu pratyālīḍhāntamāditaḥ
prayoktavyamiti prāhuḥ kecinnāṭyaviśāradāḥ 811
iti sthirahastaḥ
talapuṣpapuṭaṃ pūrvamapaviddhaṃ ca vartitam
nikuṭṭakorūdvṛttākhyākṣiptoromaṇḍalānyatha 812
nitambaṃ karihastaṃ ca kaṭīchinnamiti kramāt
daśabhiḥ karaṇairebhiḥ proktaḥ paryastako budhaiḥ 813
iti paryastakaḥ
ardhasūcyatha vikṣiptamāvartaṃ ca nikuṭṭakam
ūrūdvṛttamathā''kṣiptamuromaṇḍalasaṃjñakam 814
karihastaṃ kaṭīchinnaṃ navabhiḥ karaṇairiti
sūcīviddhābhidhaḥ prokto'ṅgahāro bharatādibhiḥ 815
iti sūcīviddhaḥ
daṇḍapādaṃ dvyaṃsitaṃ ca prasarpitakasaṃjñakam
nikuṭṭārdhanikuṭṭe cā''kṣiptoromaṇḍale tataḥ 816
karihastaṃ kaṭīchinnamiti lakṣmāparājite
navabhiḥ karaṇairyuktamuktaṃ niḥśaṅkasūriṇā 817
ityaparājitaḥ
vaiśākharecitaṃ dvābhyāmaṅgābhyāmatha nūpuram 818
bhujaṃgatrāsitonmattamaṇḍalasvastikānyatha
nikuṭṭakorūdvṛttākhyākṣiptoromaṇḍalāni ca 819
karihastaṃ kaṭīchinnamityekādaśabhiḥ kramāt
karaṇairaṅgahāraḥ syānnāmnā vaiśākharecitaḥ 820
iti vaiśākharecitaḥ
diksvastikaṃ tataścāṅgenaikenārdhanikuṭṭakam
punardiksvastikamathānyāṅgenārdhanikuṭṭakam 821
apaviddhorūdvṛttākhye tathā''kṣiptaṃ nitambakam

syātpārśvasvastiko'bhyāsāttvādyayordaśabhirbhavet 822
iti pārśvasvastikaḥ
nūpurākṣiptakacchinnasūcīnyatha nitambakam 823
karihastaṃ ca karaṇamuromaṇḍalakaṃ tataḥ
kaṭīchinnamiti proktaḥ karaṇairbhramaro'ṣṭabhiḥ 824
iti bhramaraḥ
kṛtvā nūpuravikṣiptālātānyākṣiptakaṃ tataḥ
uromaṇḍalasaṃjñaṃ ca karaṇairebhirākṣiptako'ṣṭabhiḥ
vikṣiptālātakākṣiptaprayogaṃ dviḥ pare jaguḥ 826
ityākṣiptakaḥ
kṛtvā samanakhaṃ chinnaṃ saṃbhrāntamatha vāmataḥ
bhramaraṃ vāmapārśvārdhasūcyaṅghrikrāntasaṃjñakam 827
bhujaṃgatrāsitādūrdhvaṃ karihastaṃ vidhīyate

iti paricchinnaḥ
madaskhalitamattallitalasaṃsphoṭitāni ca

ūrūdvṛttaṃ ca karaṇaṃ karihastasamāhvayam
kaṭīchinnaṃ ca sapteti madādvilasite jaguḥ 830
karaṇāni trayāṇāṃ tu trirabhyāsāttrayodaśa
abhyāsāṃstriṣu vāñchanti catuṣpañcādikānapi 831
iti madabilasitaḥ
dvyaṃsitaṃ sanikuṭṭaṃ syānnūpuraṃ vāmato'ṅghritaḥ
anyato'lātakākṣipte uromaṇḍalakaṃ tataḥ 832

bhavedaṣṭabhirālīḍho'ṅgahāro munisaṃmataḥ 833
ityālīḍhaḥ
nūpuraṃ bhramaraṃ cātha vyaṃsitālātake tataḥ 834
nitambaṃ sūcisaṃjñaṃ ca karihastamataḥ param
kaṭīchinnaṃ ca karaṇānyaṣṭetyācchurite viduḥ 835
ityācchuritaḥ
vṛścikādyaṃ kuṭṭitaṃ syādūrdhvajānu tataḥ param
ākṣiptasvastikaṃ kṛtvā trikasya parivartanam 836
uromaṇḍalasaṃjñaṃ ca nitambaṃ karihastakam
kaṭīchinnaṃ ca karaṇaṃ pārśvacchede'ṣṭamaṃ matam 837
iti pārśvacchedaḥ
apakrāntaṃ vyaṃsitasya kevalaṃ karayoḥ kriyā

sārdhāni dviḥ kaṭīchinnakarihastakṛternava 839
ityapasarpitaḥ
bhramaraṃ nūpurākhyaṃ ca bhujaṃgatrāsitābhidham 840

ākṣiptacchinnakaraṇe bhramaravyaṃsite tataḥ 841
uromaṇḍalasaṃjñaṃ ca nitambaṃ karihastakam
kaṭīchinnaṃ ca karaṇairebhirdvādaśabhiḥ kramāt 842
ekādaśabhirabhyāsādgaṇanā bhramarasya tu
mattākrīḍābhidhānaḥ syādaṅgahāro harapriyaḥ 843
iti mattākrīḍaḥ
vāmāṅgenārdhasūci syādvidyuddhāntaṃ tu dakṣiṇe
punaraṅgaviparyāsāddvayaṃ chinnamataḥ param 844
atikrāntaṃ vāmato'tha latāvṛścikamācaret
kaṭīchinnaṃ ca ṣaḍiti dvayorabhyāsataḥ punaḥ
karaṇānyaṣṭasaṃkhyāni vidyudbhrānte'bhyadhurbudhāḥ 845
iti vidyudbhrāntaḥ
nikuṭṭakākhyakaraṇaṃ vidhāyārdhanikuṭṭakam
bhujaṃgatrāsitādūrdhvaṃ bhujaṃgatrastarecitam 846

kaṭīchinnaṃ saptamaṃ tu viṣkambhāpasṛte bhavet 847
iti viṣkambhāpasṛtaḥ
mattalligaṇḍasūcyākhye līnaṃ mattāpaviddhakam

iti mattaskhalitaḥ
maṇḍalasvastikādūrdhvaṃ niveśonmattasaṃjñake
udghaṭṭitākhyaṃ mattalli syādākṣiptamataḥ param 850
uromaṇḍalakaṃ chinnaṃ kaṭyādi gatimaṇḍale
ityaṣṭau karaṇāni syuriti niḥśaṅkabhāṣitam 851
iti gatimaṇḍalaḥ
apaviddhaṃ prathamataḥ sūcīviddhamatho karau
udveṣṭite samaṃ cāryā baddhayā valayettrikam 852
ūrūdvṛttaṃ ca karaṇamuromaṇḍalakaṃ tataḥ
kaṭīchinnaṃ pañcamaṃ cedapaviddhastadā bhavet 853
ityapaviddhaḥ
nikuṭṭakākhyaṃ karaṇaṃ syānnikuñcitamañcitam
ūrūdvṛttaṃ tato'pyardhanikuṭṭakaraṇaṃ tataḥ 854

karihastaṃ kaṭīchinnaṃ viṣkambhe navamaṃ bhavet 855
iti viṣkambhaḥ
udghaṭṭite nikuṭṭākhyamuromaṇḍalakaṃ tataḥ
nitambaṃ karihastaṃ syātkaṭīchinnaṃ ca pañcamam 856
ityudghaṭṭitaḥ
svastikādyaṃ recitaṃ syātpṛṣṭhasvastikasaṃjñakam
diksvastikaṃ kaṭīchinnaṃ samaṃ ghūrṇitanāmakam 857
bhramaraṃ vṛścikādyaṃ ca recitaṃ pārśvapūrvakam
nikuṭṭakamuraḥpūrvaṃ maṇḍalaṃ saṃnataṃ tataḥ 858
siṃhākarṣitakaṃ nāgāpasarpitasamāhvayam
atha vakṣaḥsvastikaṃ tu kecidicchanti sūrayaḥ 859
daṇḍapakṣaṃ ca karaṇaṃ lalāṭatilakāhvayam
vilāsitaṃ talādyaṃ ca bhavedatha niśumbhitam 860
vidyuddhāntābhidhādūrdhvaṃ gajakrīḍitakaṃ tataḥ
nitambaviṣṇukrāntākhyorūdvṛttākṣiptakāni ca 861
uromaṇḍalasaṃjñaṃ ca nitambaṃ karihastakam
vaikalpikaṃ kaṭīchinnamicchantyākṣiptarecite 862
pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyāni karaṇāni purātanāḥ
nitamboromaṇḍalayostvāvṛttyā saptaviṃśatiḥ 863
ye vakṣaḥsvastikaṃ cātra kaṭīchinnaṃ ca manvate
pañcaviṃśatireva syādāvṛttāvapi tanmate 864
ityākṣiptarecitaḥ
svastikādyaṃ recitaṃ syādardharecitakaṃ tataḥ
vakṣaḥsvastikamunmattasaṃjñamākṣiptarecitam 865
ardhamattallikaraṇaṃ syādrecakanikuṭṭakam
bhujaṃgatrastapūrvaṃ ca recitaṃ nūpuraṃ tataḥ 866
vaiśākharecitaṃ kṛtvā bhujaṃgāñcitamācaret
daṇḍarecitakaṃ cakramaṇḍalaṃ vṛścikādimam 867
recitaṃ vṛścikādūrdhvaṃ vivṛttavinivṛttake
vivartitaṃ ca garuḍaplutakaṃ lalitaṃ bhavet 868
mayūrādyaṃ sarpitaṃ ca skhalitākhyaṃ prasarpitam
talasaṃghaṭṭitamatho vṛṣabhakrīḍitaṃ tataḥ 869
lelitaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatiryā karaṇānāmitīritā
tāṃ kṛtvā viṣamairbhāgaiścaturdhā dikcatuṣṭāye 870
parivṛttaprakāreṇa prayujyānte samācaret
uromaṇḍalakaṃ yatra kaṭīchinnaṃ sa recitaḥ 871
iti recitaḥ
nūpurākhyaṃ vivṛttaṃ ca nikuṭṭārdhanikuṭṭāke
ardharecitakādūrdhvaṃ syādrecakanikuṭṭakam 872
lalitākhyaṃ ca vaiśākharecitaṃ caturaṃ tataḥ
daṇḍarecitakādūrdhvaṃ bhavedvṛścikakuṭṭitam 873
nikuṭṭākaṃ pāśvapūrvaṃ saṃbhrāntodghaṭṭite tataḥ
uromaṇḍalakādūrdhvaṃ karihastasamāhvayam
kaṭīchinnaṃ saptadaśa bhavedardhanikuṭṭake 874
ityardhanikuṭṭakaḥ
latāvṛścikamādau syānninkuñcitamataḥ param
mattalli ca nitambākhyaṃ karaṇaṃ karihastakam 875
kaṭīchinnaṃ smṛtaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ vṛścikākpasṛte budhaiḥ
atra sthāne nitambasya bhramaraṃ kecidūcire 876
iti vṛścikāpasṛtaḥ
svastikaṃ vyaṃsitaṃ tu dviralātākhyordhvajānunī
nikuñcitārdhasūcyākhyavikṣiptodvṛttakānyatha 877
ākṣiptaṃ karihastaṃ syātkaṭīchinnamalātake
ekādaśa syuḥ karaṇānyekaṃ dvirvyaṃsite'dhikam 878
ityalātakaḥ
dakṣiṇāṅgena janitaṃ śakaṭāsyamalātakam 879
bhramaraṃ cātha gaṇḍasthaninkuṭṭitakarānvitam
karihastaṃ kaṭīchinnaṃ karaṇāni kramāditi 880
parāvṛtte ṣaḍuktāni śrīmatsoḍhalasūnunā
namanonnamanaṃ tajjñairaṅgasyoktaṃ nikuṭṭākam 881
iti parāvṛttaḥ
nitambaṃ karaṇaṃ kṛtvā kuryātskvastikarocitam 882
vikṣiptākṣiptakamatho latāvṛścikasaṃjñakam
unmattaṃ karihastaṃ ca bhujaṃgatrāsitaṃ tataḥ 883
ākṣiptakaṃ nitambaṃ ca nitambāntānyamūnyatha
nava bhramarakākhyeṇa parivṛttaṃ samācaret 884
digantaramukhe kṛtvā vyāvṛtya parayordiśoḥ
karihastakaṭīchinne kuryādādyadiśi sthitaḥ 885
yatra taṃ prāhurācāryāḥ parivṛttakarecitam
parivṛttavidhiścāyaṃ tyaktvā'ntyaṃ karaṇadvayam 886
sarveṣāmaṅgahārāṇāṃ śārṅgadevena sūriṇā
bhaṭṭābhinayaguptādimatajñena nigadyate 887
iti parivṛttakarecitaḥ
nūpurātkaraṇādūrdhvaṃ bhujaṃgāñcitamācaret
gṛdhrāvalīnakaṃ dve ca vikṣipte pṛthagaṅgaje 888
udvṛttamadhyera sūcyākhyaṃ nitambamatha vṛścikam
latāpūrvaṃ kaṭīchinnaṃ navabhiḥ karaṇairiti
udvṛttako dvirvikṣiptodvṛttākhyamadhikaṃ dvayam 889
ityudvṛttakaḥ
vikṣiptamañcitaṃ gaṇḍasūcī sarvāntimaṃ tataḥ
ardhasūci tato daṇḍapādaṃ vāmāṅgasādhitam 890
caturaṃ bhramaraṃ cātha nūpurākṣiptanāmake
ardhasvastikasaṃjñaṃ ca nitambaṃ karihastakam 891
uromaṇḍalasaṃjñaṃ ca kaṭīchinnamiti kramāt
karaṇaiḥ pañcadaśabhiḥ saṃbhrāntamabhaṇanbudhāḥ 892
iti saṃbhrāntaḥ
vaiśākharecitādūrdhvaṃ vṛścikaṃ dviridaṃ dvayam
nikuṭṭakābhidhādūrdhvaṃ kramāvāptalatākaram 893
kaṭīchinnaṃ caturthaṃ cettadā svastikarecitam
jñeyamādyadvayābhyāsādadhikaṃ karaṇadvayam 894
iti svastikarecitaḥ
mṛdaṅgairgomukhairbhambhābherīpaṭahaḍiṇḍimaiḥ 895
paṇavairdardurādyaiśca vādyaistālalayānugaiḥ
vardhamānāsāriteṣu pāṇikāgītakādiṣu 896
pūrvaraṅgasya cāṅgeṣu dhīrairutthāpanādiṣu
aṅgahārāḥ prayoktavyāḥ śreyaḥ paramabhīpsubhiḥ 897
viniyogo'ṅgahāreṣu karaṇānāmitīritaḥ
āhuḥ pṛthakprayoge'pi karaṇānāṃ mahatphalam 898
iti dvātriṃśadaṅgahāralakṣaṇam
recakānatha vakṣyāmaścaturo bharatoditān
pādayoḥ karayoḥ kaṭyā grīvāyāśca bhavanti te 899
pārṣṇyaṅguṣṭhāgrayorantarbahiśca satataṃ gatiḥ
namanonnamanopetā procyate pādarecakaḥ 900
parito bhramaṇaṃ tūrṇaṃ hastayorhaṃsapakṣayoḥ 901
yatparyāyeṇa racitaṃ sa bhavetkararecakaḥ
viralaprasṛtāṅguṣṭhāṅgulestiryagbhrameṇa ca 902
sarvato bhramaṇaṃ kaṭyāḥ kaṭirecakamūcire
grīvāyā vidhutabhrāntiḥ kathyate kaṇṭharecakaḥ
aṅgahārāṅgamapyete janayanti pṛkphalam 903
iti recakalakṣaṇam
vicitramaṅghrijaṅghorukaṭīkarma samaṃ kṛtam
cārī syātkaraṇe ṅīṣi careriñpratyayāntataḥ 904
hasto vā'bhinaye gatyāṃ caraṇo yo yadepsitaḥ
tatsaṃpattyucitā cārī kāryā taducitā parā 905
evamanyonyaniyamātsaiva vyāyāma ucyate
cārī ca karaṇaṃ khaṇḍo maṇḍalaṃ ceti tadbhidāḥ 906
tatraikapādaniṣpādyā cārī cāryeva kīrtitā
pādadvayena karaṇaṃ tannṛttakaraṇātpṛthak 907
karaṇaiḥ syāttribhiḥ khaṇḍo maṇḍalaṃ khaṇḍakaistribhiḥ
caturbhirvā kramāttāle tryasre ca caturasrake 908
bhaumī cā''kāśikītyeṣā dvedhā bhaumyo'tra ṣoḍaśa
samapādā sthitāvartā śakaṭāsyā ca vicyavā 909
adhyardhikā cāṣagatirelakākrīḍitā tathā
samosaritamattallī mattallyutsanditā'ḍḍitā 910
syanditā'pasyanditākhyā baddhā ca janitābhidhā
ūrūdvṛttetyatha brūmaḥ ṣoḍaśā''kāśikīrimāḥ 911
atikrāntā'pyapakrāntā pārśvakrāntā mṛgaplutā
ūrdhvajānuralātā ca sūcī nūpurapādikā 912
dolāpādā daṇḍapādā vidyuddhāntā bhramaryapi
bhujaṃgatrāsitā''kṣiptā viddhodvṛtteti kīrtitāḥ 913
bharatābhimatāścāryo dvātriṃśanmilitāstu tāḥ
deśīprasiddhāḥ santyanyāstāḥ saṃpratyabhidadhmahe 914
rathacakrā parāvṛttatalā nūpuraviddhikā 915
tiryaṅmukhā marālā ca karihastā kulīrikā
viśliṣṭā kātārā pārṣṇirecitā'pyūrutāḍitā 916
ūruveṇī talodvṛttā hariṇatrāsikā parā
ardhamaṇḍalikā tiryakkuñcitā ca madālasā 917
saṃcāritotkuñcitā ca stambhakrīḍanikā tataḥ
cārī laṅghitajaṅghākhyā sphuritā'pyapakuñcitā 918
api saṃghaṭṭitā khuttā svastikā taladarśinī
purāṭyardhapurāṭī ca sarikā sphurikā tataḥ 919
nikuṭṭakā latākṣepā'pyuḍuskhalitikā parā
samaskhalitikā bhaumyaḥ pañcatriṃśaditīritāḥ 920
vidyuddhāntā puraḥkṣepā vikṣepā hariṇaplutā
apakṣepā ca ḍamarī daṇḍapādāṅghritāḍitā 921
jaṅghālaṅghanikā'lātā jaṅghāvartā ca veṣṭanam
udveṣṭanamathotkṣepaḥ pṛṣṭhotkṣepaśca sūcikā 922
viddhā prāvṛttamullola ityatraikonaviṃśatiḥ
vyomagāḥ syurubhayāstu catuṣpañcāśadūcire 923
mārgadeśīgatācāryaḥ ṣaḍaśītirimā matāḥ
lakṣaṇāni kramāttāsāṃ cārīṇāmabhidadhmahe 924
nirantarau samanakhau pādau kṛtvā sthito yadā 925
sthānena samapādena samapādā tadoditā
pracārayogyatāmātrāccārī sthāne'pyasau matā 926
iti samapādā
caraṇāntarapārśvaṃ cedgatvā'gratalasaṃcaraḥ
antarjānuḥ svastikatvaṃ prāptaḥ pādastathetaraḥ
viśliṣya pārśvaṃ saṃnītaḥ sthitāvartā tadocyate 927
iti sthitāvartā
yatra dhṛtvā pūrvakāyaṃ pādo'gratalasaṃcaraḥ 928
prasāryodvāhitamuraḥ śakaṭāsyā tadā bhavet
āsyaṃ kṣepyaṃ hi śakaṭaṃ cārīmenāmupāśritaiḥ 929
iti śakaṭāsyā
vicyutya samapādāyāḥ pādau yadi talāgrataḥ
nikuṭṭayetāṃ dharaṇīmucyate vicyavā tadā 930
iti vicyavā
dakṣiṇāṅghreḥ pārṣṇideśe vāmaḥ pādo nidhīyate
dakṣiṇastvapasṛtya sve pārśve tryasratayā sthitaḥ 931
sārdhatālāntaratvena dakṣiṇo'pyevameva cet
vāmapārṣṇau sa ca tryasro bhavedadhyardhikā tadā 932
ityadhyardhikā
tālamātraṃ puraḥ sṛtvā dvitālaṃ pṛṣṭhato gate
dakṣiṇe'ṅghrau samaṃ pādau kiṃcidutplutipūrvakam 933
upasṛtyāpasarpetāmapasṛtyopasarpataḥ
yatra satrāsagatyādau saiṣā cāṣagatirmatā 934
iti cāṣagatiḥ
kiṃcidutplutya caraṇau yatrāgratalasaṃcarau
paryāyaśo nipatataḥ sailakākrīḍitā matā 935
ityelakākrīḍitā
abhyantare'ṅghreranyasya pāde'gratalasaṃcare
sajaṅghāsvastike'thānyapāde'gratalasaṃcare 936
kṛte'ṅghryorghūrṇatoryatrāpasṛtiścopasarpaṇam
samosaritamattallī sā bhavenmadhyame made 937
iti samosaritamattallī
bhūmiśliṣṭākhilatalau jaṅghāsvastikasaṃyutau 938
ardhatryasrau yadā pādau ghūrṇantau vopasarpataḥ
yadvā'pasarpataḥ sā syānmattallī taruṇe made 939
iti mattallī
kaniṣṭhāṅgulibhāgenāṅguṣṭhabhāgena ca kramāt
recakasyānukāreṇa śanairaṅghrirgatāgatam 940
kurute yatra sā cārī budhairutsanditoditā
recitaṃ nṛttahastaṃ ca kecidatra pracakṣate 941
ityutsanditā
samāṅghreragrapṛṣṭhābhyāmanyo'gratalasaṃcaraḥ
nighṛṣṭaścaraṇo yatra kramāttāmaḍḍitāṃ viduḥ 942
ityaḍḍitā
niṣaṇṇoruḥ samo vāmo dakṣiṇo'ṅghriḥ prasāritaḥ
pañcatālāntaraṃ tiryagyasyāṃ sā syanditā matā 943
iti syanditā
aṅghricāraviparyāsātsaivāpasyanditā matā 944
ityapasyanditā
ūrudvayasya valanaṃ jaṅghāsvastikasaṃyutam
bhaṅktvā vā svastikaṃ pādatalāgre maṇḍalabhramam
kṛtvā pārśvaṃ gate svaṃ svaṃ yatra baddheti sā matā 945
iti baddhā
cārī sā janitā yasyāṃ pādo'gratalasaṃcaraḥ 946
muṣṭirvakṣasi hasto'nyo yathāśobhaṃ pravartate
aṅghrikriyā pradhānaṃ syāditikartavyatetarā 947
iti janitā
pārṣṇiḥ pādasya cedagratalasaṃcarasaṃjñitaḥ
anyāṅghripṛṣṭhābhimukhī viparyāso'thavā bhavet 948
abhyanyajaṅghaṃ valitā jaṅghā cennatajānukā
ūrūdvṛttā tadā cārī lajjerṣyādau niyujyate 949
ityūrūdvṛttā
niyuddhe cāṅgahāreṣu nāṭye caitāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ
anādivedamūlena nāṭyavedena kīrtitāḥ 950
iti ṣoḍaśa bhaumacāryaḥ
athā''kāśikyaḥ
gulphakṣetre'nyapādasyodvṛtyāṅghriṃ kuñcitaṃ puraḥ
kiṃcitprasārya cā''kṣipya yathāprakṛti lokavat 951
catustālādyantareṇa tato'greṇa nipātayet
yatra cārīmatikrāntāṃ niḥśaṅkastāmakīrtayat 952
ityatikrāntā
baddhāṃ vidhāya cārīṃ cedudvṛtyāṅghriṃ ca kuñcitam
pārśve vinikṣipeccārīmapakrāntāṃ tadā''diśet 953
ityapakrāntā
nītvopari svapārśvena kuñcitaṃ caraṇaṃ tataḥ
pārṣṇyā cetpātayedbhūmau pārśvakrāntā tadocyate 954
sā pārśvadaṇḍapādeti prasiddhā lokavartmani 955
anyorukṣetraparyantamutkṣipya caraṇaṃ tataḥ
udghaṭṭitaṃ bhuvi nyasyedasyāmityapare jaguḥ 956
iti pārśvakrāntā
kuñcitaṃ pādamutkṣipyotplutya bhūmau nipātya tam
anyāñcitāṅghrijaṅghāṃ ca paścāddeśe kṣipedyadā
tadā mṛgaplutā jñeyā sā vidūṣakakartṛkā 957
iti mṛgaplutā
kuñcitotkṣiptapādasya jānustanasamaṃ yadā
nyasya stabdhīkṛto'nyo'ṅghrirūrdhvajānustadā bhavet 958
ityūrdhvajānuḥ
pṛṣṭhaprasāritāṅghreścedanyorvabhimukhaṃ talam
kṛtvā pārṣṇiḥ svapārśve kṣmānyastā'lātā tadoditā 959
ityalātā
utkṣipya kuñcitaṃ pādaṃ jaṅghāmasya prasārya ca 960
jānvantāṃ voruparyantāmagrayoge nataṃ bhuvi
caraṇaṃ pātayedyasyāṃ sā sūcīti nigadyate 961
iti sūcī
paścānnītvā'ñcitaṃ pādaṃ tasya pārṣṇyā sphijaṃ spṛśet
taṃ tato'ñcitajaṅghaṃ ca bhūmāvagratalena cet
vipātayettadā cārī proktā nūpurapādikā 962
iti nūpurapādikā
pādaṃ kuñcitamuddhṛtya dolayitvā ca pārśvayoḥ
nyasyetpārṣṇyā svapārśve ceddolāpādā tadocyate 963
iti dolāpādā
nūpuraścaraṇo'nyasya pārṣṇideśe vidhāya cet 964
svadehakṣetrābhimukhaṃ jānvagratvena vegataḥ
agre prasāryate cārī daṇḍapādā tadoditā 965
iti daṇḍapādā
pṛṣṭhato valitaḥ spṛṣṭvā śiro bhrāntvā ca sarvataḥ
prasṛtaścaraṇo yatra vidyuddhāntā bhavedasau 966
iti vidyuddhāntā atikrāntāgataṃ pādaṃ tryasrasyorovivartanam
kṛtvā pādāntaratalabhrameṇa bhrāmyate tanuḥ
yatra sā bhramarī cārī śārṅgadevena kīrtitā 967
iti bhramarī
anyorumūlakṣetrāntaṃ pādamutkṣipya kuñcitam
nitambābhimukhī pārṣṇiḥ kuryājjānu svapārśvagam 968
yatrottānaṃ pādatalaṃ kaṭījānuvivartanāt
bhujaṃgatrāsitā sā syādgujaṃgatrāsamūcikā 969
iti bhujaṃgatrāsitā
tālatrayāntarotkṣiptaṃ kuñcitaṃ pādamānayet 970
pārśvāntaraṃ tato jaṅghāṃ svastikīkṛtya pātayet
dharaṇyāṃ pārṣṇibhāgena yatrā''kṣiptāmamūṃ viduḥ 971
ityākṣiptā
viśliṣṭajaṅghayoḥ kṛtvā svastikaṃ tasya kuñcitaḥ
pādaḥ prasārito vakraḥ svapāśvastho'tha pātyate
pārṣṇyā pārṣṇyantarakṣetre yatrā''viddhā bhavatyasau 972
ityāviddhā
āviddhāpādamanyorupradeśasthāsnupārṣṇikam 973
kṛtvotplutya bhramarakaṃ dattvā yatra nipātayet
tathā'nyaṃ pādamuddhṛtya sodvṛttāśeṣarūpiṇī 974
ityudvṛttā
lalitāṅgakriyāsādhyāścāryo yuddhaniyuddhayoḥ
nṛtte nāṭye gatau caitāḥ prayoktavyā manīṣibhiḥ 975
pradhānaṃ yo yadā yatra hastaḥ pādo'thavā bhavet
so'gre tadanugo'nyaḥ syātsāmye tu samakālatā 976
yataḥ pādastato hasto yato hastastatastrikaḥ
caraṇānucarāṇyāhuraṅgopāṅgāni sūrayaḥ 977
evaṃ cārīpradhānatve syādaṅgaviniyojanam
prādhānye hastakānāṃ tu hastamaṅgānyupāsate 978
cāraṃ cāraṃ yathā cāryāṃ caraṇaḥ śrayate mahīm
kāraṃ kāraṃ karastadvadviśrāmyati kaṭītaṭe 979
ardhacandraḥ karo nāṭye saṃśrayeta kaṭītaṭam
pakṣavañcitako nṛtye pakṣapradyotako'thavā 980
iti dvātriṃśaccārīlakṣaṇam
caturasraṃ samāśritya saṃlagnau cetprasarpataḥ
purataḥ pṛṣṭhato vā'ṅghrī rathacakrā tadocyate 981
iti rathacakrā
paścāduttānitatalaścaraṇaḥ prasṛto bahiḥ
yasyāṃ parāvṛttatalā śārṅgadevena soditā 982
iti parāvṛttatalā
sthitvā svastikabandhena pārṣṇyoḥ prapadayostathā
recitau caraṇau yatra soktā nūpuraviddhikā 983
iti nūpuraviddhā
vardhamāne sthitau sthāne vāmadakṣiṇato yadā
saratodrutamānena pādau tiryaṅmukhā tadā 984
iti tiryaṅmukhā
nandyāvartasthitau pādau pārṣṇipādāgrarecitau
puraḥ prasāritau yasyāṃ sā marālā'bhidhīyate 985
iti marālā
saṃhatasthānake sthitvā pārśvābhyāṃ gharṣataḥ kṣitim
caraṇau yatra sā cārī karihastā prakīrtitā 986
iti karihastā
nandyāvartasthayoraṅghryostiryaksṛtvā kulīrikā
iti kulīrikā
pārṣṇividdhasthitau pādau cedviśliṣyopasarpataḥ
yadvā'pasarpataḥ proktā sā viśliṣṭābhidhā budhaiḥ 987
iti viśliṣṭā
nandyāvartasthapādābhyāṃ paścātsṛtvā tu kātarā
iti kātarā
pārṣṇipārśvagate sthāne sthitvā cedrecitā kṛtā
pārṣṇistadoditā cārī niṣṇātaiḥ pārṣṇirecitā 988
iti pārṣṇirecitā
ekapāde sthitaḥ sthāne bhūsthena caraṇena cet
ūruṃ tāḍayati proktā tadā cāryūrutāḍitā 989
ityūrutāḍitā
ūrusthasvastikākārāvaṅghrī saṃgharṣato bhuvam
pārśvābhyāṃ yatra tāmāhurūruveṇīṃ manīṣiṇaḥ 990
ityūruveṇī
aṅgulīpṛṣṭhabhāgena purataḥ sarato drutam
prapade yatra sā cārī talodvṛttā matā satām 991
iti talodvṛttā
kuñcite svastikīkṛtya valitānte tale yadā
aṅghryorutplutya nipateddhariṇatrāsikā tadā 992
iti hariṇatrāsikā
bhūmighṛṣṭyā bahirnītāvāvartete śanaiḥ kramāt
caraṇau yatra tāmāhurardhamaṇḍalikāṃ budhāḥ 993
ityardhamaṇḍalikā
yatrā''kuñcya tiraścīnaṃ caraṇaṃ prakṣipenmuhuḥ
tāṃ tiryakkuñcitāṃ cārīmāha śrīkaraṇeśvaraḥ 994
iti tiryakkuñcitā
itastataśca caraṇau sthāpayeta visaṃsthulau
mattavadyatra tāmāhuścārīṃ dhīrā madālasām 995
iti madālasā
ākuñcito'ṅghrirutkṣipyotkṣipyānyenāṅghriṇā yadā
yujyate'nyastu saṃsarpettiryaksaṃcāritā tadā 996
iti tiryaksaṃcāritā
utkṣipya kuñcitau pādau nyasyedekaikamagrataḥ
yasyāṃ sotkuñcitā cārī proktā soḍhalasūnunā 997
ityutkuñcitā
tiryakprasāritaikāṅghri pārśvamanyattalena cet
muhuḥ saṃyojayeduktā stambhakrīḍanikā tadā 998
iti stambhakrīḍanikā
sthāne'ṅghriḥ khaṇḍasūcyākhye tiṣṭhannākṛṣya vegataḥ
laṅghyate'nyāṅghriṇā yatra soktā laṅghitajaṅghikā 999
iti laṅghitajaṅghikā
sphuritā'gre sṛtau vegādbhūspṛśoḥ pādapārśvayoḥ
iti sphuritā
kramādākuñcitāṅghribhyāṃ paścādgatyā'pakuñcitā 1000
ityapakuñcitā
sthāne viṣamasūcyākhye sthitvotplutya patanbhuvi
aṅghrī saṃghaṭṭayedyatra soktā saṃghaṭṭitābhidhā 1001
iti saṃghaṭṭitā
dharaṇyāṃ caraṇāgreṇa ghātaḥ khuttā nigadyate
iti khuttā
caraṇaḥ svastikākārakāritaḥ svastikā bhavet 1002
iti svastikā
yatrāṅghrī saṃhatasthāne sthitvā tiryakpṛthakkṛtau
spṛśato bāhyapārśvābhyāṃ bhuvaṃ sā taladarśinī 1003
iti taladarśinī
nikuṭṭanaṃ mitho'ṅghribhyāmuddhatābhyāṃ purāṭikā
iti purāṭikā
uddhṛtena nikuṭṭena caraṇena nikuṭṭanam
uddhṛtasyānyapādasya yatra sā'rdhapurāṭikā 1004
ityardhapurāṭikā
caraṇo'gre saratyeko yatra sā sarikā matā 1005
iti sarikā
puraḥsaraṇamaṅghribhyāṃ samābhyāṃ sphurikā bhavet
iti sphurikā
sthitistu caraṇāgreṇa kuñcitena nikuṭṭikā 1006
iti nikuṭṭikā
paścānnyasya purastaṃ ca prasārya caraṇaṃ yadi
nikuṭṭayedbhuvaṃ tena latākṣepastadoditaḥ 1007
iti latākṣepaḥ
skhalite caraṇe tiryagaḍḍaskhalitikā bhavet 1008
ityaḍḍaskhalitikā
purataḥ pṛṣṭhatastiryakcaraṇau yugapadyadā
skhalataḥ procyate cārī samaskhalitikā tadā 1009
iti samaskhalitikā
yasyāṃ puro'ṅghrimutkṣipya lalāṭasyopari drutam
bhrāmayitvā bhuvi nyasyedvidyuddhāntāmamūṃ viduḥ 1010
iti vidyuddhāntā
aṅghriṃ kuñcitamutkṣipya puro vistārya vegataḥ
vinyasedavanau yatra puraḥkṣepā bhavedasau 1011
iti puraḥkṣepā
puro gaganabhāge cetprasārya caraṇaṃ muhuḥ
ākuñcayettadā cārī vikṣepā śārṅgiṇoditā 1012
iti vikṣepā
natāṅghrerutplutyābhīkṣṇaṃ nipātāddhariṇaplutā 1013
iti hariṇaplutā
bāhyapārśvena yatroroḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ spṛṣṭvetaratpadam
nitambanikaṭaṃ yāti sā'pakṣepā prakīrtitā 1014
ityapakṣepā
ḍamaryākuñcitasyāṅghrervāmadakṣiṇato bhramāt
iti ḍamarī
yatra svastikamāvartya protkṣipettiryagūrdhvataḥ
caraṇau daṇḍapādā sā cārī niḥśaṅkakīrtitā 1015
iti daṇḍapādā
vistāryāṅghrī plutaṃ kṛtvā gamanaṃ tāḍayenmitathaḥ
yadā pādataladvaṃdvaṃ tadā cāryaṅghritāḍitā 1016
ityaṅghritāḍitā
kiṃcidākuñcitaṃ pādamaṅghriṇā'nyena laṅghayet
gagane cettadā cārī jaṅghālaṅghanikā matā 1017
iti jaṅghālaṅghanikā
alātāṅghrau pṛṣṭhagate śīghramanyāṅghrilaṅghite 1018
ityalātā
talamantarbhramasyāṅghrerjānupṛṣṭhe kṣipedyadi
bahirbhramasya tatpārśve jaṅghāvartā tadā bhaved 1019
iti jaṅghāvartā
aṅghriṇaikena cedanyaṃ veṣṭayedveṣṭānaṃ tadā
tadeva valanaṃ kecidavadannṛttakovidāḥ 1020
iti veṣṭanam
udveṣṭānaṃ veṣṭayitvā pṛṣṭhato'ṅghrau prasārite 1021
ityudveṣṭānam
ākuñcitasya pādasya purataḥ pṛṣṭhatastathā
utkṣepo jānuparyantamutkṣepaḥ kathyate budhaiḥ 1022
ityutkṣepaḥ
sa cetpṛṣṭhata eva syātpṛṣṭhotkṣepamamuṃ viduḥ
iti pṛṣṭhotkṣepaḥ
ūrau pārśvena vinyasya padaṃ yatra prasārayet
tīkṣṇāgrīkṛtya tāmāha sūcīṃ śrīkaṇṭhavallabhaḥ 1023
iti sūcī
pādayoḥ svastike'syaikaḥ kiṃcidāndolitaḥ puraḥ
kuñcitaścaraṇo yatra sā viddhā bodhitā budhaiḥ 1024
iti viddhā
lalitā valitā mūrtirudvṛttaścaraṇo bhavet
yatra tatprāvṛtaṃ jñeyaṃ makaradhvajajīvanam 1025
iti prāvṛtam
ullolaḥ syāccaraṇayoḥ krameṇollālanāddivi 1026
ityullolaḥ
sthānaṃ sthitirgatiścārī sthānamādyantayorgateḥ
sthitvā gacchati gatvā ca tiṣṭhatītyavinākṛtāḥ
cāryaḥ sthānena tābhyo'to'nantaraṃ sthānamucyate 1027
saṃniveśaviśeṣo'ṅge niścalaḥ sthānamucyate 1028
vaiṣṇavaṃ samapādaṃ ca vaiśākhaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tathā
ālīḍhapratyāliḍhe ca sthānānīti nareṣu ṣaṭ 1029
atha sthānāni vakṣyāmastrīṇi strīṇāṃ munermatāt
āyatākhyāvahitthāśvakrāntānyanyaccatuṣṭayam 1030
athāpi ceti vadatā muninā sūcitaṃ bruve
gatāgataṃ ca valitaṃ moṭitaṃ vinivartitam 1031
atha deśīsthānakānāṃ trayoviṃśatirucyate
svastikaṃ vardhamānākhyaṃ nandyāvartaṃ ca saṃhatam 1032
samapādaṃ caikapādaṃ pṛṣṭhottānatalaṃ tathā
caturasraṃ pāṣṇividdhaṃ pārṣṇipārśvagataṃ tathā 1033
ekapārśvagataṃ ca syādekajānunatābhidham
parāvṛttāhvayaṃ sūci samādi viṣamādyapi 1034
khaṇḍasūci tato brāhmaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ śaivagāruḍe
kūrmāsanaṃ nāgabandhaṃ vṛṣabhāsanamityapi 1035
svasthaṃ madālasaṃ krāntaṃ syādviṣkambhitamutkaṭam
srastālasaṃ jānugataṃ muktajānu vimuktakam 1036
upaviṣṭasthānakāni naveti bharato'bhyadhāt
samamākuñcitaṃ sthānaṃ prasāritavivartite 1037
udvāhitaṃ nataṃ ceti suptasthānāni santi ṣaṭ
iti sthānāni ṣaṭpuṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ saptāparāṇyapi 1038
trayoviṃśatiruktāni nava sthānāni cā'sane
suptasthānāni ṣaṭtāni sarvāṇi militāni tu 1039
ekapañcāśadācaṣṭa niḥśaṅkaḥ karaṇāgraṇīḥ
sarveṣāṃ vacmi lakṣmaiṣāmidānīṃ tadvidāṃ matam 1040
ekaḥ samasthitaḥ pādastryasraḥ pakṣasthito'paraḥ 1041
sārdhadvitālāntarālo jaṅghā kiṃcinnatā bhavet
sauṣṭhavaṃ yatra tajjñeyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ viṣṇudaivatam 1042
prakṛtisthasya saṃlāpenānākāryāntarānvite
prayojyaṃ tanmuniḥ prāha nṛbhiruttamamadhyamaiḥ 1043
viṣṇuveṣadhareṇaiva naṭenetyapare jaguḥ
sūtradhārādinā nāṭyasthitikartreti cāpare 1044
pakṣasthito'sau caraṇo yaḥ pārśvābhimukhāṅguliḥ
tryasraḥ sa eva kiṃciccetpurodeśābhimukhyabhāk 1045
aṅguṣṭhamadhyamāṅgulyau ye hastasya prasārite
tadagrayorantarālaṃ tālamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ 1046
kaṭī jānusamā yatra kūrparāṃsaśiraḥsamam
uraḥ samunnataṃ sannaṃ gātraṃ tatsauṣṭhavaṃ bhavet 1047
sannaṃ svasthānaviśrāntaṃ niṣaṇṇaṃ tvacalasthiti 1048
calapādamanatyuccamacañcalamakubjakam 1049
sauṣṭhave'ṅgaṃ bhavettacca kāryamuttamamadhyamaiḥ
etacca vaiṣṇavaṃ sthānaṃ caturasrasya jīvanam 1050
vaiṣṇavaṃ sthānakaṃ yatra kaṭīnābhicarau karau
pṛthaksamunnataṃ vakṣaścaturasraṃ taducyate 1051
iti vaiṣṇavam
pradhānaṃ sauṣṭhavaṃ pādāvekatālāntarau samau 1052
yatra tatsamapādaṃ syāccaturānanadaivatam
etacca vipradattāśīḥsvīkāre'tha nirīkṣaṇe 1053
madhyamānāṃ vihaṃgānāṃ kanyāvarakutūhale
liṅgivrativimānasthasyandanastheṣu ceṣyate 1054
iti samapādam
bhūmerūrdhvaniṣaṇṇau cetsārdhatālatrayāntare
nabhūsyūrū caraṇayostāvadevāntaraṃ bhuvi 1055
tryasrapakṣasthayoḥ sthānaṃ vaiśākhaṃ kathitaṃ tadā
viśākhadaivataṃ tacca sthūlapakṣīkṣaṇe bhavet
yaddhādau preraṇe'śvānāṃ vegādāne ca vāhane 1056
iti vaiśākham
pakṣasthau caraṇau tryasrāvekatālāntarau bhuvi
kaṭījānusamau vyomni sārdhatāladvayāntare 1057
ūrū yatra niṣaṇṇau tanmaṇḍalaṃ śakradaivatam
dhanurvajrādiśastrāṇāṃ prayoge gajavāhane 1058
vīkṣaṇe garuḍādīnāmidaṃ munirupādiśat
catustālāntarau pādau maṇḍale'nye pracakṣate 1059
iti maṇḍalam
vāmo yatra niṣaṇṇorurambare pūrvamānataḥ
dakṣiṇaścaraṇaścāgre pañcatālaṃ prasāritaḥ 1060
tryasrau dvāvapi tadvidyādālīḍhaṃ rudradaivatam
īrṣyākrodhakṛto jalpaḥ kāryastenottarottaraḥ 1061
vīraraudrakṛtaṃ mallasaṃgharṣāsphoṭanādikam
tathā saṃdhāya śastrāṇi pratyālīḍhaṃ samāśrayet 1062
ityālīḍham
ālīḍhāṅgaviparyāsātpratyālīḍhamudīritam
ālīḍhakṛtasaṃdhānaśastrametena mokṣayet
rudraṃ ca daivataṃ tatra bhāṣate śaṃbhuvallabhaḥ 1063
iti pratyālīḍham
eṣāmādyāni catvāri dṛśyante nāṭyanṛttayoḥ
prayogastvantyayostajjñairiṣṭo nāṭyaikagocaraḥ 1064
anye pañcavidhe'pyāhurnartane sthānakāni ṣaṭ 1065
iti ṣaṭpuruṣasthānakāni
ghāmastālāntarastryasro dakṣiṇaścaraṇaḥ samaḥ
prasannaṃ vadanaṃ vakṣaḥ samunnatamanunnatā 1066
kaṭīnitambago hasto dakṣiṇo'nyo latākaraḥ
yatrā''yataṃ tadākhyātaṃ kamalā cātra devatā 1067
sthānaṃ cikīrṣitāsu syātkṣatāsu ca gatiṣvidam
ābhāṣaṇe ca kartavye sakhīpriyatamādibhiḥ 1068
raṅgāvataraṇārambhe puṣpāñjalivisarjane
amarṣe pratiṣedhe ca kāryaṃ mānāvalambane
garvagāmbhīryamaune syādāvāhanavisarjane 1970
pūrvaraṅge strībhireva prayojyaṃ kecidūcire
naro nāryo'thavā kuryuḥ praveśe sthānakaṃ striyāḥ 1071
sthānaṃ yathābhineyaṃ syātpraviṣṭeṣviti cāpare
āyatānantaraṃ kāryā raṅgāvataraṇādayaḥ 1072
hastapādapracārastu teṣu jñeyo yathocitam
bhaṭṭābhinavaguptasya matametadudīritam 1073
ityāyatam
avahitthaṃ tadeva syādviparyāsena pādayoḥ
cintāvitarkayostoṣe saṃlāpe ca nisargaje 1074
vismaye bhūrisaubhāgyagarvaje svāṅgavīkṣaṇe
līlāvilāsalāvaṇyavaramārgavilokane 1075
kāryaṃ daurgamidaṃ sthānamavahitthasya sūcakam 1076
ityavahittham
samasthaikasya pādasya pārṣṇideśaśato'paraḥ
sūcīpādaḥ svapārśve vā samastālāntare sthitaḥ 1077
yatrāśvārohaṇārambhe tadaśvakrāntamucyate
skhalite vigaladvastradhāraṇe gopyagopane 1078
prasūnastabakādāne taruśākhāvalambane
naisargike ca saṃlāpe vibhrame lalite tathā
prayoktavyamidaṃ sthānaṃ bhāratī cātra devatā 1079
ityaśvakrāntam
gatyunmukhī ca yatraikaṃ padamuddhṛtya nartakī
udāste tadgatisthityornirodhātsyādgatāgatam 1080
iti gatāgatam
śarīramīṣadvalitaṃ pādo valitadigbhavaḥ
kaniṣṭhāśliṣṭabhūranyo bhūlagnāṅguṣṭhako yadā
tadā valitamākhyātaṃ sābhilāṣavilokane 1081
iti valitam
ekaḥ samo'ṅghriranyastu kuñcito'rdhatalāṅguliḥ
agre yadordhvagau hastau karkaṭau moṭitaṃ tadā
kāmāvasthāsu sarvāsu viniyogo'sya kīrtitaḥ 1082
iti moṭitam
pṛṣṭhato'ṅgaparāvṛttyā tadeva vinivartitam 1083
iti vinivartitam
svastiko haṃsakasthāne caraṇau kuñcitau yadā
mithaḥ śliṣṭakaniṣṭhau ca tadāhuḥ svastikaṃ budhāḥ 1084
iti svastikam
vardhamāne tu caraṇau tiryañcau pārṣṇisaṃgatau 1085
iti vardhamānam
asyaiva ceccaraṇayorantaraṃ syātṣaḍaṅgulam
vitastimātramathavā nandyāvartaṃ tadoditam 1086
iti nandyāvartam
dehaḥ svābhāviko'ṅguṣṭhau pādayoścenmitho yutau
gulphau ca saṃhataṃ tatsyātpuṣpāñjalivisarjane 1087
iti saṃhatam
vapuḥ svābhāvikaṃ pādau vitastyantaritāvṛjū
yatra tatsamapādākhyaṃ śārṅgadevaḥ samādiśat 1088
iti samapādam
samasyāṅghrerjānumūrdhvaṃ bāhyapārśve yadītaraḥ
bāhyapārśvena lagno'ṅghrirekapādaṃ tadocyate 1089
ityekapādam
bhūlagnāṅgulipṛṣṭho'ṅghriḥ paścādeko'paraḥ puraḥ
samo yatra tadādiṣṭaṃ pṛṣṭhottānatalaṃ budhaiḥ 1090
iti pṛṣṭhottānatalam
nandyāvartasya cedaṅghryorbhavedaṣṭādaśāṅgulam
antaraṃ caturaiḥ sthānaṃ caturasraṃ tadoditam 1091
iti caturasram
pārṣṇiraṅguṣṭhasaṃśliṣṭā pārṣṇividdhe vidhīyate
iti pārṣṇividdham
pārṣṇipārśvagate pārṣṇirantaḥ pārśvāntarasthitaḥ 1092
iti pārṣṇipārśvagatam
samasyāṅghreḥ puraḥ kiṃcidaparaścaraṇo yadā
syādbāhyapārśvagastiryagekapārśvagataṃ tadā 1093
ityekapārśvagatam
ekaḥ samo'ṅghriranyaścedantare caturaṅgule
tiryakkuñcitajānuḥ syādekajānunataṃ tadā 1094
ityekajānunatam
parāvṛtte same syātāṃ pārṣṇyā'ṅguṣṭhakaniṣṭhike
iti parāvṛttam
pārṣṇijaṅghorusaṃlagnadharaṇī caraṇau yadā
tiryakprasāritau syātāṃ samasūci tadocyate 1095
iti samasūci
sūcīpādau pṛthagyatra puraḥ paścātprasāritau 1096
yugapadgaditaṃ tajjñaiḥ sthānaṃ viṣamasūci tat
bhūlagnajānugulphau tau caraṇau kecidūcire 1097
iti viṣamasūci
eko'ṅghriḥ kuñcito'nyastu bhūsaṃlagnorupārṣṇikaḥ
tiryakprasārito yatra khaṇḍasūci tadocyate 1098
iti khaṇḍasūci
ekaḥ samo'ṅghriranyaścetkuñcitīkṛtya pṛṣṭhataḥ
jānusaṃdhisamatvenotkṣipto brāhmaṃ taducyate 1099
iti brāhmam
samamekaṃ vidhāyāṅghrimanyaścetkuñcito manāk
puraḥ prasāritastiryakpadaṃ syādvaiṣṇavaṃ tadā 1100
iti vaiṣṇavam
aṅghreḥ samasya vāmasya jānuśīrṣasamo'paraḥ
uddhṛtaḥ kuñcitākāro yatra śaivaṃ taducyate 1101
iti śaivam
ākuñcitaḥ puro vāmaḥ paścādanyastu jānunā
bhuvaṃ gatau yadā pādau gāruḍaṃ gaditaṃ tadā 1102
iti gāruḍam
dakṣiṇaścaraṇo jānubāhyagulphamilatkṣitiḥ
vāmaḥ samo bhavedyatra kūrmāsanamado viduḥ 1103
iti kūrmāsanam
yadopaviśya vāmoroḥ pṛṣṭhe nyasyati dakṣiṇām
jaṅghāṃ tadā nāgabandhamabhyadhātkaraṇāgraṇīḥ 1104
iti nāgabandham
viyute saṃyute vā cejjānunī saṃsthite kṣitau
vṛṣabhāsanamākhyātaṃ sauṣṭhavādhiṣṭhitaṃ tadā 1105
iti vṛṣabhāsanam
