iti sabhāsaṃniveśaḥ
rasapradhānamicchanti tauryatrikamidaṃ vidaḥ
tatsāmānyaviśeṣābhyāmadhunā'bhidadhe rasam 1362
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca naṭasthairvyabhicāribhiḥ
janitā''tmaparāmitramitrādyāśrayatāṃ vinā 1363
avasthādeśakālādibhedasaṃbhedavarjitam
kevalaṃ ratihāsādisthāyirūpaṃ pragṛhṇatī 1364
ato nirantarāyatvātparāṃ viśrāntimāśritā
pratibhānubhavasmṛtyādyanyabodhavilakṣaṇā 1365
brahmasaṃvidvisadṛśī nānāratyādisaṃgamāt
sukharūpā svasaṃvedyā saṃvidāsvādanābhidhā
rasaḥ syādathavā sthāyī rasastadgocarībhavan 1266
dadhyādivyañjanaiścaiva haridrādibhirauṣadhaiḥ
madhurādirasopetairyadvaddravyairguḍādibhiḥ 1367
yuktaiḥ pākaviśeṣeṇa ṣāḍavākhyo'paro rasaḥ
utpādyate vibhāvādyaiḥ prayogeṇa tathā rasaḥ 1368
śṛṅgārahāsyau karuṇo raudro vīro bhayānakaḥ
bībhatsaścādbhutaḥ śānto navadheti raso mataḥ 1369
śāntasya śamasādhyatvānnaṭe ca tadasaṃbhavāt
aṣṭāveva rasā nāṭyeṣviti kecidacūcudan 1370
tadacodyaṃ yataḥ kiṃcinna rasaṃ svadate naṭaḥ
sāmājikāstu lihate rasānpātraṃ naṭo mataḥ 1371
te śuddhahṛdayāḥ śāntaṃ svavibhāvavibhāvitam
ekāgracetasaḥ santo'nubhavantīti yujyate 1372
ratihāsaśucaḥ krodhotsāhau bhayajugupsane
vismayaścātha nirvedaḥ sthāyibhāvā navetyamī 1373
jugupsāṃ sthāyibhāvaṃ tu śāntaṃ kecidbabhāṣire
utsāhamāhuranye'nye śamaṃ sarvānpare viduḥ 1374
uddiśya sthāyinaḥ prāpte samaye vyabhicāriṇām
amaṅgalamapi brūte pūrvaṃ nirvedameva yat 1375
munirmene'sya tannūnaṃ sthāyitāvyabhicārite
pūrvāparānvayo hyasya madhyasthasyānuṣaṅgataḥ 1376
sthāpī syādviṣayeṣveṣa tattvajñānodbhavo yadi
iṣṭāniṣṭaviyogāptikṛtastu vyabhicāryasau 1377
nirvedaglāniśaṅkaugryadainyāsūyādamaśramāḥ
cintā dhṛtiḥ smṛtirvrīḍāmohālasyāni cāpalam 1378
harṣāmarṣaviṣādāścāpasmāro jaḍatā tathā
vitarkasuptamautsukyamavahitthaṃ tathā matiḥ 1379
vibodho vyādhirunmādo garvāvegau mṛtistathā
trāso nidrā trayastriṃśaditi syurvyabhicāriṇaḥ 1380
stambhaḥ svedo'tha romāñcaḥ svarabhedo'tha vepathuḥ
vaivarṇyamaśru pralayo'ṣṭau bhāvāḥ sāttvikā iti 1381
vibhāvādyairanucitaḥ sthānatvādvikṛtiṃ gataiḥ
śṛṅgārādirasābhāsāḥ kṛtā hāsyasya hetavaḥ 1382
phalaṃ bandhuvadho raudre vibhāvaḥ karuṇe ca saḥ
jagadustadvido raudramataḥ karuṇakāraṇam 1383
raudraṃ bhayānake hetumata eva pracakṣate
śṛṅgārādvipralambhākhyātkaruṇotpattiriṣyate
bhayānake'dbhute hetuṃ vīraṃ dhīrā babhāṣire 1384
vidūṣakasya hāsastu nāyake hāsyakāraṇam
sāmyādrudhiramohādivibhāvādvyabhicāriṇām 1385
bībhatse sati bhīrūṇāmutpadyeta bhayānakaḥ
anyonyajanyajanakā bhavantyevamime rasāḥ 1386
śyāmaḥ sito dhūsaraśca rakto gauro'sitastathā
nīlaḥ pītastataḥ śveto rasavarṇāḥ kramādime 1387
viṣṇumanmathakīnāśarudrendrāḥ kālasaṃjñakaḥ
mahākālaḥ kramādbrahmā buddhaśca rasadevatāḥ
śṛṅgāre devatāmāhurapare makaradhvajam 1388
vakṣye viśeṣalakṣmāṇi rasāderadhunā kramāt 1389
vibhāvairvarakāntādyaiḥ svakāvye kavigumphitaiḥ
sākṣātkāramivā''nītairnaṭena svaprayogataḥ 1390
nīto ratisthāyibhāvaḥ sadasyarasanīyatām
naṭena kāntadṛṣṭyādyairanubhāvaiḥ pradarśitaiḥ 1391
āveśātiśayaṃ nītaścitrito vyabhicāribhiḥ
harṣacintādibhiḥ proktaḥ śṛṅgāraḥ sūriśārṅgiṇā 1392
saṃbhogavipralambhau vā'vasthe dve tasya kīrtite
kāntāsaṃyogavirahau lakṣaṇe ca tayoḥ kramāt 1393
strīpuṃsayoruttamayoryūnoḥ pūrṇasukhodayā
prārambhātphalaparyantavyāpinī smarasaṃbhṛtā
saṃvidoraikyasaṃpattyā krīḍā'tra sthāyinī ratiḥ 1394
loke sthāyyādibhāvānāṃ dṛṣṭaye janmahetavaḥ
upanītāḥ kavivarairnaṭaiḥ sākṣātkṛtā iva
rasānprasunvate te syurvibhāvāstadvibhāvanāt 1395
kāntau tīvrasmarākrāntau dūtīsakhyādayo janāḥ 1396
tattadyogyo'pyalaṃkāraḥ kirīṭakaṭakādikaḥ
samayo'pi vasantādirviṣayāścandanādayaḥ 1397
gītādayaśca deśo'pi ramyaharmyavanādikaḥ
kāntānukaraṇaṃ haṃsayugmacitrādidarśanam 1398
jalakelītyevamādyāḥ śrutā yadvā vilokitāḥ
vibhāvatvena vikhyātā rase śṛṅgāranāmani 1399
bhāvānāṃ yāni kāryāṇi nāṭyante kuśalairnaṭaiḥ
anubhāvā hetavaste svahetvanubhave yataḥ 1400
te bhāvānābhimukhyena nayanti gamayanti hi
ato'bhinayaśabdenāpyucyante kṛtrimā naṭaiḥ 1401
kāntā dṛṣṭiḥ kaṭākṣākhyaṃ tārakākarma śarmakṛt
cature ca bhruvau cārugocarā madhurā giraḥ 1402
romāñcamukharāgādiracitaḥ sāttvikotkaraḥ
anubhāvā bhavantyete śṛṅgārarasapoṣiṇaḥ 1403
ālasyaugryajugupsābhyo'nye'tra syurvyabhicāriṇaḥ
ālasyāditrayaṃ varjyaṃ svavibhāvaikagocaram 1404
eṣa saṃbhogaśṛṅgāragataḥ parikaro mataḥ
vipralambhe nānubhāvaḥ kāntā dṛṣṭyādiriṣyate 1405
cintāsūyāśramautsukyanirvedaglānimṛtyavaḥ
unmādavyādhyapasmāravibodhajaḍatāstathā 1406
dainyaṃ nidrā tathā svapnasuptacintādayaśca ye
tadīyairanubhāvaistu vipralambho'bhinīyate 1407
unmādādidaśāṃ cātikaṣṭāṃ nātra pradarśayet
na cātra maraṇaṃ sākṣāddarśayettasya sūcikām 1408
tadanantarajātāṃ tu darśayetsukhinīṃ daśām
daśāṃ vā bhāvinidhanāṃ sūcayenna tvimāṃ vadet 1409
nanu duḥkhātmakaḥ kasmādvipralambho raso mataḥ
maivaṃ ratyanusaṃdhānaṃ vipralambhe'pi dṛśyate 1410
vipralambhodbhavā śaṅkā saṃbhoge dustyajā dhruvam
itaretaracitratvādrasatvaṃ sulabhaṃ tayoḥ 1411
naikarūpe cirāsvādyaḥ śarkarāvadbhavedasau 1412
saṃbhoge'pi na nidrā'sti nanvanyonyābhilāṣiṇoḥ
vibodhastvastyasau kasmādvipralambhaikagocaraḥ 1413
naitannidrāvināśo hi vibodhastāmṛte kutaḥ 1414
ratiśramakṛtā nidrā saṃbhoge'pyasti cenna tat
bhavantyapi na sodbhāvyā ratyutkarṣavirodhinī 1415
karuṇe vipralambhe ca cintādivyabhicāriṇām
sāṣāraṇyāttayorbhedo nāsti cettadasāṃpratam 1416
śoko hi karuṇe sthāyī vipralambhe ratirmatā 1417
sthāyibhedādvibhāgo'tastayoruttamapūruṣe
natu nīce tu mālyādivibhāvānāmasaṃbhavāt 1418
ratyabhāve vipralambhaḥ śṛṅgāro jāyate katham
ataḥ syātkaruṇaḥ spaṣṭastatreṣṭavirahodbhavaḥ 1419
mālyādyasaṃbhavānnīce saṃbhogāsaṃbhavo'pi cet
tanna nīcaratiryasmānnārīmātravibhāvajā
viyoge'pi tathā mālyādyabhāvena kathaṃ ratiḥ 1420
āśābandhaślathībhūtacintādivyabhicārikaḥ
virahaḥ karuṇādbhinnaḥ priyanairāśyakāritāt 1421
kāmasūtre ca śṛṅgāro daśāvasthaḥ pradarśitaḥ
abhilāṣastataścintānusmṛtirguṇakīrtanam 1422
udvego'tha vilāpaḥ syādunmādo vyādhisaṃbhavaḥ
jaḍatā maraṇaṃ ceti daśāvasthāḥ prakīrtitāḥ 1423
bhoktā pradhāno bhogyā tu kāntā tadupasarjanam
ato narāntarāsaktistasyāḥ śṛṅgārabhaṅgakṛt
bhoktastvaparatantratvātkāntāntaramabhañjakam 1424
vāṅnepathyāṅgaveṣṭābhiḥ śṛṅgārastrividho mataḥ
rāmādivyañjako veṣo naṭe nepathyamiṣyate 1425
iti śṛṅgāraḥ
veṣālaṃkāragamanagaditānāṃ vikāritā 1426
eteṣāṃ ca parasthānāmanukāro vilajjatā
viṣaye'rucitā dṛṣṭyā'pyasaṃgatavibhāṣaṇam 1427
hāsanīyasya kakṣādisaṃsparśaḥ kuhanāvahaḥ
ityādayo vibhāvāḥ syuranubhāvāstu netrayoḥ 1428
kapolayoroṣṭhayośca spando dṛṣṭyorvikāsanam
atyantamīlanaṃ kiṃcitkuñcanaṃ pārśvapīḍanam 1429
prasvedamukharāgādyā yasyātha vyabhicāriṇaḥ
svapno'vahitthamālasyanidrātandrādayo'pare 1430
ete smitādibhedeṣu yathāyogaṃ vyavasthitāḥ
sthāyibhāvaśca hāsaḥ syādasau hāsyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ 1431
ātmasthaḥ parasaṃsthaścetyasya bhedadvayaṃ matam
ātmastho draṣṭurutpanno vibhāvekṣaṇamātrataḥ 1432
hasantamaparaṃ dṛṣṭvā vibhāvaiścopajāyate
yo'sau hāsyarasastajjñaiḥ parasthaḥ parikīrtitaḥ 1433
uttamānāṃ madhyamānāṃ nīcānāmapyasau bhavet
tryavasthaḥ kathitastasya ṣaḍbhedāḥ santi cāpare 1434
smitaṃ ca hasitaṃ proktamuttame puruṣe budhaiḥ
bhavedvihasitaṃ copahasitaṃ madhyame nare
nīce'pahasitaṃ cātihasitaṃ parikīrtitam 1435
īṣatphullakapolābhyāṃ kaṭākṣairapyanulbaṇaiḥ
adṛśyadaśano hāsyo mantharasmitamucyate 1436
vaktranetrakapolaiścedutphullairupalakṣitaḥ
kiṃcillakṣitadantaśca tadā hasitamiṣyate 1437
saśabdaṃ madhuraṃ kālāgataṃ vadanarāgavat
ākuñcitākṣigaṇḍaṃ ca vidurvihasitaṃ budhāḥ 1438
nikuñcitāṃsaśīrṣaśca jihmadṛṣṭivilokanaḥ
utphullanāsiko hāsyo nāmnopahasitaṃ mataḥ 1439
asthānajaḥ sāśrudṛṣṭirākampraskandhamūrdhakaḥ
śārṅgadevena gadito hāsyo'pahasitāhvayaḥ 1440
sthūlakarṇaḥ kaṭudhvāno bāṣpapūraplutekṣaṇaḥ
karopagūḍhapārśvaśca hāsyo'tihasitaṃ mataḥ 1441
pūrvāṇi svasamutthāni smitādiyugalatraye
parāṇi tvaparasthāni pūrvasaṃkrāntijatvataḥ 1442
hāsyaraudrāvapi tredhā vāṅnepathyāṅgaceṣṭitaiḥ
upalakṣaṇametattu teṣāṃ sarveṣu saṃbhavāt 1443
iti hāsyaḥ
iṣṭabandhviyogaśca śrīnāśo vadhabandhane 1444
vyasanaprabhavo'narthaḥ sutādinidhanaṃ tathā
deśabhraṃśādayaścaite śrutā yadvā vilokitāḥ 1445
vibhāvāḥ saṃmatāḥ puṃsāmuttamānāṃ parāśritāḥ
madhyamādhamapuṃsāṃ tu te syurātmaikagocarāḥ 1446
aśrupāto mukhe śoṣo vilāpaḥ paridevanam
stambho vivarṇatā srastagātratā pralayastathā 1447
śvāsocchvāsau dehapātaghātorastāḍanādayaḥ
ete yatrānubhāvāḥ syuratha glāniḥ śramo bhayam 1448
moho viṣādanirvedau cintautsukye ca dīnatā
jaḍatā vyādhirunmādālasyāpasmāramṛtyavaḥ 1449
stambhakampāśruvaivarṇyasvarabhaṅgādayastathā
yatra saṃcāriṇaḥ sthāyī śokaḥ sa karuṇo mataḥ 1450
vyavasthā'trānubhāvānāṃ yogyatvāduttamādiṣu 1451
vilāpo rodanaṃ śocyaguṇastavapuraḥsaram
syāddaivātmaparāṇāṃ yadupālambhena rodanam
paridevanamityuktaṃ tadidaṃ śabdakovidaiḥ 1452
dharmopaghātajo vittanāśajo bandhunāśajaḥ
karuṇastrividhasteṣāmuttameṣveva pūrvajaḥ 1453
tredhā ruditamānandārtīrṣyāhetukṛtaṃ bhavet 1454
duḥkhasmṛtiyutānandājjātaṃ phullakapolakam
saromāñcamapāṅgasthabāṣpamānandajaṃ matam 1455
uccaiḥ svaravilāpaṃ ca sabhrūprāntavivartitam
srutāśrudhāramasvasthaceṣṭāṅgaṃ cā''rtijaṃ matam 1456
śiraḥkalpitaniśvāsaṃ sphuradoṣṭhakapolakam
kaṭākṣabhrukuṭīvakraṃ strīṇāmīrṣyākṛtaṃ bhavet 1457
dhairyāduttamamadhyānāṃ nāśru svavyasane sravet
parasthe tu sravennārīnīcānāṃ tūbhayatra tat 1458
iti karuṇaḥ
hantṛprakṛtayo rakṣodaityādyanyāyakāriṇaḥ
yuddhaṃ krodho'nṛtaṃ vākyaṃ paradārādidharṣaṇam 1459
deśajātikulācāravidyānindā parasya
vadhānyadārayātrādipratijñāparuṣoktayaḥ 1460
gṛhādibhañjanaṃ rājyaharaṇaṃ matsarastathā
jighāṃsādyāśca yatra syurvibhāvāḥ kavikīrtitāḥ 1461
bhrukuṭīraktanetratvaṃ kapolasphuraṇaṃ tathā
dantoṣṭhapīḍanaṃ hastaniṣpeṣo'thānyavigrahe 1462
talādyaistāḍanaṃ chedo mardaḥ pāṭanamoṭane
śastrāṇāṃ grahaṇaṃ pātaḥ prahāro rudhirasrutiḥ 1463
ete'nubhāvāsteṣāṃ tu karma yattāḍanādikam
na tatsākṣātprayoktavyaṃ kīrtanīyaṃ paraṃ naṭaiḥ 1464
utsāhasamyagbodhau cāmarṣāvegaugryacāpalam
svedavepathuromāñcagadgadasvaratādayaḥ 1465
sākṣātsvedo'bhineyo vā vyajanagrahaṇādinā
utsāhastvatra saṃcārī na tasmādvīrasaṃkaraḥ 1467
nanu cāvyabhicāreṇa bhāvasya sthāyitā matā
sthāyī tatkathamutsāho vīre'pyeṣa bhaviṣyati 1468
maivaṃ kṣaṇikavidyotā vidyudvadvyabhicāriṇaḥ
sthirāḥ syuḥ sthāyinastena dvirūpo'yaṃ rasadvaye 1469
nanu sthāyyapi bhāvatvānnisargātkṣaṇiko bhavet
satyaṃ kiṃtu sa saṃskārarūpeṇa sthāyitāṃ gataḥ 1470
tattiraskṛtasaṃskārāstvanye tatsthairyayoginaḥ
āvirbhāvatirobhāvadharmāṇaścātra yantritāḥ 1471
gurau priye ripau bhṛtye bhavetkrodhaścaturvidhaḥ
gurāvavyaktaceṣṭaḥ syātkrodho vinayayantritaḥ 1472
apāṅganirgatālpāsro bhrukuṭīkuṭilānanaḥ
sphuritoṣṭho mṛgākṣīṇāṃ kopaḥ praṇayajaḥ priye 1472
daṣṭoṣṭho hastaniṣpeṣī vikaṭabhrukuṭīpuṭaḥ
svabhujaprekṣaṇaiḥ śatrau bhavetkrodho nirargalaḥ 1474
vikṛtaprekṣaṇairakṣivistārairbhūribhartsanaiḥ
bhṛtye kāryavaśātkopaḥ kṛtrimaḥ krūratojjhitaḥ 1475
avismayādasaṃmohādaviṣādācca yaḥ satām
dharprādyarthaviśeṣeṣu kāryastattvaviniścayaḥ 1476
nayaśca vinayaḥ kīrtiḥ parākramaṇaśaktatā
pratāpaḥ prabhuśaktiśca durdharṣaprauḍhasainyatā 1477
mantraśaktiśca saṃpannadhanābhijanamantritā
ityādayo vibhāvāḥ syurnaṭe kāvyasamanvitāḥ 1478
sāmādīnāmupāyānāṃ yathāsvaṃ viniyojane
vijñatvaṃ sthairyadhairye ca śauryaṃ tyāgo yathocitam 1479
bhāṣaṇaṃ bhāvagambhīravākyasyetyevamādayaḥ
sarve garvānubhāvāścānubhāvāḥ syuratha smṛtiḥ 1480
augryamāvegaromāñcāvamarṣaśca dhṛtirmatiḥ
evaṃprabhṛtayo bhāvā yatra syurvyabhicāriṇaḥ 1481
utsāhaḥ sthāyibhāvaśca dhīrā vīraṃ tamūcire
uttamaprakṛtiṣveva puruṣeṣveṣa jāyate 1482
samyagvijñāya ṣāḍguṇyaprayogo gīyate nayaḥ 1483
saṃdhiśca vigraho yānamāsanaṃ saṃśrayastathā
dvaidhībhāva iti proktā nītiśāstreṣu ṣaḍguṇāḥ 1484
indriyāṇāṃ jayaṃ prāha vinayaṃ śaṃkarapriyaḥ
pratāpaḥ pauruṣodbhūtā prasiddhiḥ śatrutāpinī 1485
kīrtiḥ suhṛdudāsīnajanānandapradaṃ yaśaḥ
sāmadāne bhedadaṇḍāvityupāyacatuṣṭayam 1486
sthairyaṃ tvacalacittatvaṃ dhairyaṃ gāmbhīryaśālitā
śauryaṃ bhavedanudbhūtabhayaṃ yuddhapravartanam 1487
vīraḥ syāducite yuddhe raudrastvanucite bhavet 1488
dānavīro dharmavīro yuddhavīra iti tridhā
vīro dānādayastatra vibhāvāḥ pratināyake
nāyake tvanubhāvāḥ syurvīrasyeti vidāṃ matam 1489
iti vīraḥ
rakṣaḥpiśācabhallūkaprabhṛterbhīṣaṇākṛteḥ
darśanaṃ śravaṇaṃ śūnyāgārāraṇyapraveśayoḥ 1490
vikṛtasya dhvanestrāsodvegayoḥ parasaṃsthayoḥ
śravaṇaṃ cānusaṃdhānaṃ bandhūnāṃ vadhabandhayoḥ 1491
evamādyā vibhāvāḥ syuratha netrakarāṅghriṇaḥ
madhye madhye stambhakampau romāñcanicayastathā 1492
śuṣkoṣṭhatālutā kamprahṛdayatvaṃ vivarṇatā
mukhasya svarabhedaśca gātrasraṃso vilakṣyatā 1493
kāṃdiśīkatayā dṛṣṭeranubhāvā bhavantyamī
atha stambhādayo'pyaṣṭau dainyamāvegacāpale 1494
śaṅkāmohāvapi trāsāpasmāramaraṇādayaḥ
yatra saṃcāriṇaḥ sthāyi bhayaṃ sa syādbhayānakaḥ 1495
vāstavaṃ kṛtrimaṃ ceti bhayaṃ dvedhā nigadyate 1496
strīṇāṃ ca nīcaprakṛteḥ puṃsaḥ syādvāstavaṃ bhayam
madhyame cottame puṃsi kṛtrimaṃ tadudāhṛtam 1497
svabhāvādabhayau tau hi svāminaṃ ca guruṃ prati
bhayaṃ vinayabodhāya darśayetāmatāttvikam 1498
nīcaiḥ svabhāvabodhārthaṃ tatkāryaṃ mṛduceṣṭitaiḥ 1499
svahetūtthaḥ kṛtrimaśca vitrāsitakamityapi
bhayānakastridhā tatra prathamo'nvarthanāmakaḥ 1500
kṛtrimastūttamakṛto gurvādīnpratyavāstavaḥ
vibhīṣikārtho bālādervitrāsitakamiṣyate 1501
iti bhayānakaḥ
svabhāvāddhātudoṣādvā vastvatyantāpriyātmakam
niṣiddhamatitṛpteścārucitaṃ malamiśritam 1502
aniṣṭaphaladānena muhurudvegadāyakam
syādvibhāvo'thānubhāvā utkampo gātradhūnanam 1503
saṃkocanaṃ ca nāsoṣṭhahanūnāṃ ṣṭhīvanaṃ tathā
padanyāso'pyaniyataḥ pidhānaṃ nāsayordṛśoḥ 1504
atha saṃcāriṇo mohāvegāpasmāramṛtyavaḥ
vyādhiśca yatra bībhatsaḥ sa sthāyinyā jugupsayā 1505
śuddho'śuddho'tyantaśuddho bībhatsastrividho mataḥ 1506
ādyau rudhiraviṣṭhādiśuddhāśuddhavibhāvajau
śuddhadharmodbhavo'tyantaśuddhaḥ saṃsāragocaraḥ 1507
iti bībhatsaḥ
durlabhābhīṣṭasaṃprāptiḥ khecarāṇāṃ vilokanam
vimānānāṃ ca māyānāmindrajālasya darśanam 1508
prāsādopavanāderapyapūrvasyātiśāyinaḥ
darśanaṃ śravaṇaṃ vākyaṃ śilpādyatiśayānvitam 1509
ete yatra vibhāvāḥ syuranubhāvāstu netrayoḥ
vistāraṇaṃ nirnimeṣamīkṣaṇaṃ pulakodgamaḥ 1510
sādhuvādollukasane cā''nandāddhāravastathā
gadgadaṃ vacanaṃ svedavepathū harṣagocarāḥ 1511
sparśagrahāśritāścānubhāvāḥ saṃcāriṇastvamī
stambhaḥ svedaśca romāñcaḥ pralayo gadgadaṃ vacaḥ 1512
āvegasaṃbhramau jāḍyamiti yatrātha vismayaḥ
sthāyī tamadbhutaṃ prāha śrīmatsoḍhalanandanaḥ 1513
māyā mithyāprakaṭitaṃ rūpādiparivartanam 1514
asaṃbhāvyasya sattvasya darśanaṃ vividhauṣadhaiḥ
hastalāghavato mantrairindrajālaṃ prakīrtitam 1515
gātroddhūnanamāhlādādatrollukasanaṃ matam
kiṃcidākuñcite netre sparśaḥ skandhakapolayoḥ
bhrūkṣepaṇaṃ cānubhāvāstatra sparśagrahāśritāḥ 1516
ānandajastathā divyo dvividho'bhidadhe'dbhutaḥ
ādyo manorathāvāpterdivyavastūdbhavo'paraḥ 1517
ityadbhutaḥ
saṃsārabhīrutā doṣadarśanaṃ viṣayeṣu ca
yogisaṅgo munīnāṃ ca śrutāḥ śamadamakṣamāḥ 1518
adhyātmaviṣayā goṣṭhī tāpasāstāpasāśramāḥ
bhūrinirjharajhāṃkāri vāri tīrthāni tīrthikāḥ 1519
saritaḥ puṇyapayaso nīvārāṅkitasaikatāḥ
śaivaṃ ca vaiṣṇavaṃ kṣetraṃ vijanāni vanāni ca 1520
bhaktā bhaktirasonmattāḥ śaṃkarasya harerapi
viṣṇubhaktiprabhāvādyā vibhāvā yatra saṃmatāḥ 1521
mandaspandaṃ bahiścittamānandāśruplute dṛśau
romāñcakañcukā mūrtirmokṣaśāstrārthacintanam 1522
brahmavidyopadeśaśca saṃvādastattvagocaraḥ
nāsāgrānugate netre jñānamudrāpradarśanam 1523
ityādayo'nubhāvāḥ syurime tu vyabhicāriṇaḥ
unmādaḥ paramānandarasapānasamudbhavaḥ 1524
harṣo dhṛtiḥ samīcīno vibodhaśca smṛtirmatiḥ
nirvedastattvabodhotthaḥ sthāyī śānto bhavedasau 1525
ratyādibhedavidhuro viṣayopaplavojjhitaḥ
parānandaghanaikātmanirbhāsaḥ śānta ucyate 1526
svato viṣayavaimukhyaṃ śamaḥ sthāyyathavā bhavet
nāṭyanirvāhako madhye madhye saṃcārisaṃbhavaḥ 1527
svabhāve'bhinayāḥ pūrvamāṅkikā ye nirūpitāḥ
te śāntaviṣayā yasmācchāntaḥ prakṛtirātmanaḥ 1528
vihāya viṣayaunmukhyaṃ nijānandasthitiryataḥ
ātmanaḥ kathyate śāntaḥ svabhāvo'sau matastataḥ 1529
iti śāntaḥ
bhaktiṃ snehaṃ tathā laulyaṃ kecittrīnmanvate rasān
śraddhārdratābhilāṣāṃśca sthāyinasteṣu te viduḥ 1530
tadasadratibhedau hi bhaktisnehau nṛgocarau
vyabhicāritvamanayornṛnāryoḥ sthāyinau tu tau 1531
ayuktaviṣayā tṛṣṇā laulyaṃ taddhāsyakāraṇam
ato rasā navaiveti muninā saṃpradhāritam 1532
ratyādayaḥ sthāyibhāvāḥ syurbhūyiṣṭhavibhāvajāḥ
stokairvibhāvairutpannāsta eva vyabhicāriṇaḥ
rasāntareṣvapi tadā yathāyogaṃ bhavanti te 1533
yathā hi hāsaḥ śṛṅgāre ratiḥ śānte ca dṛśyate
vīre krodho bhayaṃ śoke jgupsā ca bhayānake 1534
utsāhavismayau sarvaraseṣu vyabhicāriṇau
śamaḥ sarvaraseṣvasti sthairyatve'vyabhicāryasau 1535
iti navarasalakṣaṇam
atha vyabhicāriṇāṃ nirvedādīnāṃ lakṣaṇam
ākrośanamadhikṣepo vyādhikrodhau ca tāḍanam
dāridryamiṣṭavirahaḥ paravṛddheśca darśanam 1536
nīceṣviti vibhāvāḥ syuruttame tvapamānanam
tattvabodhaśca yatra syādanubhāvāstu rodanam 1537
niśvāsocchvasite dīnamukhatā saṃpradhāraṇam
ityādayaḥ sa nirvedo bhāvaḥ sūribhiriṣyate 1538
iti nirvedaḥ
vyādhirvāntirvirekaścopavāso niyamastapaḥ
manastāpo'tipānāṭivyāyāmasuratāni ca 1539
nidrāchedo'dhvagamanaṃ kṣutpipāsādayastathā
vibhāvāḥ syuratha srastādharanetrakapolatā 1540
mandāvutkṣepanikṣepau pādayostanugātratā
vivarṇaśithilāṅgatvaṃ kampānutsāhatādayaḥ
yatra bhāvānubhāvāḥ syuḥ sā glānirabhidhīyate 1541
iti glāniḥ
cauryarājāparādhāderakāryādgrahaṇaṃ nṛṇām
anyeṣu tatsahāyeṣu vibhāvaścedathā''tmanaḥ 1542
anyenādarśane yatra pārśvayormuhurīkṣaṇam
kaṇṭhoṣṭhamukhaśoṣaśca jihvāyāḥ parilehanam 1543
vepathurmukhavaivarṇyaṃ guruvihvalajihvatā
unmukhekṣaṇamityādiranubhāvagaṇo yadā 1544
nīceṣūttamamadhyeṣu nareṣu mṛduceṣṭitaiḥ
sottamānāmapi strīṇāṃ bhīrutvādbhayakṛdbhavet 1545
priyavipriyajā śaṅkā śṛṅgāre śārṅgiṇoditā
cauryarājāparādhādiprabhavā tu bhayānake 1546
parasthā svasamutthā ca dvedhā śaṅkā nigadyate
nṛṣu bhīrusvabhāveṣu taralālokanādibhiḥ 1547
sāparādhatvaśaṅkā yā'nyeṣāṃ sā parasaṃsthitā
svasamutthā pare jñāsyantyaparādhaṃ mametyasau 1548
svaśaṅkāyāṃ parajñānaśaṅkā yā svasamutthitā
sahabhāvo'vahitthena śaṅkāyā dṛśyate rase 1549
iti śaṅkā
nṛpāparādho'saddoṣakīrtanaṃ copadhāraṇam
vibhāvāḥ syuratho bandho vadhastāḍanabhartsane
ete yatrānubhāvāstadaugryaṃ nirdayatātmakam 1550
ityaugryam
cintautsukyānmanastāpāddaurgatyācca vibhāvataḥ 1551
anubhāvāttu śiraso vyāvṛttergātragauravāt
dehopaskaraṇatyāgāddainyaṃ bhāvaṃ vibhāvayet 1552
iti dainyam
nānāparādho vidveṣaḥ parasyaiśvaryasaṃpadām
saubhagyamedhāvidyāderdarśanaṃ ca vibhāvakam 1553
anubhāvāstvamarṣādidoṣoktirguṇanihnavaḥ
guṇasya doṣīkaraṇamaprekṣaṇamadhomukham
avajñābhrukuṭītyādyā yatrāsūyāmamūṃ viduḥ 1554
ityasūyā
madyapānaṃ vibhāvo'thānubhāvāḥ pañca yatra ca
śayanaṃ hasanaṃ gānaṃ rodanaṃ parūṣoktayaḥ 1555
uttame puruṣe svāpo hāsagāne tu madhyame
rodanaṃ puruṣoktiśca bhavataḥ puruṣe'dhame 1556
sa syānmadaḥ sa ca tredhā taruṇo madhyamo'dhamaḥ
taruṇo'lpo'dhamastvatra pravṛddho'dhamasaṃśrayāt 1557
sarveṣāṃ taruṇaḥ prokto madhyamo madhyanīcayoḥ
pumāṃso'dhamasattvā ye teṣāmevādhamo madaḥ 1558
avyaktāsaṃgatairvākyai romāñcanicayaistanoḥ
sukumāraskhaladgatyā'bhinayettaruṇaṃ madam 1559
srastavyākulavikṣiptau bhujau skhalitaghūrṇite
netre gatiśca kuṭilā'nubhāvā madhyame made 1560
gatibhaṅgaḥ smṛternāśo hikkā chardiḥ kaphasnutiḥ
gurvī jihvā ṣṭhīvanaṃ cānubhāvāḥ syurmade'dhame 1561
raṅge vidhāya pānaṃ tu nāṭayenmadavardhanam
pītvā raṅgapraveśe tu madakṣaiṇyāya buddhimān
harṣaśokabhayopāyānyugapaddarśayedbahūn 1562
iti madaḥ
adhvavyāyāmasevādyairvibhāvairanubhāvakaiḥ 1563
gātrasaṃvāhanairāsyasaṃkocairaṅgamoṭanaiḥ
niśvāsairjṛmbhitairmandaiḥ pādakṣepaiḥ śramo yataḥ 1564
iti śramaḥ
vibhāvā yatra dāridryamaiśvaryaṃ bhraṃśanaṃ tathā
iṣṭārthāpahṛtiścātha śvāsocchvāsāvadhomukham 1565
saṃtāpaḥ smaraṇaṃ dhyānaṃ kārśyaṃ dehānupaskṛtiḥ
adhṛtiścānubhāvāḥ syuḥ sā cintā parikīrtitā
vitarko'syāḥ kṣaṇe pūrve pāścātye vopajāyate 1566
iti cintā
vivekaḥ śrutisaṃpattirgurubhaktistapasvitā 1567
ete viṣayabhāvena karaṇatvena ca sthitāḥ
iṣṭādhikānāmiṣṭānāṃ lābhastu viṣayatvataḥ 1568
krīḍā karaṇabhāvena vibhāvā yatra saṃmatāḥ
prāptabhāgyopabhogasya tvaprāptātītabhogayoḥ 1569
jīrṇe naṣṭe cāviṣādo na tu śocanamityapi
anubhāvadvayaṃ yatra dhṛtiṃ tāṃ bruvate budhāḥ 1570
iti dhṛtiḥ
paścimaprahare rātreḥ svāsthyaṃ nidrākṣayastathā
dhyānaṃ ca muhurabhyāsaḥ sadṛśaḥ śrutidarśane 1571
ete yatra vibhāvāḥ syuranubhāvāstu kampitam
udvāhitaṃ ca śīrṣasyāsadṛśasyāvalokanam 1572
bhrūnatiśca smṛtiḥ sā syātsukhaduḥkhapradāyinām
ciravismṛtavastūnāṃ smaraṇaṃ smṛtirucyate 1573
iti smṛtiḥguruvyatikramo'vajñā kṛte tyāge'nutāpitā
pratijñātārthanirvāho vibhāvairebhirudgatā 1574
mukhāvanamanaṃ gūḍhavacanaṃ ca vicintanam
vastrāṅgulīyakasparśo nakhānāṃ kartanaṃ muhuḥ 1575
bhuvi lekhanamityādyairvrīḍābhāvo'nubhāvyate
vrīḍānutāpaśucibhirdṛṣṭā kārye kṛte sati 1576
iti vrīḍā
devajopadravāddehapīḍeṣṭavirahādikam
marmaprahāro'pyasthāne ghoracorādijaṃ bhayam 1577
tatpratīkāraśūnyasya vairānusmaraṇādayaḥ
vibhāvāḥ santi yatrātha patanaṃ dehaghūrṇanam 1578
hṛdayasmānavasthānamindriyāṇāmaceṣṭatā
ityādayo'nubhāvāstaṃ mohamāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ 1579
paśyato bhītihetuṃ tatpratīkāramapaśyataḥ
kāryāniścayinī cittavṛttirmoho'bhidhīyate 1580
iti mohaḥ
atitṛptiḥ svabhāvaśca garbhavyādhiśramādayaḥ
vibhāvā anubhāvāstu nidrā tandrāsanāśanāt
ṛte sarvakriyādveṣo yatrā''lasyaṃ taducyate 1581
ityālasyam
amarṣaprātikūlyerṣyārāgadveṣāśca matsaraḥ
iti yatra vibhāvāḥ syuranubhāvāstu bhartsanam 1582
vākpāruṣyaṃ prahāraśca tāḍanaṃ vadhabandhane
taccāpalamanālocyākāryakāritvamiṣyate 1583
iti cāpalam
devabhartṛgurusvāmiprasādaḥ priyasaṃgamaḥ 1584
manorathāptiraprāpyamanorathadhanāgamaḥ
samutpattiśca putrādervibhāvo yatra jāyate 1585
netravaktraprasādaśca priyoktiḥ pulakodgamaḥ
aśrusvedādayaścānubhāvā harṣaṃ tamādiśet 1586
iti harṣaḥ
vidyādhanabalaiśvaryādhikairmadhyesabhaṃ nṛbhiḥ
adhikṣepādamarṣaḥ syātpratīkāraspṛhātmakaḥ
nṛṇāmutsāhināmeva sa syāttasyānubhāvakaḥ 1587
svedo'dhomukhatā mūrdhakampo nirlakṣacittatā
upāyānveṣaṇaṃ cittotsāhastasyānubhāvakāḥ 1588
ityamarṣaḥ
satyupāye'pi kāryasyāsiddhirdurdaivakāritā 1589
nṛpāparādhaścauryādigrahaṇaṃ ca vibhāvakāḥ
sahāyānveṣaṇopāyacintane vimanaskatā 1590
uttame madhyame cānubhāvāścotsāhasaṃśrayāḥ
adhame dhāvanaṃ dhyānaṃ mukhaśoṣavilokane 1591
nidrā niḥśvasitaṃ sṛkkalehanaṃ cānubhāvakāḥ
santi yatra viṣādo'sau bhāvo bhāvavidāṃ mataḥ 1592
iti viṣādaḥ
devairnāgaistathā yakṣaiḥ piśācairbrahmarākṣasaiḥ
bhūtādyaiśca grahairugrairāveśastatsmṛtistathā 1593
aśuceścirasaṃsthānaṃ śūnyāgārasya sevanam
dhātuvaiṣamyamityādyairvibhāvairanubhāvakaiḥ 1594
spandanaṃ kampaniḥśvāsau dhāvanaṃ patanaṃ bhuvi
jihvāyā lehanaṃ svedaḥ stambho vaktraṃ ca phenilam 1595
niḥsaṃjñatetyādibhiścāpasmāramupalakṣayet 1596
ityapasmāraḥ
kāryāviveko jaḍatā paśyataḥ śṛṇvato'pi vā
tadvibhāvāḥ priyāniṣṭadarśanaśravaṇe rujā 1597
anubhāvāstu caivāsyāḥ prativādāvabhāṣaṇe
iṣṭāniṣṭāparijñānaṃ cānimeṣekṣaṇādayaḥ
sā pūrvaṃ parato vā syānmohāditi vidāṃ matam 1598
iti jaḍatā
yatra vipratipattiḥ syātsaṃśayo'pi vimarśanam
pakṣayorubhayorukte māne sādhakabādhake 1599
vibhāvāḥ syurime cānubhāvāḥ śīrṣasya kampanam
bhrūkṣepaścaturo hastastaṃ vitarkaṃ pracakṣate 1600
yo vitarkānvitasthāyī so'vahitthena yujyate 1601
iti vitarkaḥ
nidrāvibhāvajaṃ suptaṃ suptāvasthātmakaṃ matam
tasyānubhāvā nibhṛtaṃ gātraṃ netranimīlitam
svapnapralāpanaṃ śvāsocchvāsau bāhyākṣalīnatā 1602
iti suptam
saṃjātamiṣṭavirahāduddīptaṃ priyasaṃsmṛteḥ 1603
nidrayā tandrayā gātragauraveṇa ca cintayā
anubhāvitamākhyātamautsukyaṃ bhāvakovidaiḥ 1604
ityautsukyam
anubhāvapidhānārtho'vahitthaṃ bhāva ucyate 1605
tadvibhāvyaṃ bhayavrīḍādhārṣṭyakauṭilyagauravaiḥ
garvādagaṇanenāpi tasyete tvanubhāvakāḥ 1606
priyādigocarakathābhaṅgo dhairyaṃ ca kṛtrimam
anyathākathanaṃ tadvadanyathā vekṣaṇādayaḥ 1607
ityavahittham
a pūrvapratibhānaṃ syānmatistāṃ tu vibhāvayet
anvayavyatirekotthaiḥ pratyayaiḥ śāstracintanaiḥ 1608
ūhāpohaiśca vividhairatha tāmanubhāvayet
saṃdaṃśacaturādyaiśca karairutkṣepaṇairbhruvoḥ 1609
nānāśāstrārthaviṣayaiḥ śiṣyāṇāmupadeśanaiḥ
tarkākhyau pratyayāvūhāpohau vidhiniṣedhayoḥ 1610
iti matiḥ
svapnāntanidrāchedābhyāmāhārapariṇāmataḥ
śabdasparśādibhiḥ svapnaiḥ smṛtairvā jāgrati sthitaiḥ 1611
vibhāvyate'tha jṛmbhākṣimardanaiḥ śayanodgamaiḥ
aṅgulitroṭanairaṅgavalanairyo'nubhāvyate 1612
bhujavikṣepaṇaiḥ śvāsairvibodhapratibodhanam
samyagbodho'thavā lokāttadvibhāvānubhāvadhīḥ 1613
iti bodhaḥ
ekaikaśo dvaṃdvaśo vā trayāṇāṃ vā prakopataḥ
vātapittakaphānāṃ bhāvo vyādhirityabhidhīyate
stambhāṅgasraṃsavikṣiptagātrasaṃkucitānanaiḥ 1615
utkrośakampastanitaiścābhineyo jvaraḥ punaḥ
kāmajo'pyasti sa dvedhā śītadāhapravartanāt 1616
hanucālena sarvāṅgakampena mukhaśoṣataḥ
paridevanaromāñcamukhasaṃkocanādibhiḥ 1617
śītajvaro'bhineyo'tha dāhako'mbupipāsayā
karāṅgagātravikṣepādbhūmiśayyābhilāṣataḥ
śītānulepanākāṅkṣāvikṛṣṭaparidevanaiḥ 1618
iti vyādhiḥ
uttamānāṃ vipralambhe bhavetpriyaviyogataḥ
nīcānāṃ vibhavabhraṃśātsarveṣāṃ saṃnipātataḥ 1619
abhighātāttathonmādastamebhiranubhāvayet
vinā nimittaṃ hasitaṃ ruditaṃ paṭhitaṃ tathā 1620
nṛttaṃ gītaṃ ca śayanopaveśotthānadhāvanam
asaṃbaddhapralapanaṃ codvṛttirdhūlibhasmanoḥ 1621
kapālacīranirmālyādyalaṃkaraṇamityapi
unmādaḥ pṛthagukto'yaṃ vyādhiṣvantarbhavannapi 1622
ayaṃ hi vipralambhādau vaicitryaṃ kurute tamām
apasmāro'pyevameva bībhatse ca bhayānake 1623
ityunmādaḥ
kularūpabalaiśvaryavidyādraviṇayauvanaiḥ 1624
nīcānāṃ jāyate garvaḥ prādhānyena muhurmuhuḥ
vidyudvaduttamānāṃ tu bāhulyāttveṣa yoṣitām 1525
udgrīvāvekṣaṇāvajñāsūyānuttaradānataḥ
abhāṣaṇādamarṣācca pāruṣyādvibhramādapi 1626
aṅganetravikārāccādhikṣepātikramādguroḥ
tasyābhinayanaṃ kāryamityāha karaṇāgraṇīḥ 1627
vāgaṅgasāttvikāhāryakriyāvyatyāsanaṃ bhavet
vibhramo yoṣitāṃ harṣānurāgamadagarvajaḥ 1628
iti garvaḥ
adhīrāṇāṃ jātamātre śoke'nantaramudbhavet
āvegaḥ prabalo'yaṃ tu dhīrairdhairyānnirūpyate 1629
asau prativibhāvaṃ ca pṛthakcitrānubhāvabhāk
ato vibhāvarūpāṇāṃ hetūnāṃ bhedato'ṣṭadhā 1630
utpātavātavarṣāgnikuñjaroddhamaṇāni ca
priyāpriyaśrutirvairavyasanaṃ ceti hetavaḥ 1631
vidyudulkānipātābhyāṃ nirghātādbhūmikampanāt
sūryacandroparāgābhyāmāvegaḥ ketudarśanāt 1632
utpātajo'syānubhāvo vaivarṇyaṃ srastagātratā 1633
viṣādavismayagatānubhāvāśceha saṃmatāḥ
vātaje tvatra vastreṇa pidhānaṃ netramardanam 1634
tvaritāgamanaṃ cānubhāvāḥ syurvarṣaje punaḥ
sarvāṅgapīḍanaṃ gehādyāśrayaḥ śīghradhāvanam 1635
agnije dhūtiraṅgānāṃ dhūmākulitanetratā
cāryā'tikrāntayā cāpakrāntayā caraṇaṃ bhavet 1636
kuñjarāṇāṃ niravadhibhramaṇādyastu jāyate
saraṇāpasṛtī tatra tvarite bhayavepathuḥ 1637
vismayo vīkṣaṇaṃ paścātpriyaśravaṇaje punaḥ
vastrābharaṇadānaṃ cābhyutthānaṃ parirambhaṇam 1638
kathāyāmaśruromāñcāścāpriyaśrutije tvamī
vilāpaḥ patanaṃ bhūmau calanaṃ parivartanam 1639
paribhāvanamityādyā vairivyasanaje tvamī
sahasā'pasṛtiḥ śastracarmavarmādidhāraṇam 1640
gajānāṃ turagāṇāṃ cā''rohaṇaṃ saṃpradhāraṇam
pṛthagityanubhāvāḥ syurāvege saṃbhramātmake 1641
ityāvegaḥ
maraṇaṃ dvividhaṃ proktaṃ vyādhijaṃ cāpaghātajam
doṣavaiṣamyajairgaṇḍajvarādyairvyādhijaṃ bhavet 1642
vātapittakaphā duṣṭā doṣatveneha saṃmatāḥ
śastrādiviṣatoyebhyaḥ śvāpadātturagādgajāt 1643
agnyuccapatanādibhyo jātaṃ syādabhighātajam
niśceṣṭaprasṛtairaṅgairmīlanena ca netrayoḥ 1644
hikkordhvaśvāsavamanajanatāparivāraṇaiḥ
avyaktākṣarabhāṣādyairvyādhijasyānubhāvanam 1645
śastraje sahasā bhūmau patanaṃ ca vikampanam
sphuraṇādi prayoktavyamathāhiviṣapānaje 1646
anubhāvā bhavantyaṣṭau viṣavegātkramādime
kārṣṇyavepathudāhāśca hikkāphenaśca pañcamaḥ 1647
skandhabhaṅgaśca jaḍatā maraṇaṃ ceti saṃmatāḥ
abhighātāntarodbhūte'nubhāvāḥ śastrajātavat 1648
vyādhyāderanivartyatvānniścite maraṇe sati
atra prāṅnidhanāccittavṛttirmaraṇamiṣyate 1649
iti maraṇam
gātrotkampī camatkāraḥ sahasā trāsa ucyate
tasyotpātādghoranādaśruterbhīṣaṇadarśanāt 1650
utpattiranubhāvāstu gātrasaṃkocakampane
stambho gadgadaśabdaśca romāñcāḥ srastagātratā 1651
nimīlanaṃ locanayoḥ pralayaśceti kīrtitāḥ
pūrvāparavicārotthaṃ bhayaṃ trāsātpṛthagbhavet 1652
iti trāsaḥ
nidrendriyāṇāṃ prathamā nivṛttiḥ svasvagocarāt
atyāhāraḥ svabhāvaśca cintālasye madaḥ klamaḥ 1653
vyākhyānādiprayāsena vailakṣaṇyaṃ ca hetavaḥ
nidrāyāmanubhāvāḥ syurjṛmbhāvadanagauravam 1654
śarīralolanaṃ netraghūrṇanaṃ gātramoṭanam
niḥśvāsocchvasitaṃ srastagātratā'kṣinimīlanam 1655
iti nidrā
trayastriṃśadvicitratvakāritvātsthāyinaṃ rasam
kurvantīti mayā proktāḥ santi tvanye sahasraśaḥ 1656
antarbhāvo'sti keṣāṃcidabhilāṣasya vā ratau 1657
avahitthe tu dambhasya glānau kṣuttṛṣṇayoryathā
udvegasya tu nirvede'nyeṣāmapyevamūhyatām 1658
iti trayastriṃśadvyabhicāribhāvāḥ
atha sāttvikalakṣaṇam
uktai ratyādibhirbhāvaiḥ saṃvidvikriyate yadā
prāṇe'dhyasyati sā''tmānaṃ dehaṃ prāṇastanoti saḥ
tadā stambhādayo dehe vikārāḥ prabhavantyamī 1659
evaṃ sati svādyamānaratyādisvairvibhāvakaiḥ
vibhāvitā dehasaṃsthaiḥ stambhādyairanubhāvitāḥ 1660
adhyastasaṃvidi prāṇe prakāśante'ntare bhavāḥ
ete syuḥ sāttvikā bhāvāḥ sattvaprāṇaprakāśanāt 1661
sāṃkhyokto vā guṇaḥ sattvaṃ sādhutvaṃ vā taducyate
nirmalaprāṇadehatvaṃ sādhutvamiha saṃmatam
tatra sattve bhavā bhāvāḥ sāttvikāḥ saṃmatāḥ satām 1662
catvāri kṣmādibhūtāni prādhānyenāvalambate
kadācitsvapradhānaḥ sandehe prāṇaścaratyayam 1663
yadā'dhyāste dharāṃ stambhaṃ bhāvaṃ bhāvayate tadā
prāṇājjalasthādaśrūṇi tejaḥsthāttu vivarṇatā 1664
svedaścā''kāśaniṣṭhāttu pralayo jāyate tataḥ
svatantro'sau kramānmandamadhyatīvratvabhedabhāk 1665
romāñcaṃ vepathumatha svarabhedaṃ ca bhāvayet 1666
bāhyāḥ stambhādayo dehe nṛṇāṃ dehātmamāninām
sulabhā durlabhāstvete satāmanabhimāninām 1667
harṣādrāgādbhayādduḥkhādviṣādādvismayādruṣaḥ
vikṣepācca bhavetstambho'nubhāvā jāḍyaśūnyate
niḥsaṃjñastabdhagātratve niśceṣṭatvāprakampane 1668
iti stambhaḥ
manastāpo rujā harṣo lajjā krodho bhayaṃ śramaḥ
pīḍā''ghātātapau mohavyāyāmau ca vibhāvakāḥ 1669
vyajanagrahaṇaṃ svedo'pyarālo bhālamārjakaḥ
ityete'bhinayā yatra taṃ svedaṃ jagadurbudhāḥ 1670
iti svedaḥ
āliṅganācchuritake śītaharṣabhayakrudhaḥ 1671
vibhāvayanti romāñcaṃ vyaktasyābhinayāstvamī
gātrasparśollukusane muhuḥ kaṇṭakitā tanuḥ 1672
iti romāñcaḥ
augryarogajarākrodhabhayaharṣamadādayaḥ
vibhāvā visvaro bhinno gadgadaśca dhvanirbhavet 1673
anubhāvastadā bhāvaḥ svarabhedo'bhidhīyate
sthānabhraṣṭo visvaraḥ syādvicchinno bhinnasaṃjñakaḥ
avyavasthitatānoccanīcabhāvastu gadgadaḥ 1674
iti svarabhedaḥ
āliṅganācchuritake harṣo roṣo bhayaṃ jarā
vibhāvāḥ śītarogau ca yatraite tvanubhāvakāḥ 1675
vepanaṃ sphuraṇaṃ kampo vepathuṃ kathayanti tam
vepanādyāḥ kampabhedāḥ pūrvātsātiśayaḥ paraḥ 1676
iti vepathuḥ
rogamohabhayakrodhaśītatāpaśramairbhavet 1677
vaivarṇyamasyābhinayo nāḍīpīḍanayogataḥ
sindūrādyena vā varṇāntarasaṃcāraṇaṃ mukhe 1678
iti vaivarṇyam
harṣāmarṣāñjanāddhūmādbhayācchokācca jṛmbhaṇāt
animeṣekṣaṇācchītādrogādbhāvo'śru jāyate
taṃ cānubhāvayedaśrusravaṇairnetramārjanaiḥ 1679
ityaśru
madamūrchābhighātebhyo nidrāmohaśramādibhiḥ
pralayaḥ syādabhinayenmahīnipatanena tam 1680
iti pralayaḥ
ityuktāstrividhā bhāvāḥ sthāyino vyabhicāriṇaḥ
sāttvikāśca raseṣu syuḥ sarve sarveṣu sāttvikāḥ 1681
ekaḥ kāryo rasaḥ sthāyī rasānāṃ nāṭake sadā
rasāstadanuyāyitvādanye tu vyabhicāriṇaḥ 1682
virodhino rasāsteṣu saṃdarbhyaḥ pṛthagāśrayaḥ
gumphaścitro rasānāṃ syānnāṭye kusumagumphavat
sthāyī tu sūtrasthānīyo raso rasavidāṃ mataḥ 1683
sūriśrīśāraṅgadevena nāṭyavedāmbudheridam
samastamuddhṛtaṃ sāraṃ dhīrairātṛpti sevyatām 1684
granthe madīye yadi kaścidasti guṇastatastasya parigrahāya
nābhyarthaye vaḥ sudhiyaḥ pareṣāṃ guṇo'ṇurapyadrisamo bhavatsu 1685
sāvadyatā vā niravadyatā'stu granthe mayā'tra grathite tayā kim
ārādhane vaḥ pravaṇā matirme santo guṇaḥ kaṃ nanu nātiśete 1686
yuṣmatkṣodakṣamaṃ vastu kiyatrāsti jagattraye
kiṃ tumatpremataḥ santaḥ puraskuruta me kṛtim 1687
yadvā purāṇaṃ panthānaṃ munīnāmahamanvagām
snihyanti ca nisargeṇa santaḥ sanmārgagāmini 1688
ārirādhayiṣoḥ sādhūnkiṃ prajñāvibhavena me
rāmamānandayanti sma tiryañco'pi kapīśvarāḥ 1689
na vidyādarpato granthapravṛttirmama kiṃ tvidam
vidvanmānasavāsāya gantuṃ pātheyamāsthitam 1690
iti śrīmadanavadyavidyāvinodaśrīkaraṇādhipatiśrīsoḍhaladevanandananiḥśaṅkaśrīśārṅgadevaviracite saṃgītaratnākare saptamo nartanādhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ 7
samāptimagamadayaṃ kalānidhisametaḥ saṃgītaratnākaraḥ
<8>
pariśiṣṭam 1
ṣaḍjagrāmasamutpannāḥ ṣaḍjakaiśikamadhyamāḥ
śuddhasādhāritaḥ ṣaḍjagrāmo grāme tu madhyamaḥ 8
pañcamo madhyamagrāmaḥ ṣāḍavaḥ śuddhakaiśikaḥ
śuddhāḥ sapteti bhinnāḥ syuḥ pañca kaiśikamadhyamaḥ 9
bhinnaṣaḍjaśca ṣaḍjākhye madhyame tānakaiśikau
bhinnapañcama ityete gauḍakaiśikamadhyamaḥ 10
gauḍapañcamakaḥ ṣaḍje madhyame gauḍakaiśikaḥ
iti gauḍāstrayaḥ ṣaḍje ṭakkavesaraṣāḍavau 11
sasauvīrīmadhyame tu boṭṭamālavakaiśikau
mālavaḥ pañcamānto'tha dvigrāmaṣṭakkakaiśikaḥ 12
hindolo'ṣṭau vesarāste sapta sādhāraṇastataḥ
ṣaḍje syādrūpasādhāraḥ śako bhammāṇapañcamaḥ 13
madhyame nartagāndhārapañcamau ṣāḍjakaiśikaḥ
dvigrāmaḥ kakubhastriṃśadgrāmarāgā amī matāḥ 14
aṣṭoparāgāstilakaḥ śakādiṣṭakkasaindhavaḥ
kokilā pañcamo revaguptaḥ pañcamaṣāḍavaḥ
bhāvanāpañcamo nāgagāndhāro nāgapañcamaḥ 15
śrīrāganaṭṭau baṅgālo bhāsamadhyamaṣāḍavau
raktahaṃsaḥ kohlahāsaḥ prasavo bhairavadhvaniḥ 16
megharāgaḥ somarāgakāmodau cābhrapañcamaḥ
syātāṃ kandarpadeśākhyau kakubhāntaśca kaiśikaḥ
naṭṭanārāyaṇaśceti rāgā viṃśatirīritāḥ 17
sauvīraḥ kakubhaṣṭakkaḥ pañcamo bhinnapañcamaḥ 18
ṭakkakaiśikahindolaboṭṭabhālavakaiśikāḥ
gāndhārapañcamo bhinnaṣaḍjo vesaraṣāḍavaḥ 19
mālavaḥ pañcamāntaśca tānaḥ pañcamaṣāḍavaḥ
bhāṣāṇāṃ janakāḥ pañcadaśaite yāṣṭikoditāḥ 20
bhāṣāścatasraḥ sovīre sauvīrī vegamadhyamā
sādhāritā ca gāndhārī kakubhe bhinnapañcamī 21
kāmbojī madhyamagrāmā ragantī madhurī tathā
śakamiśreti ṣaṭtisro vibhāṣā bhogavardhanī 22
ābhīrikā madhukarī tathaikāntarabhāṣikā
śālavāhanikā ṭakke travaṇā travaṇodbhavā 23
vairañjī madhyamagrāmadehā mālavavesarī
chevāṭī saindhavī kolāhalā pañcamalakṣitā
saurāṣṭrī pañcamī vegarañjī gāndhārapañcamī 24
mālavī tānavalitā lalitā ravicandrikā 25
tānā vāherikā dohyā vesarītyekaviṃśatiḥ
bhāṣāḥ syuratha devāravardhanyāndhrī ca gurjarī 26
bhāvanīti vibhāṣāḥ syuścatasraḥ pañcame punaḥ
kaiśikī trāvaṇī tānodbhavā''bhīrī ca gurjarī 27
saindhavī dākṣiṇātyā''ndhrī māṅgalī bhāvanī daśa
iti bhāṣā vibhāṣe dve bhammāṇyandhālike tataḥ 28
catasraḥ pañcame bhinne bhāṣā dhaivatabhūṣitā
śuddhā bhinnā ca vārāṭī viśāletyatha kauśalī 29
vibhāṣā mālavābhinnavalite ṭakkakaiśike
bhāṣe dve drāviḍītyekā vibhāṣā preṅkhake nava 30
bhāṣāḥ syurvesarī cūtamañjarī ṣaḍjamadhyamā
madhurī bhinnapaurālī gauḍī mālavavesarī
chevāṭī piñjarītyekā boṭṭe bhāṣā tu māṅgalī 31
vāṅgālī māṅgalī harṣapurī mālavavesarī 32
khañjinī gurjarī gauḍī paurālī cārdhavesarī
śuddhā mālavarūpā ca saindhavyābhīriketyamūḥ 33
bhāṣāstrayodaśa jñeyā vijñairmālavakaiśike
vibhāṣe dve tu kāmbojī tadvaddevāravardhanī 34
gāndhārapañcame bhāṣā gāndhārī bhinnaṣaḍjakā
gāndhāravallī kacchellī svaravallī niṣādinī 35
travaṇā madhyamā śuddhā dākṣiṇātyā pulindikā
tumburā ṣaḍjabhāṣā ca kālindī lalitā tataḥ 36
śrīkaṇṭhikā ca bāṅgālī gāndhārī saindhavītyamūḥ
bhāṣāḥ saptadaśa jñeyāścatasrastu vibhāṣikāḥ
paurālī mālavā kālindyapi devāravardhanī 37
vesare ṣāḍave bhāṣe dve bāhyā bāhyaṣāḍavā 38
vibhāṣe pārvatī śrīkaṭhyatha mālavapañcame
bhāṣāstisro vedavatī bhāvinī ca vibhāvinī 39
tāne tānodbhavā bhāṣā bhāṣā pañcamaṣāḍave
potā bhāṣāṃ śakāmeke revagupte vidurviduḥ 40
vibhāṣāpallavī bhāsavalitā kiraṇāvalī
śaṅkādyāvalitetyetāstisrastvantarabhāṣikāḥ 41
catasro'nuktajanakā bṛhadveśyāmimāḥ smṛtāḥ
evaṃ ṣaṇṇavatirbhāṣā vibhāṣā viṃśatiḥ smṛtāḥ
catasro'ntarabhāṣāḥ syuḥ śārṅgadevasya saṃmatāḥ 42
bhāṣā mukhyā svarākhyā ca deśākhyā coparāgajā 43
caturvidhā mataṅgoktā mukhyā'nanyopajīvinī
svaradeśākhyayā khyātā svarākhyā deśajā kramāt 44
anyoparāgajā tābhyo yāṣṭikenoditāḥ punaḥ
saṃkīrṇadeśajā mūlā chāyāmātretināmabhiḥ 45
śuddhā''bhirī ragantī ca tridhā mālavavesarī
mukhyā ṣaḍiti śeṣāstu sujñātāḥ sphuṭalakṣaṇāḥ
nāmasāmyaṃ tu kāsāṃcidbhinnānānapi lakṣmataḥ 46
atha rāgāṅgabhāṣāṅgakriyāṅgopāṅganirṇayam
keṣāṃcinmatamāśritya kurute ṣoḍhalātmajaḥ 47
rañjanādrāgatā bhāṣārāgāṅgāderapīṣyate
deśīrāgatayā proktaṃ rāgāṅgādicatuṣṭayam 48
prasiddhā grāmarāgādyāḥ keciddeśītyapīritāḥ
tatra pūrvaprasiddhānāmuddeśaḥ kriyate'dhunā 49
śaṃkarābharaṇo ghaṇṭārava āhaṃsadīpakau
rītiḥ pūrṇāṭikā lāṭī pallavīti babhāṣire 50
rāgāṅgānyaṣṭa gāmbhīrī vauhāṭī khaśikotpalī
gollī nādāntarī nīlotpalī chāyā taraṅgiṇī 51
gāndhāragatikārañjāvityekādaśa menire
bhāṣāṅgānyatha bhāvakrīsvabhāvakrīśivakriyaḥ 52
marukakrītrinetrakrīkumudakrīdanukriyaḥ
ojakrīndrakriyo nāgakṛtirdhanyakṛtistathā 53
vipāyakrīḥ kriyāṅgāṇi dvādaśeti jagurbudhāḥ
trīṇyupāṅgāni pūrṇāṭo devālaśca guruñjikā 54
catustriṃśadime rāgāḥ prākprasiddhāḥ prakīrtitāḥ
athādhunā prasiddhānāmuddeśaḥ pratipādyate 55
madhyamādirmālavaśrīstroḍī baṅgālabhairavau
varāṭī gurjarī gauḍakolāhalavasantakāḥ
dhānyāsīdeśideśākhyā rāgāṅgāṇi trayodaśa 56
ḍaumbavanyāsāvarī velāvalī prathamamañjarī 57
āḍikā modikā nāgadhvaniḥ śuddhavarāṭikā
naṭṭā karṇāṭabaṅgālau bhāṣāṅgāṇi navābruvan 58
kriyāṅgatritayaṃ rāyakṛtirgauḍakṛtistathā
devakrīrityathopāṅgasaptaviṃśatirucyate 59
kauntalī drāviḍī saindhavyatha sthānavarāṭikā
hatasvaravarāṭī ca syātpratāpavarāṭikā 60
varāṭyaḥ ṣaḍiti cchāyā turuṣkādye tu toḍike
mahārāṣṭrī ca saurāṣṭrī dakṣiṇā drāviḍītyamūḥ 61
uktāścatasro gurjaryo bhuñchikā stambhatīrthikā
chāyāpratāpopapade belābalyau ca bhairavī 62
